/**
 * WTC stock report - server code (Google Apps Script).  VERSION 17 (DAILY, login tracking + sign out, PDF test + fallback, landscape stock-list PDF, office-only cancel, history filters, stock-list PDF, transaction log, past-day view, transfers, shortages, received / used / damaged, All Sites Stock + Changes formulas, self-check)
 *
 * - Storekeepers update stock on any day: they change only the items that moved.
 * - Once a week (from Monday) each site confirms a full weekly check.
 * - Transfers between sites: send, receive, cancel. Quantities update by themselves.
 * - Every change is one row on the "Stock" tab (the permanent record).
 * - "All Sites Stock" tab = current stock of every site, built from formulas (updates by itself).
 * - "Checks" tab = weekly checks. "Transfers" tab = every transfer.
 * - The first time it runs, it copies your existing weekly data (Data + Saves tabs) automatically.
 *
 * Updating from an older version: paste this file, then Deploy > Manage deployments > Edit > New version.
 * New sheet only: run setup() once, then Deploy > New deployment > Web app (Execute as: Me, Access: Anyone).
 */

var TZ_ = 'Asia/Dubai';
var ALL_ = 'ALL SITES';
var ST_HEAD_ = ['Time', 'Date', 'Site', 'Item ID', 'Item', 'Unit', 'Qty', 'Old', 'By', 'Reason'];
var CK_HEAD_ = ['Time', 'Week of (Monday)', 'Site', 'By', 'Items changed'];
var TR_HEAD_ = ['ID', 'Sent', 'From', 'To', 'Item ID', 'Item', 'Unit', 'Qty', 'Sent by', 'Note',
                'Status', 'Received', 'Received by', 'Received qty', 'Shortage', 'Shortage by', 'Shortage time'];

var SS_OVR_ = null;   // set while a dashboard button runs (simple trigger)
function ss_() {
  if (SS_OVR_) return SS_OVR_;
  var id = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('SSID');
  return id ? SpreadsheetApp.openById(id) : SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
}

function doGet() {
  return HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile('Index')
    .setTitle('WTC Inventory Report')
    .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1')
    .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
}

/** Adds a small menu in the Google Sheet. */
/* ================= Google Sheet: menu, live refresh, tidy-up ================= */
var VERSION_ = '25.1';
var OLD_TABS_ = ['Report', 'Totals', 'Submissions', 'Saves', 'Data'];
var TAB_ORDER_ = [['Dashboard', '#C42D4E'], ['All Sites Stock', '#1b7a3a'], ['Idle Items', '#8F5400'], ['Machinery Repairs', '#B8274A'], ['Rentals', '#0E7C86'], ['Site Services', '#4E7D2B'], ['Excess Items', '#5A3E8F'], ['Excess Moves', '#5A3E8F'], ['Excess Photos', '#5A3E8F'], ['Excess Stores', '#5A3E8F'], ['Notices', '#E07A00'], ['Site Items', '#2E7D32'], ['Notice Replies', '#E07A00'], ['Assistant Log', '#00897B'], ['Site Item Moves', '#2E7D32'], ['Changes', '#4a4a55'], ['Stock', '#4a4a55'], ['Received', '#1b7a3a'], ['Used', '#b26a00'],
                  ['Damaged', '#C42D4E'], ['Transfers', '#1f5fa8'], ['Checks', '#4a4a55'], ['Idle Log', '#8F5400'], ['Repair Log', '#B8274A'], ['Logins', '#6b4fa0'], ['Login Summary', '#6b4fa0'], ['Changes - weekly history', null], ['Sites', null], ['Items', null], ['Images', null], ['Rental Types', null], ['Rental Companies', null]];

/** Runs by itself when the sheet is opened: adds the menu and checks the All Sites Stock tab. */
function onOpen() {
  try {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().createMenu('WTC Stock')
      .addItem('🏠 Open dashboard', 'openDashboard')
      .addItem('Build / refresh dashboard', 'buildDashboard')
      .addItem('🔄 Refresh dashboard numbers', 'refreshDashboard')
      .addItem('🙈 Hide helper tabs', 'hideHelperTabs')
      .addItem('👁 Show all tabs', 'showAllTabs')
      .addSeparator()
      .addItem('⏰ Turn on daily reminders & assistant (08:00)', 'installDaily')
      .addItem('🤖 Run the assistant now', 'runAssistantMenu')
      .addItem('📋 Send this week’s weekly-check task now', 'weeklyNowMenu')
      .addSeparator()
      .addItem('Rebuild All Sites Stock tab', 'rebuildLiveNow')
      .addItem('Check system', 'checkSystem')
      .addItem('Connect the page to this sheet', 'connectHere')
      .addItem('Cancel a transaction (office only)…', 'cancelByOffice')
      .addItem('Make a test PDF (allows PDFs)', 'testPdf')
      .addItem('Link excess store photos (after uploading them to Drive)', 'linkExcessPhotos')
      .addItem('🦺 Update the PPE Excel file in Drive', 'ppeRebuildFile')
      .addItem('🦺 PPE view-only login (Camp Boss) – set PIN', 'ppeViewersMenu')
      .addItem('🤖 AI voice assistant – set Gemini key', 'aiSetupMenu')
      .addItem('Bring back old weekly Changes from backup', 'restoreOldChanges')
      .addSeparator()
      .addItem('Tidy up tabs + turn on auto-refresh (one time)', 'tidyUp')
      .addToUi();
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    if (ss.getSheetByName('Stock')) rebuildLive_(ss, ledger_(ss));
  } catch (e) {}
  try { var ssp = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); if (ssp.getSheetByName(PI_TAB_)) pvSheet_(ssp); } catch (e) {}
  try { var d = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName('Dashboard'); if (d) d.getRange('A1').activate(); } catch (e) {}
}

/** Runs by itself when you edit the sheet by hand: Stock, Items or Sites changes re-check the All Sites Stock tab. */
function onEdit(e) {
  try {
    var n = e && e.range ? e.range.getSheet().getName() : '';
    if (n === 'Stock' || n === 'Items' || n === 'Sites') {
      var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
      rebuildLive_(ss, ledger_(ss));
    }
  } catch (err) {}
}

function rebuildLiveNow() { var ss = ss_(); ensure_(ss); rebuildLive_(ss, [], true); }

/** Every 5 minutes (after Tidy up): re-checks the All Sites Stock tab layout. */
function refreshAll() {
  try { var ss = ss_(); ensure_(ss); MEMO_.fullCheck = true; rebuildLive_(ss, ledger_(ss)); } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
  try { idleSafe_(ss_(), null); } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
  try { machSafe_(ss_(), null); } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
  try { sweepSessions_(ss_()); } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
}

/** An All Sites Stock problem must never stop a storekeeper's save. Problems are remembered for Check system. */
function liveSafe_(ss, rows) {
  var p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  try { rebuildLive_(ss, rows); p.setProperty('LAST_LIVE_ERROR', ''); }
  catch (e) { console.error('All Sites Stock rebuild failed: ' + e); try { p.setProperty('LAST_LIVE_ERROR', new Date().toISOString() + ' ' + e); } catch (x) {} }
}

/** Google tabs start with 1,000 rows / 26 columns. Adds more before writing past the end. */
function room_(sh, lastRow, lastCol) {
  var mr = sh.getMaxRows(), mc = sh.getMaxColumns();
  if (lastRow > mr) sh.insertRowsAfter(mr, lastRow - mr + 500);
  if (lastCol && lastCol > mc) sh.insertColumnsAfter(mc, lastCol - mc + 5);
}

/** Remembers the last save that came from the page (shown by Check system). */
function mark_(ss, what, site, name) {
  try {
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('LAST_PAGE_SAVE', JSON.stringify(
      { t: new Date().toISOString(), what: what, site: site, by: name, ss: ss.getId(), v: VERSION_ }));
  } catch (e) {}
}

/** Makes the page save into THIS spreadsheet (use if Check system says it saves somewhere else). */
function connectHere() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('SSID', ss.getId());
  ensure_(ss);
  rebuildLive_(ss, ledger_(ss));
  var msg = 'Done. The page now saves into this spreadsheet: ' + ss.getName() + '.';
  try { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(msg); } catch (e) {}
  return msg;
}

/**
 * ONE TIME: backs up the old weekly tabs to a new file in your Google Drive, deletes them here,
 * puts the tabs in order with colours, and turns on the 10-minute auto-refresh.
 */
function tidyUp() {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  ensureChanges_(ss);
  Object.keys(MV_).forEach(function (t) { mvSheet_(ss, t); });
  trSheet_(ss);
  idleSheet_(ss);
  machSheet_(ss);
  try { rnSheet_(ss); rtSheet_(ss); rcSheet_(ss); svSheet_(ss); } catch (e) {}
  try { ensureItemCols_(ss); } catch (e) {}
  try { oneSitesTab_(ss); } catch (e) {}
  idleSafe_(ss, null);
  machSafe_(ss, null);
  var old = OLD_TABS_.filter(function (n) { return ss.getSheetByName(n); }), note = '';
  if (old.length) {
    var bk = SpreadsheetApp.create('WTC Stock - old weekly tabs backup ' + today_());
    ['Data', 'Saves', 'Report', 'Totals', 'Submissions'].forEach(function (n) {
      var sh = ss.getSheetByName(n);
      if (sh) sh.copyTo(bk).setName(n);
    });
    var first = bk.getSheets()[0];
    if (OLD_TABS_.indexOf(first.getName()) < 0) bk.deleteSheet(first);
    old.forEach(function (n) { ss.deleteSheet(ss.getSheetByName(n)); });
    note = 'Removed old tabs: ' + old.join(', ') + '. A backup copy is in your Google Drive: "' + bk.getName() + '". ';
  } else {
    note = 'No old tabs to remove. ';
  }
  var pos = 1;
  TAB_ORDER_.forEach(function (t) {
    var sh = ss.getSheetByName(t[0]);
    if (!sh) return;
    ss.setActiveSheet(sh);
    ss.moveActiveSheet(pos++);
    if (t[1]) sh.setTabColor(t[1]);
  });
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (tr) {
    if (tr.getHandlerFunction() === 'refreshAll') ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(tr);
  });
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('refreshAll').timeBased().everyMinutes(5).create();
  rebuildLive_(ss, ledger_(ss));
  ss.setActiveSheet(ss.getSheetByName(LIVE_));
  var msg = note + 'Tabs are in order. The All Sites Stock tab updates by itself from the Stock tab.';
  try { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(msg); } catch (e) {}
  return msg;
}

/** Shows in one message whether everything is working, and what to do if not. */
function checkSystem() {
  var ss = ss_(), here = null, lines = [], fix = [];
  try { here = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); } catch (e) {}
  ensure_(ss);
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  lines.push('Code version here: ' + VERSION_);
  if (here && here.getId() !== ss.getId()) {
    lines.push('!! The page saves into ANOTHER spreadsheet: "' + ss.getName() + '"');
    fix.push('Click WTC Stock > Connect the page to this sheet.');
  }
  var mk = null;
  try { mk = JSON.parse(props.getProperty('LAST_PAGE_SAVE') || 'null'); } catch (e) {}
  if (mk) {
    lines.push('Last save from the page: ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(mk.t), TZ_, 'dd MMM HH:mm') + ' · ' + mk.site + ' · ' + mk.by + ' · ' + mk.what + ' (page version ' + mk.v + ')');
    if (mk.v !== VERSION_) fix.push('Publish the new version: Deploy > Manage deployments > pencil > Version: New version > Deploy.');
  } else {
    lines.push('Last save from the page: none received by this version yet');
    fix.push('If storekeepers have saved since you updated the code, the link is still on an old version: Deploy > Manage deployments > pencil > Version: New version > Deploy.');
  }
  var rows = ledger_(ss), last = rows[rows.length - 1];
  lines.push('Stock tab: ' + rows.length + ' rows. Last: ' + (last ? Utilities.formatDate(last[0] instanceof Date ? last[0] : new Date(last[0]), TZ_, 'dd MMM HH:mm') + ' · ' + last[2] + ' · ' + last[9] : 'none'));
  try {
    var sm = JSON.parse(props.getProperty('SAVE_MS') || '[]');
    if (sm.length) {
      var avg = sm.reduce(function (a, x) { return a + x[1]; }, 0) / sm.length;
      lines.push('Save speed (inside Google, last ' + sm.length + ' saves): average ' + (avg / 1000).toFixed(1) + ' s, slowest ' + (Math.max.apply(null, sm.map(function (x) { return x[1]; })) / 1000).toFixed(1) + ' s');
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  var err = props.getProperty('LAST_LIVE_ERROR') || '';
  try { rebuildLive_(ss, rows, true); lines.push('All Sites Stock tab: rebuilt just now ✓ (updates by itself from the Stock tab)'); props.setProperty('LAST_LIVE_ERROR', ''); }
  catch (e) { lines.push('!! All Sites Stock tab could not be built: ' + e); fix.push('Send this message to Claude.'); }
  if (err) lines.push('Earlier problem: ' + err);
  var auto = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().some(function (tr) { return tr.getHandlerFunction() === 'refreshAll'; });
  lines.push('Auto-refresh every 5 min: ' + (auto ? 'ON' : 'OFF'));
  if (!auto) fix.push('Click WTC Stock > Tidy up tabs + turn on auto-refresh.');
  var old = OLD_TABS_.filter(function (n) { return ss.getSheetByName(n); });
  if (old.length) lines.push('Old tabs still here: ' + old.join(', '));
  var msg = lines.join('\n') + '\n\n' + (fix.length ? 'TO FIX:\n- ' + fix.join('\n- ') : 'Everything looks right ✓');
  try { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(msg); } catch (e) {}
  return msg;
}

/** Only needed for a brand-new sheet. */
function setup() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('SSID', ss.getId());
  ss.setSpreadsheetTimeZone(TZ_);
  if (!ss.getSheetByName('Items') || !ss.getSheetByName('Images')) {
    throw new Error('The Items and Images tabs are missing. Import WTC_Weekly_Stock_Setup.xlsx first.');
  }
  tab_(ss, 'Sites', SITES_HEAD_, ['B']);
  ensure_(ss);
  try { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Setup finished. On the Sites tab add a row ALL SITES with your office PIN, then one row per storekeeper: Site, their own PIN, their name. Then deploy the web app.'); } catch (e) {}
}

/* ---------------- dates (always Dubai time) ---------------- */
function today_() { return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); }
function fmtUTC_(d) {
  return d.getUTCFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getUTCMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + d.getUTCDate()).slice(-2);
}
function mondayOf_(ds) {
  var p = String(ds).split('-'), d = new Date(Date.UTC(+p[0], +p[1] - 1, +p[2]));
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() - ((d.getUTCDay() + 6) % 7));
  return fmtUTC_(d);
}
/** Monday number k (1-5) of a 'yyyy-MM' month, as 'yyyy-MM-dd'. Used to import the old weekly data. */
function weekDate_(month, k) {
  var p = String(month).split('-'), y = +p[0], m = +p[1] - 1;
  var dow = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 1)).getUTCDay();
  return fmtUTC_(new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 1 + ((8 - dow) % 7) + 7 * (k - 1))));
}
function dstr_(x) { return x instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(x, TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd') : String(x == null ? '' : x).trim(); }
function mstr_(x) { return x instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(x, TZ_, 'yyyy-MM') : String(x == null ? '' : x).trim(); }
function iso_(x) { return x instanceof Date ? x.toISOString() : String(x || ''); }
function r3_(n) { return Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000; }

/* ---------------- tabs ---------------- */
function tab_(ss, name, head, textCols) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ss.insertSheet(name);
    (textCols || []).forEach(function (c) { sh.getRange(c + ':' + c).setNumberFormat('@'); });
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setValues([head])
      .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#C42D4E').setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
    sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sh;
}
function trSheet_(ss) {
  var sh = tab_(ss, 'Transfers', TR_HEAD_, []);
  if (sh.getRange(1, 15).getValue() !== 'Shortage') {
    room_(sh, 1, 17);
    sh.getRange(1, 15, 1, 3).setValues([TR_HEAD_.slice(14)])
      .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#C42D4E').setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
  }
  return sh;
}

/** Creates the daily tabs the first time and imports the old weekly data. */
function ensure_(ss) {
  if (ss.getSheetByName('Stock')) return;
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    if (ss.getSheetByName('Stock')) return;
    var ck = tab_(ss, 'Checks', CK_HEAD_, ['B']);
    trSheet_(ss);
    var rows = importWeekly_(ss, ck);
    var st = tab_(ss, 'Stock', ST_HEAD_, ['B']);
    if (rows.length) { room_(st, rows.length + 1); st.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, 10).setValues(rows); }
    liveSafe_(ss, rows);
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/** Old weekly Data -> one Stock row per change (dated the Monday of that week). Old Saves -> Checks. */
function importWeekly_(ss, ck) {
  var out = [], data = ss.getSheetByName('Data');
  if (data && data.getLastRow() > 1) {
    var ent = [];
    data.getRange(2, 1, data.getLastRow() - 1, 11).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
      var site = String(r[0]).trim(), m = mstr_(r[1]);
      if (!site || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(m)) return;
      for (var k = 1; k <= 5; k++) {
        var x = r[5 + k];
        if (x === '' || x == null) continue;
        ent.push([weekDate_(m, k), site, r[2], String(r[3]), String(r[5]), Number(x)]);
      }
    });
    ent.sort(function (a, b) { return a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : a[0] > b[0] ? 1 : 0; });
    var last = {};
    ent.forEach(function (e) {
      var key = e[1] + '\u0001' + e[2], had = last.hasOwnProperty(key);
      if (had && last[key] === e[5]) return;
      out.push([new Date(e[0] + 'T09:00:00+04:00'), e[0], e[1], e[2], e[3], e[4], e[5], had ? last[key] : '',
                'Imported', 'From weekly sheet']);
      last[key] = e[5];
    });
  }
  var sv = ss.getSheetByName('Saves');
  if (sv && sv.getLastRow() > 1) {
    var byKey = {};
    sv.getRange(2, 1, sv.getLastRow() - 1, 6).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
      var m = mstr_(r[2]), w = Number(r[3]), site = String(r[1]).trim();
      if (!site || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(m) || !(w >= 1 && w <= 5)) return;
      var wk = weekDate_(m, w);
      byKey[site + '|' + wk] = [r[0], wk, site, String(r[4]), r[5]];
    });
    var cks = Object.keys(byKey).map(function (k) { return byKey[k]; });
    if (cks.length) { room_(ck, cks.length + 1); ck.getRange(2, 1, cks.length, 5).setValues(cks); }
  }
  return out;
}

/* ---------------- sites, items, PINs ---------------- */
var MEMO_ = {};   // filled once per request (each page call is a fresh run), so Sites/Items are read only once
function siteRows_(ss) { return MEMO_.sites || (MEMO_.sites = ss.getSheetByName('Sites').getDataRange().getValues().slice(1)); }
function isAll_(site) { return String(site).trim().toUpperCase() === ALL_; }

/* ---- Sites tab = Site | PIN | Storekeeper — one row per storekeeper, each with their OWN PIN ----
 * A site with two storekeepers has two rows (same site, different PINs). The ALL SITES row holds the office PIN.
 * Sign-in: the PIN must belong to the chosen name on that site.
 */
var SITES_HEAD_ = ['Site', 'PIN', 'Storekeeper'];
var OLD_KEEPERS_ = 'Storekeepers';
/** Converts older layouts (comma lists of names, Site PIN + Personal PIN columns, or a separate Storekeepers tab) into one row per person. */
function oneSitesTab_(ss) {
  var st = ss.getSheetByName('Sites'); if (!st) return;
  var old = ss.getSheetByName(OLD_KEEPERS_);
  var lc = Math.max(3, st.getLastColumn());
  var head = st.getRange(1, 1, 1, lc).getValues()[0].map(function (h) { return String(h).trim(); });
  var rows = siteRows_(ss);
  var multi = rows.some(function (r) { return /[,;\n]/.test(String(r[2] == null ? '' : r[2])); });
  var fourCol = head[3] === 'Personal PIN', oldHead = head[1] !== 'PIN' || head[2] !== 'Storekeeper';
  if (!old && !fourCol && !oldHead && !multi) return;
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(10000)) return;
  try {
    var n = st.getLastRow(), v = n > 1 ? st.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, Math.max(4, lc)).getValues() : [];
    var out = [], seen = {}, sitePin = {}, order = [];
    var add = function (site, pin, name) {
      var k = site + '|' + name;
      if (name && seen[k] !== undefined) { if (pin && !out[seen[k]][1]) out[seen[k]][1] = pin; return; }
      if (name) seen[k] = out.length;
      out.push([site, pin, name]);
    };
    v.forEach(function (r) {
      var site = String(r[0]).trim(); if (!site) return;
      var sp = String(r[1] == null ? '' : r[1]).trim();
      if (sp && !sitePin[site]) sitePin[site] = sp;
      if (order.indexOf(site) < 0) order.push(site);
      var names = String(r[2] == null ? '' : r[2]).split(/[,;\n]/).map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(String);
      if (/^Storekeepers/.test(head[2]) && !fourCol) names = [];   // 18.7 layout: names were on the Storekeepers tab
      var own = fourCol && names.length === 1 ? String(r[3] == null ? '' : r[3]).trim() : '';
      if (!names.length) { if (isAll_(site) || !old) add(site, sp, ''); return; }
      names.forEach(function (nm) { add(site, own || sp, nm); });
    });
    if (old && old.getLastRow() > 1) old.getRange(2, 1, old.getLastRow() - 1, 3).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
      var site = String(r[0]).trim(), nm = String(r[1]).trim();
      if (!site || !nm) return;
      add(site, String(r[2] == null ? '' : r[2]).trim() || sitePin[site] || '', nm);
    });
    // a site row without a name is only kept when that site has no storekeeper rows (and for ALL SITES)
    var named = {};
    out.forEach(function (r) { if (r[2]) named[r[0]] = 1; });
    out = out.filter(function (r) { return r[2] || isAll_(r[0]) || !named[r[0]]; });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return order.indexOf(a[0]) - order.indexOf(b[0]); });
    st.clearContents();
    room_(st, out.length + 1, 3);
    st.getRange('B:B').setNumberFormat('@');
    st.getRange(1, 1, 1, 3).setValues([SITES_HEAD_]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#C42D4E').setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
    if (out.length) st.getRange(2, 1, out.length, 3).setValues(out);
    try { st.getRange(1, 2).setNote('Each storekeeper\'s own PIN. A site with two storekeepers has two rows, each with its own PIN. ALL SITES row = office PIN.'); } catch (e) {}
    st.setFrozenRows(1);
    if (old) ss.deleteSheet(old);
    MEMO_.sites = null;
  } finally { lock.releaseLock(); }
}
/** PIN check. With a name: the PIN on that person's row. Without a name: any PIN of that site. */
function checkPin_(ss, site, pin, name) {
  var p = String(pin || '').trim();
  if (p === '') return false;
  var nm = name != null ? String(name).trim() : '';
  return siteRows_(ss).some(function (r) {
    if (String(r[0]).trim() !== site || String(r[1] == null ? '' : r[1]).trim() !== p) return false;
    if (!nm) return true;
    return String(r[2] == null ? '' : r[2]).split(/[,;\n]/).some(function (x) { return x.trim() === nm; });
  });
}
/** Storekeeper names per site (one per row; older comma lists still work). */
function siteNames_(ss) {
  var names = {};
  siteRows_(ss).forEach(function (r) {
    var s = String(r[0]).trim();
    if (!s) return;
    if (!names[s]) names[s] = [];
    String(r[2] == null ? '' : r[2]).split(/[,;\n]/).forEach(function (n) {
      n = n.trim();
      if (n && names[s].indexOf(n) < 0) names[s].push(n);
    });
  });
  return names;
}
function sitesList_(ss) { return Object.keys(siteNames_(ss)).filter(function (s) { return !isAll_(s); }); }

function itemRows_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.items) return MEMO_.items;
  return (MEMO_.items = itemsTab_(ss).slice(1).filter(function (r) { return r[0] !== ''; }));
}
function itemsTab_(ss) { return MEMO_.itemsTab || (MEMO_.itemsTab = ss.getSheetByName('Items').getDataRange().getValues()); }
/* ---- Items the storekeepers may NOT pick in Used or Damaged ----
 * Two columns on the Items tab: "Used allowed" and "Damaged allowed". Blank or Yes = allowed, No = hidden + refused.
 */
var ITEM_FLAGS_ = ['Used allowed', 'Damaged allowed', 'Rent allowed'];
function ensureItemCols_(ss) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Items'); if (!sh) return;
  var lc = Math.max(1, sh.getLastColumn()), head = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lc).getValues()[0].map(function (h) { return String(h).trim().toLowerCase(); });
  ITEM_FLAGS_.forEach(function (name) {
    if (head.indexOf(name.toLowerCase()) >= 0) return;
    var c = head.length + 1;
    room_(sh, 1, c);
    sh.getRange(1, c).setValue(name).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#C42D4E').setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
    try {
      var rule = SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().requireValueInList(['Yes', 'No'], true).setAllowInvalid(false).build();
      sh.getRange(2, c, Math.max(1, sh.getMaxRows() - 1), 1).setDataValidation(rule);
      sh.getRange(1, c).setNote(name === 'Rent allowed' ? 'Blank or Yes = storekeepers can choose this item under Rented machines (From our item list). No = hidden there.' : 'Blank or Yes = storekeepers can choose this item in ' + name.split(' ')[0] + '. No = the item is hidden there.');
    } catch (e) {}
    head.push(name.toLowerCase());
    MEMO_.items = MEMO_.itemsTab = null;
  });
}
/** { itemId: [usedOK, damagedOK] } from the Items tab. */
function itemFlags_(ss) {
  var v = itemsTab_(ss), out = {};
  var head = (v[0] || []).map(function (h) { return String(h).trim().toLowerCase(); });
  var cu = head.indexOf('used allowed'), cd = head.indexOf('damaged allowed'), cr = head.indexOf('rent allowed');
  var no = function (x) { return /^(no|n|false|0)$/i.test(String(x == null ? '' : x).trim()); };
  v.slice(1).forEach(function (r) { if (r[0] !== '') out[String(r[0])] = [cu < 0 || !no(r[cu]), cd < 0 || !no(r[cd]), cr < 0 || !no(r[cr])]; });
  return out;
}
function itemsById_(ss) {
  var items = {};
  itemRows_(ss).forEach(function (r) { items[String(r[0])] = r; });
  return items;
}

function trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name) {
  if (!checkPin_(ss, site, pin)) return 'Wrong site or PIN';
  if (!isAll_(site) && name && !checkPin_(ss, site, pin, name)) return 'This PIN is not for ' + String(name).trim() + '. Type your own PIN.';
  if (isAll_(site)) return 'ALL SITES is view only';
  var allowed = siteNames_(ss)[site] || [];
  if (!allowed.length) return 'No storekeeper names are set for this site yet. Please ask the office.';
  if (allowed.indexOf(String(name || '').trim()) < 0) return 'Choose your name from the list first';
  return '';
}

function withLock_(fn) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try { lock.waitLock(30000); } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: 'The sheet is busy. Please try again.' }; }
  try { return fn(); } finally { lock.releaseLock(); }
}

/* ---------------- the Stock record ---------------- */
function ledger_(ss) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Stock'), n = sh.getLastRow();
  return n > 1 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, 10).getValues() : [];
}
function appendLedger_(ss, rows, add) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Stock');
  room_(sh, rows.length + 1 + add.length);
  sh.getRange(rows.length + 2, 1, add.length, 10).setValues(add);
  add.forEach(function (r) { rows.push(r); });
}

/** Stock at the end of a date ('' = now): { itemId: { site: [qty, date, timeIso, by] } }. */
function stockAsOf_(rows, date, only) {
  var q = {};
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var d = dstr_(r[1]);
    if (date && d > date) return;
    var s = String(r[2]).trim();
    if (only && s !== only) return;
    var id = String(r[3]);
    if (!q[id]) q[id] = {};
    var p = q[id][s];
    if (!p || d >= p[1]) q[id][s] = [r[6] === '' || r[6] == null ? '' : Number(r[6]), d, iso_(r[0]), String(r[8])];
  });
  return q;
}
function haveOf_(rows, site, id) {
  // latest quantity of one item at one site: scan from the newest row back (rows are in date order)
  var sid = String(id), best = null, bd = '';
  for (var i = rows.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    var r = rows[i];
    if (String(r[3]) !== sid || String(r[2]).trim() !== site) continue;
    var d = dstr_(r[1]);
    if (best === null || d > bd) { best = r; bd = d; }
  }
  return best && best[6] !== '' && best[6] != null ? Number(best[6]) : null;
}

/* ---------------- Idle (unused) quantities ----------------
 * The storekeeper marks how many of an item's total are NOT being used at the site.
 * Every change is one row on the "Idle Log" tab. The "Idle Items" tab lists what is idle now, at every site.
 * Idle can never be more than the stock: if stock goes down (used, damaged, sent), idle shown = the smaller of the two.
 */
var IDLE_LOG_ = 'Idle Log', IDLE_TAB_ = 'Idle Items';
var IL_HEAD_ = ['Time', 'Date', 'Site', 'Item ID', 'Item', 'Unit', 'Idle qty', 'Old idle', 'By', 'Note'];
function idleSheet_(ss) { return tab_(ss, IDLE_LOG_, IL_HEAD_, ['B']); }
function idleRows_(ss) {
  var sh = idleSheet_(ss), n = sh.getLastRow();
  return n > 1 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, 10).getValues() : [];
}
/** Idle at the end of a date ('' = now): { itemId: { site: [idle, date, timeIso, by, idleSince] } }. */
function idleAsOf_(rows, date, only) {
  var q = {};
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var d = dstr_(r[1]);
    if (date && d > date) return;
    var s = String(r[2]).trim();
    if (only && s !== only) return;
    var id = String(r[3]), v = Number(r[6]) || 0;
    if (!q[id]) q[id] = {};
    var p = q[id][s], since = v > 0 ? (p && p[0] > 0 ? p[4] : d) : '';
    q[id][s] = [v, d, iso_(r[0]), String(r[8]), since];
  });
  return q;
}
/** Idle shown = never more than the stock. */
function effIdle_(idle, qty) {
  if (!idle || !(idle > 0)) return 0;
  var t = qty === '' || qty == null ? 0 : Number(qty);
  return r3_(Math.max(0, Math.min(idle, t)));
}
/** After stock goes down: refresh the Idle Items tab only if one of these items is now below its idle number. */
function idleIfNeeded_(ss, rows, site, ids) {
  try {
    var iq = idleAsOf_(idleRows_(ss), '', site);
    var hit = (ids || []).some(function (id) {
      var v = iq[String(id)] && iq[String(id)][site];
      if (!v || !(v[0] > 0)) return false;
      var h = haveOf_(rows, site, id);
      return (h === null ? 0 : h) < v[0];
    });
    if (hit) idleSafe_(ss, rows);
  } catch (e) { console.error('idle check: ' + e); }
  try {
    var mq = machAsOf_(machRows_(ss), '', site);
    var mh = (ids || []).some(function (id) {
      var v = mq[String(id)] && mq[String(id)][site];
      if (!v || !(v[0] + v[1] > 0)) return false;
      var h = haveOf_(rows, site, id);
      return (h === null ? 0 : h) < v[0] + v[1];
    });
    if (mh) machSafe_(ss, rows);
  } catch (e) { console.error('machine check: ' + e); }
}
function idleSafe_(ss, rows) {
  try { rebuildIdle_(ss, rows); } catch (e) { console.error('Idle Items rebuild failed: ' + e); }
}
/** Rebuilds the "Idle Items" tab: every item that is idle now, at every site, longest idle first. */
function rebuildIdle_(ss, rows) {
  rows = rows && rows.length ? rows : ledger_(ss);
  var q = stockAsOf_(rows, '', null), iq = idleAsOf_(idleRows_(ss), '', null), items = itemsById_(ss), t = today_();
  var sites = sitesList_(ss), out = [], byItem = {};
  Object.keys(iq).forEach(function (id) {
    Object.keys(iq[id]).forEach(function (s) {
      if (sites.indexOf(s) < 0) return;
      var v = iq[id][s], tot = q[id] && q[id][s] ? q[id][s][0] : '', idle = effIdle_(v[0], tot);
      if (!(idle > 0)) return;
      var it = items[id] || [id, id, '', '', ''], since = v[4] || v[1];
      var dd = Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.parse(t) - Date.parse(since)) / 864e5));
      out.push([s, it[0], it[1], it[2], it[4], Number(tot) || 0, r3_((Number(tot) || 0) - idle), idle, since, dd, v[3], v[1]]);
      var k = String(it[0]);
      if (!byItem[k]) byItem[k] = [it[1], it[4], 0, []];
      byItem[k][2] = r3_(byItem[k][2] + idle);
      byItem[k][3].push(s + ': ' + idle);
    });
  });
  out.sort(function (a, b) { return b[9] - a[9] || (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : a[0] > b[0] ? 1 : 0); });
  var sum = Object.keys(byItem).map(function (k) { var b = byItem[k]; return [b[0], b[1], b[2], b[3].join(' · ')]; })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return b[2] - a[2]; });
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(IDLE_TAB_) || ss.insertSheet(IDLE_TAB_);
  sh.clear();
  var head = ['Site', 'Item ID', 'Item', 'Category', 'Unit', 'Total qty', 'In use', 'Idle qty', 'Idle since', 'Days idle', 'Marked by', 'Last updated'];
  var sHead = ['Item (all sites)', 'Unit', 'Total idle', 'Where'];
  room_(sh, Math.max(out.length, sum.length) + 2, 17);
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setValues([head]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#8F5400').setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
  sh.getRange(1, 14, 1, sHead.length).setValues([sHead]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#1f5fa8').setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
  if (out.length) {
    sh.getRange(2, 1, out.length, head.length).setValues(out);
    sh.getRange(2, 9, out.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@');
    sh.getRange(2, 12, out.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@');
    sh.getRange(2, 1, out.length, head.length).setBackgrounds(out.map(function (r) {
      var c = r[9] >= 30 ? '#F9D6DE' : r[9] >= 14 ? '#FCEBD3' : '#FFFFFF';
      return head.map(function () { return c; });
    }));
  } else {
    sh.getRange(2, 1).setValue('No idle items at any site right now.');
  }
  if (sum.length) sh.getRange(2, 14, sum.length, sHead.length).setValues(sum);
  sh.getRange(Math.max(out.length, 1) + 3, 1).setValue('Updates by itself after every save. Red = idle 30+ days, orange = 14+ days. Idle items can be transferred to sites that need them.')
    .setFontStyle('italic').setFontColor('#6A7079');
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
}

/* ---------------- Machinery: needs maintenance / sent for repair ----------------
 * Only items whose Category or Sub category on the Items tab says "Machinery".
 * The storekeeper sets how many of the machine (at their site) need maintenance and how many are at a workshop,
 * with the problem and the workshop name. Every change is one row on the "Repair Log" tab.
 * The "Machinery Repairs" tab lists every machine that is not working right now, at every site.
 * The machines still count in the site's stock (they belong to the site).
 */
var MACH_LOG_ = 'Repair Log', MACH_TAB_ = 'Machinery Repairs';
var ML_HEAD_ = ['Time', 'Date', 'Site', 'Item ID', 'Item', 'Unit', 'Needs maintenance', 'At workshop', 'Problem', 'Workshop', 'By', 'Note'];
function isMach_(r) { return /machin/i.test(String(r[2] || '')) || /machin/i.test(String(r[3] || '')); }
function machSheet_(ss) { return tab_(ss, MACH_LOG_, ML_HEAD_, ['B']); }
function machRows_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.machRows) return MEMO_.machRows;
  var sh = machSheet_(ss), n = sh.getLastRow();
  return (MEMO_.machRows = n > 1 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, 12).getValues() : []);
}
/** { itemId: { site: [maint, repair, problem, workshop, maintSince, repairSince, by, date] } } at the end of a date ('' = now). */
function machAsOf_(rows, date, only) {
  var q = {};
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var d = dstr_(r[1]);
    if (date && d > date) return;
    var s = String(r[2]).trim();
    if (only && s !== only) return;
    var id = String(r[3]), m = Number(r[6]) || 0, x = Number(r[7]) || 0;
    if (!q[id]) q[id] = {};
    var p = q[id][s];
    q[id][s] = [m, x, String(r[8] || ''), String(r[9] || ''),
                m > 0 ? (p && p[0] > 0 ? p[4] : d) : '', x > 0 ? (p && p[1] > 0 ? p[5] : d) : '', String(r[10] || ''), d];
  });
  return q;
}
/** What is shown: never more machines than the site has. Workshop first, then maintenance. */
function machEff_(v, qty) {
  var t = qty === '' || qty == null ? 0 : Math.max(0, Number(qty) || 0);
  var x = r3_(Math.min(v[1] || 0, t)), m = r3_(Math.min(v[0] || 0, t - x));
  return [Math.max(0, m), Math.max(0, x)];
}
function daysSince_(d, t) { return d ? Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.parse(t) - Date.parse(d)) / 864e5)) : 0; }
/** { itemId: [maint, repair, problem, workshop, maintSince, repairSince, by] } for one site, only machines not fully working. */
function machMap_(ss, date, site, q) {
  var mq = machAsOf_(machRows_(ss), date, site), out = {};
  Object.keys(mq).forEach(function (id) {
    var v = mq[id][site], tot = q[id] && q[id][site] ? q[id][site][0] : '', e = machEff_(v, tot);
    if (e[0] + e[1] > 0) out[id] = [e[0], e[1], v[2], v[3], e[0] > 0 ? v[4] : '', e[1] > 0 ? v[5] : '', v[6]];
  });
  return out;
}
function machSafe_(ss, rows) {
  try { rebuildMach_(ss, rows); } catch (e) { console.error('Machinery Repairs rebuild failed: ' + e); }
}
/** Rebuilds the "Machinery Repairs" tab: every machine not fully working, at every site, longest first. */
function rebuildMach_(ss, rows) {
  rows = rows && rows.length ? rows : ledger_(ss);
  var q = stockAsOf_(rows, '', null), mq = machAsOf_(machRows_(ss), '', null), items = itemsById_(ss), t = today_();
  var sites = sitesList_(ss), out = [];
  Object.keys(mq).forEach(function (id) {
    Object.keys(mq[id]).forEach(function (s) {
      if (sites.indexOf(s) < 0) return;
      var v = mq[id][s], tot = q[id] && q[id][s] ? Number(q[id][s][0]) || 0 : 0, e = machEff_(v, tot);
      if (!(e[0] + e[1] > 0)) return;
      var it = items[id] || [id, id, '', '', ''];
      var st = e[1] > 0 && e[0] > 0 ? 'At workshop + needs maintenance' : e[1] > 0 ? 'At workshop' : 'Needs maintenance';
      var since = [e[1] > 0 ? v[5] : '', e[0] > 0 ? v[4] : ''].filter(String).sort()[0] || v[7];
      out.push([s, it[0], it[1], it[4], tot, r3_(tot - e[0] - e[1]), e[0] || '', e[1] || '', st, v[2], e[1] > 0 ? v[3] : '', since, daysSince_(since, t), v[6], v[7]]);
    });
  });
  out.sort(function (a, b) { return b[12] - a[12] || (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : a[0] > b[0] ? 1 : 0); });
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(MACH_TAB_) || ss.insertSheet(MACH_TAB_);
  sh.clear();
  var head = ['Site', 'Item ID', 'Machine', 'Unit', 'Total at site', 'Working', 'Needs maintenance', 'At workshop', 'Status', 'Problem', 'Workshop', 'Since', 'Days', 'Marked by', 'Last updated'];
  room_(sh, out.length + 4, head.length);
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setValues([head]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#B8274A').setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
  if (out.length) {
    sh.getRange(2, 1, out.length, head.length).setValues(out);
    sh.getRange(2, 12, out.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@');
    sh.getRange(2, 15, out.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@');
    sh.getRange(2, 1, out.length, head.length).setBackgrounds(out.map(function (r) {
      var c = r[12] >= 14 ? '#F9D6DE' : r[12] >= 7 ? '#FCEBD3' : '#FFFFFF';
      return head.map(function () { return c; });
    }));
    sh.getRange(2, 9, out.length, 1).setFontColors(out.map(function (r) { return [r[7] ? '#B8274A' : '#B26A00']; })).setFontWeight('bold');
  } else {
    sh.getRange(2, 1).setValue('All machines are working. No machine needs maintenance or is at a workshop.');
  }
  sh.getRange(out.length + 3, 1).setValue('Updates by itself after every save. Red = out 14+ days, orange = 7+ days. Full history: Repair Log tab.')
    .setFontStyle('italic').setFontColor('#6A7079');
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
}

/** Repair history of one site for the storekeeper page (newest first, last 300 changes). */
function machHistory(site, pin, name) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  if (!checkPin_(ss, site, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
  if (isAll_(site)) return { ok: false, error: 'Use Open combined report for ALL SITES' };
  if (name && !checkPin_(ss, site, pin, name)) return { ok: false, error: 'This PIN is not for ' + String(name).trim() + '. Type your own PIN.' };
  var out = [];
  machRows_(ss).forEach(function (r) {
    if (String(r[2]).trim() !== site) return;
    out.push({ t: iso_(r[0]), d: dstr_(r[1]), id: String(r[3]), item: String(r[4]), unit: String(r[5]), m: Number(r[6]) || 0, x: Number(r[7]) || 0,
               problem: String(r[8] || ''), ws: String(r[9] || ''), by: String(r[10] || ''), note: String(r[11] || '') });
  });
  return { ok: true, rows: out.reverse().slice(0, 300) };
}

/**
 * Storekeeper sets a machine's status at their site.
 * maint = how many need maintenance (still at site), rep = how many are at a workshop. 0 and 0 = all working.
 */
function saveMachine(site, pin, name, id, maint, rep, problem, workshop) {
  MEMO_.t0 = Date.now();
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    name = String(name).trim();
    id = String(id);
    var it = itemsById_(ss)[id];
    if (!it) return { ok: false, error: 'Item not found' };
    if (!isMach_(it)) return { ok: false, error: it[1] + ' is not machinery (Items tab: Category or Sub category must be Machinery)' };
    var num = function (x) { x = String(x == null ? '' : x).trim().replace(',', '.'); return x === '' ? 0 : Number(x); };
    var m = num(maint), x = num(rep);
    if (!isFinite(m) || m < 0 || !isFinite(x) || x < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Not a valid number' };
    m = r3_(m); x = r3_(x);
    problem = String(problem || '').trim().slice(0, 200);
    workshop = String(workshop || '').trim().slice(0, 100);
    var rows = ledger_(ss), have = haveOf_(rows, site, id); have = have === null ? 0 : have;
    if (m + x > have) return { ok: false, error: 'Only ' + have + ' ' + it[4] + ' of ' + it[1] + ' at your site. Needs maintenance + at workshop cannot be more.' };
    if (m + x > 0 && !problem) return { ok: false, error: 'Write the problem (what is wrong with the machine)' };
    if (x > 0 && !workshop) return { ok: false, error: 'Write the workshop / garage name' };
    var mq = machAsOf_(machRows_(ss), '', site), o = mq[id] && mq[id][site] ? mq[id][site] : [0, 0, '', '', '', '', '', ''];
    var oe = machEff_(o, have);
    if (oe[0] === m && oe[1] === x && (m + x === 0 || (o[2] === problem && (x === 0 || o[3] === workshop)))) return { ok: false, error: 'Nothing changed' };
    var t = today_(), now = new Date(), notes = [];
    if (x > oe[1]) notes.push('Sent for repair');
    if (x < oe[1]) notes.push('Back from workshop' + (o[5] ? ' after ' + daysSince_(o[5], t) + ' days' : ''));
    if (m > oe[0]) notes.push('Needs maintenance');
    if (m < oe[0] && !(x > oe[1])) notes.push('Maintenance done' + (o[4] ? ' after ' + daysSince_(o[4], t) + ' days' : ''));
    if (!notes.length) notes.push('Details updated');
    var sh = machSheet_(ss), n = sh.getLastRow();
    room_(sh, n + 1);
    sh.getRange(n + 1, 1, 1, 12).setValues([[now, t, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], m, x, m + x > 0 ? problem : '', x > 0 ? workshop : '', name, notes.join(' · ')]]);
    MEMO_.machRows = null;
    // idle + not-working can never be more than the total: lower idle by itself if needed
    var iq = idleAsOf_(idleRows_(ss), '', site), oi = iq[id] && iq[id][site] ? iq[id][site][0] : 0, idleFix = false;
    if (oi > 0 && oi + m + x > have) {
      var ni = r3_(Math.max(0, have - m - x)), ish = idleSheet_(ss), il = ish.getLastRow();
      room_(ish, il + 1);
      ish.getRange(il + 1, 1, 1, 10).setValues([[now, t, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], ni, oi, name, 'Machine not working']]);
      idleFix = true;
    }
    machSafe_(ss, rows);
    if (idleFix) idleSafe_(ss, rows);
    mark_(ss, 'Machine status', site, name);
    return withState_(ss, site, { ok: true, note: notes.join(' · '), item: it[1], idleFix: idleFix }, rows);
  });
}

/* ---------------- "All Sites Stock" tab: built from Google formulas ----------------
 * Items down, sites across, TOTAL. Every cell is a formula that reads the Stock tab, so Google
 * recalculates it by itself the moment anything is saved. The script only lays out the columns
 * (again only when the list of sites changes).
 */
var LIVE_ = 'All Sites Stock';
function colL_(n) { var t = ''; while (n > 0) { var m = (n - 1) % 26; t = String.fromCharCode(65 + m) + t; n = Math.floor((n - 1) / 26); } return t; }
function liveFormulas_(sites) {
  var n = sites.length, f = { items: '=IFERROR(FILTER({Items!A2:A,Items!B2:B,Items!C2:C,Items!E2:E},Items!A2:A<>""),)', sites: [] };
  sites.forEach(function (s, i) {
    var c = colL_(6 + i);
    // newest Stock row first, so VLOOKUP's first match is the latest quantity for this item at this site
    f.sites.push('=ARRAYFORMULA(IF($A2:$A="",,IFERROR(VLOOKUP($A2:$A&"|"&' + c + '$1,SORT({Stock!$D$2:$D&"|"&Stock!$C$2:$C,Stock!$G$2:$G,ROW(Stock!$G$2:$G)},3,FALSE),2,FALSE),"")))');
  });
  var last = colL_(5 + Math.max(n, 1)), rg = '$F2:$' + last, one = 'SEQUENCE(' + Math.max(n, 1) + ',1,1,0)';
  f.total = '=ARRAYFORMULA(IF($A2:$A="",,IF(MMULT((' + rg + '<>"")*1,' + one + ')=0,"",MMULT(IFERROR(' + rg + '*1,0),' + one + '))))';
  return f;
}
function rebuildLive_(ss, rows, force) {
  var full = force || MEMO_.fullCheck || !rows || !rows.length;
  if (!full) {   // quick path after a save: only re-check when the list of sites has changed
    var p0 = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(), sh0 = ss.getSheetByName(LIVE_);
    if (sh0 && p0.getProperty('LIVE_SIG') === VERSION_ + '|' + sitesList_(ss).join('|')) return;
  }
  try { ensureChanges_(ss); } catch (e) { console.error('Changes tab: ' + e); }
  try { var old = ss.getSheetByName('Live Stock'); if (old && LIVE_ !== 'Live Stock') ss.deleteSheet(old); } catch (e) {}
  var sites = sitesList_(ss), sh = ss.getSheetByName(LIVE_), p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var sig = VERSION_ + '|' + sites.join('|');
  if (sh && !force && p.getProperty('LIVE_SIG') === sig && String(sh.getRange(1, 1).getValue()) === 'Item ID') return;
  if (!sh) sh = ss.insertSheet(LIVE_);
  var f = liveFormulas_(sites), n = sites.length;
  sh.clear();
  room_(sh, 2, 5 + n + 2);
  var head = ['Item ID', 'Item', 'Category', 'Unit', 'TOTAL'].concat(sites);
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setValues([head])
    .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#1b7a3a').setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
  sh.getRange(2, 1).setFormula(f.items);
  if (n) sh.getRange(2, 5).setFormula(f.total);
  f.sites.forEach(function (fx, i) { sh.getRange(2, 6 + i).setFormula(fx); });
  sh.getRange(1, head.length + 2).setValue('Updates automatically from the Stock tab');
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  sh.setFrozenColumns(2);
  p.setProperty('LIVE_SIG', sig);
}

function checks_(ss, week) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Checks'), n = sh.getLastRow(), m = {};
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, 5).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
    if (dstr_(r[1]) === week) m[String(r[2]).trim()] = [iso_(r[0]), String(r[3])];
  });
  return m;
}

/* ================= functions the page calls ================= */

/** Items, pictures, sites, storekeeper names and today's date. PINs are never sent. */
function getInit() {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  try { ensureItemCols_(ss); } catch (e) { console.error('Items columns: ' + e); }
  try { oneSitesTab_(ss); } catch (e) { console.error('Sites tab: ' + e); }
  var fl = itemFlags_(ss);
  var items = itemRows_(ss).map(function (r) { var f = fl[String(r[0])] || [true, true, true]; return [r[0], String(r[1]), String(r[2]), String(r[3]), String(r[4]), r[5], f[0], f[1], isMach_(r), f[2] !== false]; });
  var imgs = {};
  ss.getSheetByName('Images').getDataRange().getValues().slice(1).forEach(function (r) {
    if (r[0] !== '') imgs[r[0]] = String(r[1]);
  });
  var names = siteNames_(ss), sites = sitesList_(ss), xs = [];
  if (Object.keys(names).some(isAll_)) sites.push(ALL_);
  var xl = {};
  try { var xn = xNames_(ss), L = xLinks_(ss); xs = xStores_(ss).filter(function (s) { return !names[s] && !L[s]; }); xs.forEach(function (s) { names[s] = xn[s] || []; }); Object.keys(L).forEach(function (st) { xl[L[st]] = st; }); } catch (e) { console.error('Excess stores: ' + e); }
  var pp = '', pv = ''; try { if (ss.getSheetByName(PI_TAB_)) pvSheet_(ss); if (pOn_(ss)) { pp = pSite_(ss); var vn = pvRows_(ss).map(function (r) { return r[0]; }); if (vn.length) { pv = PV_SITE_; names[PV_SITE_] = vn; } } } catch (e) { console.error('PPE: ' + e); }
  return { items: items, imgs: imgs, sites: sites, names: names, xstores: xs, xlink: xl, ppeSite: pp, ppeView: pv, today: today_() };
}

/** One site's current stock + whether this week's check is done. */
function loadSite(site, pin, name) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  if (!checkPin_(ss, site, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
  if (isAll_(site)) return { ok: false, error: 'Use Open combined report for ALL SITES' };
  if (name) { var e0 = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name); if (e0) return { ok: false, error: e0 }; }
  return siteState_(ss, site, null);
}
/** One site's current stock, idle and weekly-check state. Saves return this too, so the page needs no second request. */
function siteState_(ss, site, rows) {
  var t = today_(), wk = mondayOf_(t), q = stockAsOf_(rows || ledger_(ss), '', site), out = {};
  Object.keys(q).forEach(function (id) { out[id] = q[id][site]; });
  return { ok: true, today: t, week: wk, qty: out, idle: idleMap_(ss, '', site, q), mach: machMap_(ss, '', site, q), check: checks_(ss, wk)[site] || null, svc: svSite_(ss, site, t), xin: xInSafe_(ss, site) };
}
function xInSafe_(ss, site) { try { return xIn_(ss, site); } catch (e) { console.error('excess in: ' + e); return []; } }
function withState_(ss, site, res, rows) {
  try { res.state = siteState_(ss, site, rows || null); } catch (e) { console.error('state: ' + e); }
  try {   // remember how long saves take (shown in WTC Stock > Check system)
    if (MEMO_.t0) {
      res.ms = Date.now() - MEMO_.t0;
      var p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(), a = JSON.parse(p.getProperty('SAVE_MS') || '[]');
      a.push([new Date().toISOString(), res.ms]); p.setProperty('SAVE_MS', JSON.stringify(a.slice(-20)));
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return res;
}

/**
 * Past-day view for a storekeeper (read only): the site's stock at the END of that date,
 * plus which items changed on that day (before -> after) and how many changes were recorded.
 */
function loadSiteDay(site, pin, date) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  if (!checkPin_(ss, site, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
  if (isAll_(site)) return { ok: false, error: 'Use Open combined report for ALL SITES' };
  var t = today_();
  date = String(date || '');
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date) || date > t) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose a date up to today' };
  var p = date.split('-'), d = new Date(Date.UTC(+p[0], +p[1] - 1, +p[2]));
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() - 1);
  var prev = fmtUTC_(d), rows = ledger_(ss), q = stockAsOf_(rows, date, site), qp = stockAsOf_(rows, prev, site);
  var out = {}, day = {}, n = 0;
  Object.keys(q).forEach(function (id) {
    var c = q[id][site];
    out[id] = c;
    if (c[1] === date) { var b = qp[id] && qp[id][site]; day[id] = [b ? b[0] : '', c[0]]; }
  });
  rows.forEach(function (r) { if (dstr_(r[1]) === date && String(r[2]).trim() === site) n++; });
  var wk = mondayOf_(date);
  return { ok: true, date: date, today: t, week: wk, qty: out, idle: idleMap_(ss, date, site, q), mach: machMap_(ss, date, site, q), day: day, changes: n, check: checks_(ss, wk)[site] || null };
}

/** { itemId: [idleShown, idleSince] } for one site, only items with idle > 0. */
function idleMap_(ss, date, site, q) {
  var iq = idleAsOf_(idleRows_(ss), date, site), out = {};
  Object.keys(iq).forEach(function (id) {
    var v = iq[id][site], tot = q[id] && q[id][site] ? q[id][site][0] : '', e = effIdle_(v[0], tot);
    if (e > 0) out[id] = [e, v[4] || v[1]];
  });
  return out;
}

/**
 * Saves today's changes. entries = [[itemId, 'qty or empty'], ...]. Only changed items are written.
 * check = true also records this week's weekly check for the site.
 */
function saveStock(site, pin, name, entries, check) {
  MEMO_.t0 = Date.now();
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    name = String(name).trim();
    var items = itemsById_(ss), rows = ledger_(ss), q = stockAsOf_(rows, '', site);
    var iq = idleAsOf_(idleRows_(ss), '', site), idleAdd = [], mq = machAsOf_(machRows_(ss), '', site), machHit = false;
    var t = today_(), now = new Date(), add = [], why = check ? 'Weekly check' : 'Daily update';
    for (var i = 0; i < (entries || []).length; i++) {
      var id = String(entries[i][0]), raw = String(entries[i][1] == null ? '' : entries[i][1]).trim().replace(',', '.'), it = items[id];
      if (!it) continue;
      var val = '';
      if (raw !== '') {
        val = Number(raw);
        if (!isFinite(val) || val < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Not a valid number for: ' + it[1] };
        val = r3_(val);
      }
      var old = q[id] && q[id][site] ? q[id][site][0] : '';
      if (String(old) !== String(val)) add.push([now, t, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], val, old, name, why]);
      // idle (unused) quantity
      var oldIdle = iq[id] && iq[id][site] ? iq[id][site][0] : 0, tot = val === '' ? 0 : val, ir = entries[i][2];
      var mv = mq[id] && mq[id][site], down = mv ? mv[0] + mv[1] : 0;     // machines needing maintenance / at workshop
      if (down > 0 && String(old) !== String(val)) machHit = true;
      if (ir != null) {
        ir = String(ir).trim().replace(',', '.');
        var iv = ir === '' ? 0 : Number(ir);
        if (!isFinite(iv) || iv < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Not a valid idle number for: ' + it[1] };
        iv = r3_(iv);
        if (iv > tot) return { ok: false, error: 'Idle cannot be more than the total for: ' + it[1] + ' (total ' + tot + ')' };
        if (down > 0 && iv > 0 && iv + down > tot) return { ok: false, error: 'Idle + not working (' + down + ') cannot be more than the total for: ' + it[1] + ' (total ' + tot + ')' };
        if (iv !== oldIdle) idleAdd.push([now, t, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], iv, oldIdle, name, why]);
      } else if (oldIdle > tot) {
        idleAdd.push([now, t, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], tot, oldIdle, name, 'Total went below idle']);
      }
    }
    if (add.length) appendLedger_(ss, rows, add);
    if (idleAdd.length) {
      var ish = idleSheet_(ss), il = ish.getLastRow();
      room_(ish, il + idleAdd.length);
      ish.getRange(il + 1, 1, idleAdd.length, 10).setValues(idleAdd);
    }
    if (check) {
      var ck = ss.getSheetByName('Checks');
      room_(ck, ck.getLastRow() + 1);
      ck.getRange(ck.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, 5).setValues([[now, mondayOf_(t), site, name, add.length]]);
    }
    if (add.length || idleAdd.length) liveSafe_(ss, rows);
    if (idleAdd.length) idleSafe_(ss, rows);
    if (machHit) machSafe_(ss, rows);
    mark_(ss, check ? 'Weekly check' : 'Stock update', site, name);
    var idleOnly = idleAdd.filter(function (r) { return r[9] !== 'Total went below idle'; }).length;
    return withState_(ss, site, { ok: true, changed: add.length, idleChanged: idleOnly }, rows);
  });
}

/** ALL SITES view: every site's stock at the end of a date, update + weekly check status, transfers. */
function loadCombined(pin, date) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  var p = String(pin || '').trim();
  var okPin = p !== '' && siteRows_(ss).some(function (r) { return isAll_(r[0]) && String(r[1]).trim() === p; });
  if (!okPin) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var t = today_();
  date = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(date)) ? String(date) : t;
  var sites = sitesList_(ss), rows = ledger_(ss), q0 = stockAsOf_(rows, date, null), q = {}, last = {};
  Object.keys(q0).forEach(function (id) {
    q[id] = {};
    Object.keys(q0[id]).forEach(function (s) { if (sites.indexOf(s) >= 0) q[id][s] = q0[id][s]; });
  });
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var d = dstr_(r[1]), s = String(r[2]).trim();
    if (d <= date && (!last[s] || d >= last[s][2])) last[s] = [iso_(r[0]), String(r[8]), d];
  });
  var wk = mondayOf_(date), trs = trList_(ss), iq = idleAsOf_(idleRows_(ss), date, null), idle = {};
  Object.keys(iq).forEach(function (id) {
    Object.keys(iq[id]).forEach(function (s) {
      var tot = q[id] && q[id][s] ? q[id][s][0] : '', e = effIdle_(iq[id][s][0], tot);
      if (e > 0 && sites.indexOf(s) >= 0) { idle[id] = idle[id] || {}; idle[id][s] = [e, iq[id][s][4] || iq[id][s][1]]; }
    });
  });
  var mq = machAsOf_(machRows_(ss), date, null), mach = {};
  Object.keys(mq).forEach(function (id) {
    Object.keys(mq[id]).forEach(function (s) {
      var v = mq[id][s], tot = q[id] && q[id][s] ? q[id][s][0] : '', e = machEff_(v, tot);
      if (e[0] + e[1] > 0 && sites.indexOf(s) >= 0) { mach[id] = mach[id] || {}; mach[id][s] = [e[0], e[1], v[2], v[3], e[0] > 0 ? v[4] : '', e[1] > 0 ? v[5] : '', v[6]]; }
    });
  });
  return {
    ok: true, date: date, today: t, week: wk, sites: sites, q: q, idle: idle, mach: mach, last: last, checks: checks_(ss, wk),
    transit: trs.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'In transit'; }),
    recent: trs.filter(function (x) { return x.status !== 'In transit'; }).slice(-40).reverse(),
    shortages: trs.filter(function (x) { return x.pending > 0; }).reverse(),
    moves: mvRecent_(ss, 40), rent: rnByItem_(ss, date), svc: svAll_(ss, date)
  };
}

/* ======================= Transfers between sites =======================
 * One transfer (one TR number) can carry many items. The Transfers tab has one row per item,
 * and all the rows of a transfer share the same TR number.
 */

/** All transfers, grouped by TR number. Each has lines: [{ row, itemId, item, unit, qty, status, recQty }]. */
function trList_(ss) {
  var sh = trSheet_(ss), last = sh.getLastRow();
  if (last < 2) return [];
  var byId = {}, order = [];
  sh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, 17).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    var id = String(r[0]);
    if (!id) return;
    var t = byId[id];
    if (!t) {
      t = byId[id] = { id: id, sent: iso_(r[1]), date: dstr_(r[1]), from: String(r[2]), to: String(r[3]), by: String(r[8]),
                       note: String(r[9]), rec: '', recBy: '', lines: [] };
      order.push(id);
    }
    t.lines.push({ row: i + 2, itemId: r[4], item: String(r[5]), unit: String(r[6]), qty: Number(r[7]),
                   status: String(r[10]), recQty: r[13] === '' ? '' : Number(r[13]),
                   shortage: String(r[14] || ''), shortBy: String(r[15] || ''), shortAt: iso_(r[16]) });
    var L = t.lines[t.lines.length - 1];
    L.missing = L.recQty !== '' && L.status.indexOf('Received') === 0 && L.qty > L.recQty ? r3_(L.qty - L.recQty) : 0;
    L.pending = L.missing > 0 && (L.shortage === '' || L.shortage === 'Pending');
    if (r[11] !== '') { t.rec = iso_(r[11]); t.recBy = String(r[12]); }
  });
  return order.map(function (id) {
    var t = byId[id], st = t.lines.map(function (l) { return l.status; });
    t.pending = t.lines.filter(function (l) { return l.pending; }).length;
    var canc = function (s) { return s.indexOf('Cancelled') === 0; }, live = st.filter(function (s) { return !canc(s); });
    t.status = st.indexOf('In transit') >= 0 ? 'In transit'
      : !live.length ? (st.indexOf('Cancelled by office') >= 0 ? 'Cancelled by office' : 'Cancelled')
      : live.every(function (s) { return s === 'Received'; }) ? 'Received' : 'Received with differences';
    t.cancelled = !live.length;
    return t;
  });
}

function nextTrId_(ss) {
  return 'TR-' + ('000' + (maxRef_(trSheet_(ss)) + 1)).slice(-4);
}
/** Highest reference number in column A (reads only that column). */
function maxRef_(sh) {
  var last = sh.getLastRow(), max = 0;
  if (last < 2) return 0;
  sh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, 1).getValues().forEach(function (r) { var n = Number(String(r[0]).replace(/\D/g, '')); if (n > max) max = n; });
  return max;
}

/** Date range helper: 'yyyy-MM-dd' strings; default = the last 30 days. */
function range_(from, to) {
  var t = today_(), ok = function (x) { return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(x || '')); };
  var p = t.split('-'), d = new Date(Date.UTC(+p[0], +p[1] - 1, +p[2]));
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() - 30);
  var f = ok(from) ? String(from) : fmtUTC_(d), e = ok(to) ? String(to) : t;
  return f <= e ? [f, e] : [e, f];
}

/**
 * Storekeeper's transfer screen: incoming (on the way), shortages to answer, and the history
 * (sent or received by this site, between two dates). Transfers cancelled by the office are not shown.
 */
function loadTransfers(site, pin, from, to) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  if (!checkPin_(ss, site, pin) || isAll_(site)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
  var trs = trList_(ss), rg = range_(from, to);
  return {
    ok: true, from: rg[0], to: rg[1],
    incoming: trs.filter(function (x) { return x.to === site && x.status === 'In transit'; }).reverse(),
    shortages: trs.filter(function (x) { return x.from === site && x.pending > 0; }).reverse(),
    history: trs.filter(function (x) {
      return (x.from === site || x.to === site) && !x.cancelled && x.date >= rg[0] && x.date <= rg[1] &&
             !(x.to === site && x.status === 'In transit');
    }).reverse().slice(0, 300)
  };
}

/** lines = [[itemId, qty], ...]. All items go in one transfer with one TR number. */
function sendTransfer(site, pin, name, toSite, lines, note) {
  MEMO_.t0 = Date.now();
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    name = String(name).trim(); toSite = String(toSite || '').trim();
    if (sitesList_(ss).indexOf(toSite) < 0 || toSite === site) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose the site you are sending to' };
    if (!lines || !lines.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Add at least one item' };
    var items = itemsById_(ss), want = {}, order = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      var item = items[String(lines[i][0])];
      if (!item) return { ok: false, error: 'An item on the list was not found. Remove it and add it again.' };
      var q = Number(String(lines[i][1]).replace(',', '.'));
      if (!isFinite(q) || q <= 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Enter a quantity more than 0 for: ' + item[1] };
      var k = String(item[0]);
      if (!want[k]) { want[k] = 0; order.push(k); }
      want[k] = r3_(want[k] + q);
    }
    var rows = ledger_(ss), now = new Date(), t = today_(), id = nextTrId_(ss), add = [], tr = [];
    for (var j = 0; j < order.length; j++) {
      var it = items[order[j]], qty = want[order[j]], have = haveOf_(rows, site, it[0]);
      if (have === null) return { ok: false, error: 'No stock of ' + it[1] + ' is recorded at your site. Enter its quantity first.' };
      if (qty > have) return { ok: false, error: 'You only have ' + have + ' ' + it[4] + ' of ' + it[1] + ' recorded at your site.' };
      add.push([now, t, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], r3_(have - qty), have, name, 'Sent ' + id + ' to ' + toSite]);
      tr.push([id, now, site, toSite, it[0], it[1], it[4], qty, name, String(note || '').slice(0, 100), 'In transit', '', '', '', '', '', '']);
    }
    appendLedger_(ss, rows, add);
    var sh = trSheet_(ss);
    room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + tr.length, 17);
    sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, tr.length, 17).setValues(tr);
    liveSafe_(ss, rows);
    idleIfNeeded_(ss, rows, site, order);
    mark_(ss, 'Transfer ' + id, site, name);
    return withState_(ss, site, { ok: true, id: id, count: tr.length }, rows);
  });
}

/** recs = [[itemId, qtyReceived], ...]. Missing items count as fully received. Receives the whole transfer. */
function receiveTransfer(site, pin, name, id, recs) {
  MEMO_.t0 = Date.now();
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    name = String(name).trim();
    var t = trList_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.id === String(id); })[0];
    if (!t || t.to !== site) return { ok: false, error: 'Transfer not found for this site' };
    if (t.status !== 'In transit') return { ok: false, error: 'This transfer is already ' + t.status.toLowerCase() };
    var got = {};
    (recs || []).forEach(function (r) { got[String(r[0])] = r[1]; });
    var items = itemsById_(ss), rows = ledger_(ss), now = new Date(), d0 = today_(), add = [], upd = [], diff = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < t.lines.length; i++) {
      var l = t.lines[i];
      if (l.status !== 'In transit') continue;
      var raw = got[String(l.itemId)];
      var q = (raw === '' || raw == null) ? l.qty : Number(String(raw).replace(',', '.'));
      if (!isFinite(q) || q < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Enter the quantity you received for: ' + l.item };
      var it = items[String(l.itemId)];
      if (it) {
        var have = haveOf_(rows, site, it[0]);
        add.push([now, d0, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], r3_((have || 0) + q), have === null ? '' : have,
                  name, 'Received ' + t.id + ' from ' + t.from]);
        rows.push(add[add.length - 1]);
      }
      var d = r3_(l.qty - q);
      if (d !== 0) diff++;
      upd.push([l.row, d === 0 ? 'Received' : d > 0 ? 'Received (short ' + d + ')' : 'Received (extra ' + (-d) + ')', q, d > 0 ? 'Pending' : '']);
    }
    if (add.length) {
      rows.splice(rows.length - add.length, add.length);
      appendLedger_(ss, rows, add);
    }
    var sh = trSheet_(ss);
    upd.forEach(function (u) { sh.getRange(u[0], 11, 1, 5).setValues([[u[1], now, name, u[2], u[3]]]); });
    liveSafe_(ss, rows);
    mark_(ss, 'Received ' + t.id, site, name);
    return withState_(ss, site, { ok: true, status: diff ? 'Received with differences' : 'Received', diff: diff }, rows);
  });
}

/** Storekeepers can no longer cancel: only the office can (WTC Stock > Cancel a transaction). */
function cancelTransfer() { return { ok: false, error: 'Only the office can cancel a transfer. Please call the office.' }; }

/**
 * Shortage on a received transfer: the SENDING site answers for each missing item.
 * action 'here' = the missing quantity is still at the sender -> added back to the sender's stock.
 * action 'lost' = not at the sender either -> marked "Lost" for the office to check (no stock change).
 */
function resolveShortage(site, pin, name, id, itemId, action) {
  MEMO_.t0 = Date.now();
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    name = String(name).trim();
    var t = trList_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.id === String(id); })[0];
    if (!t || t.from !== site) return { ok: false, error: 'Transfer not found for this site' };
    var l = t.lines.filter(function (x) { return String(x.itemId) === String(itemId); })[0];
    if (!l || !l.pending) return { ok: false, error: 'This shortage is already answered' };
    var now = new Date(), label;
    if (action === 'here') {
      var it = itemsById_(ss)[String(l.itemId)], rows = ledger_(ss);
      if (it) {
        var have = haveOf_(rows, site, it[0]);
        appendLedger_(ss, rows, [[now, today_(), site, it[0], it[1], it[4], r3_((have || 0) + l.missing), have === null ? '' : have,
          name, 'Shortage of ' + t.id + ' still here']]);
        liveSafe_(ss, rows);
      }
      label = 'Returned to sender';
    } else if (action === 'lost') {
      label = 'Lost';
    } else {
      return { ok: false, error: 'Choose Still here or Not here' };
    }
    trSheet_(ss).getRange(l.row, 15, 1, 3).setValues([[label, name, now]]);
    mark_(ss, 'Shortage ' + t.id, site, name);
    return withState_(ss, site, { ok: true, label: label, missing: l.missing, item: l.item });
  });
}

/* ============ Stock in / out: Received from supplier, Used (consumables), Damaged (write-off) ============
 * Each type has its own tab. One entry (one Ref number) can hold many items: one row per item.
 * Received adds to the site's stock; Used and Damaged take it away. The Stock tab records every change.
 * Only the office can cancel an entry, from the sheet menu (WTC Stock > Cancel a transaction).
 */
var MV_ = {
  received: { tab: 'Received', prefix: 'RC', sign: 1, i1: 'Supplier', i2: 'Delivery note / LPO no.', need1: true, word: 'Received' },
  used:     { tab: 'Used', prefix: 'US', sign: -1, i1: 'Used for (job / area)', i2: 'Note', need1: false, word: 'Used' },
  damaged:  { tab: 'Damaged', prefix: 'DM', sign: -1, i1: 'Reason', i2: 'Note', need1: true, word: 'Damaged' }
};
function mvSheet_(ss, type) {
  var m = MV_[type];
  return tab_(ss, m.tab, ['Ref', 'Time', 'Date', 'Site', 'Item ID', 'Item', 'Unit', 'Qty', 'Stock before', 'Stock after',
                         'By', m.i1, m.i2, 'Status', 'Cancelled by', 'Cancelled time'].concat(type === 'damaged' ? ['Photos'] : []), ['C']);
}
/* ---- damaged items: a photo of every damaged item is required (saved in Drive “WTC Damaged Photos”, IDs in column Q “Photos”) ---- */
var DM_MAX_PH_ = 3;
function dmPhotos_(p) { return (p || []).filter(function (x) { return /^data:image\/\w+;base64,/.test(x || '') && String(x).length < 6e6; }); }
function dmSave_(ref, item, photos) {
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Damaged Photos'), folder = it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Damaged Photos'), ids = [];
  photos.forEach(function (p, k) {
    try { var m = String(p).match(/^data:(image\/\w+);base64,(.+)$/); ids.push(folder.createFile(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(m[2]), m[1], ref + ' ' + String(item).replace(/[\\\/:*?"<>|]/g, ' ').slice(0, 60) + ' ' + (k + 1) + '.jpg')).getId()); }
    catch (e) { console.error('damaged photo: ' + e); }
  });
  return ids;
}
/** Shows one damaged-item photo (only to the office or the site that recorded it). */
function dmPhoto(site, pin, name, id) {
  var ss = ss_(); site = String(site || '').trim(); id = String(id || '').trim();
  var office = isAll_(site);
  if (office ? !okOffice_(ss, pin) : !checkPin_(ss, site, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
  var ok = id && mvList_(ss, 'damaged').some(function (g) { return (office || g.site === site) && g.lines.some(function (l) { return (l.ph || []).indexOf(id) >= 0; }); });
  if (!ok) return { ok: false, error: 'Photo not found' };
  try { var b = DriveApp.getFileById(id).getBlob(); return { ok: true, id: id, src: 'data:' + (b.getContentType() || 'image/jpeg') + ';base64,' + Utilities.base64Encode(b.getBytes()) }; }
  catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: 'Could not open the photo.' }; }
}
function mvList_(ss, type) {
  var sh = mvSheet_(ss, type), last = sh.getLastRow();
  if (last < 2) return [];
  var by = {}, order = [], wid = type === 'damaged' ? Math.min(17, sh.getMaxColumns()) : 16;
  sh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, wid).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    var ref = String(r[0]);
    if (!ref) return;
    var g = by[ref];
    if (!g) {
      g = by[ref] = { type: type, ref: ref, time: iso_(r[1]), date: dstr_(r[2]), site: String(r[3]), by: String(r[10]),
                      info1: String(r[11]), info2: String(r[12]), status: String(r[13] || 'Recorded'),
                      cancelBy: String(r[14] || ''), lines: [] };
      order.push(ref);
    }
    g.lines.push({ row: i + 2, itemId: r[4], item: String(r[5]), unit: String(r[6]), qty: Number(r[7]),
                   before: r[8] === '' ? '' : Number(r[8]), after: Number(r[9]), ph: String(r[16] || '').split(/\s+/).filter(String) });
  });
  return order.map(function (k) { return by[k]; });
}
function mvRecent_(ss, n) {
  var all = [];
  Object.keys(MV_).forEach(function (t) { all = all.concat(mvList_(ss, t)); });
  all.sort(function (a, b) { return a.time < b.time ? 1 : a.time > b.time ? -1 : 0; });
  return all.slice(0, n);
}

/** This site's entries of one type between two dates (newest first). Entries cancelled by the office are not shown. */
function loadMovements(site, pin, type, from, to) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  if (!checkPin_(ss, site, pin) || isAll_(site)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
  if (!MV_[type]) return { ok: false, error: 'Unknown type' };
  var rg = range_(from, to);
  return { ok: true, today: today_(), from: rg[0], to: rg[1], list: mvList_(ss, type).filter(function (g) {
    return g.site === site && g.status.indexOf('Cancelled') !== 0 && g.date >= rg[0] && g.date <= rg[1];
  }).reverse().slice(0, 300) };
}

/** lines = [[itemId, qty], ...]; info1 = supplier / used for / reason; info2 = delivery note / note. */
function recordMovement(site, pin, name, type, lines, info1, info2) {
  MEMO_.t0 = Date.now();
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    var m = MV_[type];
    if (!m) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose Received, Used or Damaged' };
    name = String(name).trim(); info1 = String(info1 || '').trim().slice(0, 100); info2 = String(info2 || '').trim().slice(0, 100);
    if (m.need1 && !info1) return { ok: false, error: 'Enter the ' + m.i1.toLowerCase() };
    if (!lines || !lines.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Add at least one item' };
    var items = itemsById_(ss), want = {}, order = [], fl = itemFlags_(ss), phs = {};
    for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      var item = items[String(lines[i][0])];
      if (!item) return { ok: false, error: 'An item on the list was not found. Remove it and add it again.' };
      var f = fl[String(item[0])] || [true, true];
      if ((type === 'used' && !f[0]) || (type === 'damaged' && !f[1]))
        return { ok: false, error: item[1] + ' cannot be recorded as ' + (type === 'used' ? 'Used' : 'Damaged') + '. Remove it, or ask the office.' };
      var q = Number(String(lines[i][1]).replace(',', '.'));
      if (!isFinite(q) || q <= 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Enter a quantity more than 0 for: ' + item[1] };
      var k = String(item[0]);
      if (!want[k]) { want[k] = 0; order.push(k); }
      want[k] = r3_(want[k] + q);
      if (type === 'damaged') {
        var p = dmPhotos_(lines[i][2]);
        if (!p.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Add at least 1 photo of the damaged ' + item[1] + '.' };
        phs[k] = (phs[k] || []).concat(p).slice(0, DM_MAX_PH_ + 2);
      }
    }
    var rows = ledger_(ss), sh = mvSheet_(ss, type), now = new Date(), t = today_();
    var max = 0;
    max = maxRef_(sh);
    var ref = m.prefix + '-' + ('000' + (max + 1)).slice(-4), add = [], out = [], dmIds = {};
    for (var j = 0; j < order.length; j++) {
      var it = items[order[j]], qty = want[order[j]], have = haveOf_(rows, site, it[0]);
      if (m.sign < 0) {
        if (have === null) return { ok: false, error: 'No stock of ' + it[1] + ' is recorded at your site. Enter its quantity first.' };
        if (qty > have) return { ok: false, error: 'You only have ' + have + ' ' + it[4] + ' of ' + it[1] + ' recorded at your site.' };
      }
      var after = r3_((have || 0) + m.sign * qty);
      if (type === 'damaged') {
        dmIds[order[j]] = dmSave_(ref, it[1], phs[order[j]]);
        if (!dmIds[order[j]].length) return { ok: false, error: 'The photo of ' + it[1] + ' could not be saved. Please try again.' };
      }
      add.push([now, t, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], after, have === null ? '' : have, name,
                m.word + ' ' + ref + (type === 'received' ? ' from ' + info1 : info1 ? ' – ' + info1 : '')]);
      out.push([ref, now, t, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], qty, have === null ? '' : have, after, name, info1, info2, 'Recorded', '', ''].concat(type === 'damaged' ? [dmIds[order[j]].join(' ')] : []));
    }
    var wid = out[0].length;
    if (wid > 16) room_(sh, 1, 17);
    if (wid > 16 && String(sh.getRange(1, 17).getValue()) === '') { sh.getRange(1, 17).setValue('Photos').setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#C42D4E').setFontColor('#FFFFFF'); }
    appendLedger_(ss, rows, add);
    room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + out.length, wid);
    sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, out.length, wid).setValues(out);
    liveSafe_(ss, rows);
    if (m.sign < 0) idleIfNeeded_(ss, rows, site, order);
    mark_(ss, m.word + ' ' + ref, site, name);
    return withState_(ss, site, { ok: true, ref: ref, count: out.length }, rows);
  });
}

/** Storekeepers can no longer cancel: only the office can (WTC Stock > Cancel a transaction). */
function cancelMovement() { return { ok: false, error: 'Only the office can cancel an entry. Please call the office.' }; }

/* ---------------- "Changes" tab: every change, newest first, built from formulas ----------------
 * Reads the Stock tab, so it updates by itself the moment anything is saved.
 * The old weekly Changes tab (if still here) is kept and renamed "Changes - weekly history".
 */
var CH_HEAD_ = ['Time', 'Site', 'Item', 'Unit', 'Old qty', 'New qty', 'Change', 'By', 'Reason'];
var CH_FORMULA_ = '=IFERROR(ARRAYFORMULA(SORT(FILTER({Stock!A2:A,Stock!C2:C,Stock!E2:E,Stock!F2:F,Stock!H2:H,Stock!G2:G,' +
  'IF(Stock!G2:G="",IF(Stock!H2:H="",,0-Stock!H2:H),Stock!G2:G-IF(Stock!H2:H="",0,Stock!H2:H)),Stock!I2:I,Stock!J2:J},Stock!A2:A<>""),1,FALSE)),)';
function ensureChanges_(ss) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Changes');
  if (sh && String(sh.getRange(1, 3).getValue()) === 'Month') {          // the old weekly Changes tab
    if (!ss.getSheetByName('Changes - weekly history')) sh.setName('Changes - weekly history');
    else ss.deleteSheet(sh);
    sh = null;
  }
  if (sh && String(sh.getRange(2, 1).getFormula()).indexOf('Stock!') >= 0) return;   // already in place
  if (sh && sh.getLastRow() > 1) { sh.setName('Changes - old ' + today_()); sh = null; } // never wipe unknown data
  if (!sh) sh = ss.insertSheet('Changes');
  sh.clear();
  room_(sh, 2, CH_HEAD_.length);
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, CH_HEAD_.length).setValues([CH_HEAD_])
    .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#4a4a55').setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
  sh.getRange(2, 1).setFormula(CH_FORMULA_);
  sh.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('dd mmm yyyy hh:mm');
  sh.getRange('G:G').setNumberFormat('+0.###;-0.###;0');
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
}

/** Copies the old weekly Changes tab back from the backup file that Tidy up made in your Google Drive. */
function restoreOldChanges() {
  var ss = ss_(), msg;
  if (ss.getSheetByName('Changes - weekly history')) {
    msg = 'The old weekly Changes are already here, on the "Changes - weekly history" tab.';
  } else {
    var it = DriveApp.searchFiles('title contains "WTC Stock - old weekly tabs backup" and trashed = false'), best = null;
    while (it.hasNext()) { var f = it.next(); if (!best || f.getDateCreated() > best.getDateCreated()) best = f; }
    var src = best ? SpreadsheetApp.openById(best.getId()).getSheetByName('Changes') : null;
    if (!src) {
      msg = 'No backup with a Changes tab was found in your Google Drive, so nothing was restored.';
    } else {
      src.copyTo(ss).setName('Changes - weekly history');
      msg = 'Done. The old weekly Changes are back on the "Changes - weekly history" tab.';
    }
  }
  try { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(msg); } catch (e) {}
  return msg;
}

/* ======================= Office tools ======================= */
function okOffice_(ss, pin) {
  var p = String(pin || '').trim();
  return p !== '' && siteRows_(ss).some(function (r) { return isAll_(r[0]) && String(r[1]).trim() === p; });
}

/**
 * ALL SITES "Transactions" tab. type = received | used | damaged | transfers.
 * Every entry between two dates (cancelled ones included, marked). For transfers, anything still
 * in transit or with an open shortage is always included, whatever its date.
 */
function loadOfficeLog(pin, type, from, to) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var rg = range_(from, to), list;
  if (type === 'transfers') {
    list = trList_(ss).filter(function (x) {
      return (x.date >= rg[0] && x.date <= rg[1]) || x.status === 'In transit' || x.pending > 0;
    });
  } else if (MV_[type]) {
    list = mvList_(ss, type).filter(function (g) { return g.date >= rg[0] && g.date <= rg[1]; });
  } else {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Unknown type' };
  }
  return { ok: true, type: type, from: rg[0], to: rg[1], sites: sitesList_(ss), list: list.reverse().slice(0, 2000) };
}

/** Sheet menu: WTC Stock > Cancel a transaction (office only). Asks for the ref no., shows what will change, then cancels. */
function cancelByOffice() {
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var r = ui.prompt('Cancel a transaction',
    'Type the reference number, for example RC-0001 (received), US-0002 (used), DM-0003 (damaged), TR-0004 (transfer), SV-0005 (site service), EXM-0006 (excess store) or SIM-0007 (other site item):',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL);
  if (r.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;
  var ref = r.getResponseText().trim().toUpperCase(), ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  var plan = officeCancel_(ss, ref, true);
  if (!plan.ok) { ui.alert(plan.error); return; }
  if (ui.alert('Cancel ' + ref + '?', plan.summary + '\n\nThe stock will be corrected, and the entry will disappear from the storekeeper’s page.',
      ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO) !== ui.Button.YES) return;
  var res = withLock_(function () { return officeCancel_(ss, ref, false); });
  ui.alert(res.ok ? 'Done. ' + ref + ' is cancelled and the stock is corrected.' : res.error);
}
