/** Works out (dry = true) or applies the stock corrections that cancel one RC / US / DM / TR reference. */
function officeCancel_(ss, ref, dry) {
  ref = String(ref || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if (ref.slice(0, 3) === 'EXM') return xCancel_(ss, ref, dry);
  if (ref.slice(0, 3) === 'SIM') return siCancel_(ss, ref, dry);
  if (ref.slice(0, 2) === 'SV') return svCancel_(ss, ref, dry);
  var items = itemsById_(ss), rows = ledger_(ss), tmp = rows.slice(), now = new Date(), d0 = today_();
  var add = [], notes = [], marks = [], desc = '';
  function move(site, it, delta) {
    if (!delta) return;
    var have = haveOf_(tmp, site, it[0]), after = r3_((have || 0) + delta);
    if (after < 0) throw new Error('Cannot cancel ' + ref + ': ' + site + ' has only ' + (have || 0) + ' ' + it[4] + ' of ' + it[1] +
      ' now, but ' + (-delta) + ' must be taken back. Correct that site’s stock first.');
    var row = [now, d0, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], after, have === null ? '' : have, 'Office', 'Cancelled by office ' + ref];
    add.push(row); tmp.push(row);
    notes.push(site + ': ' + it[1] + ' ' + (delta > 0 ? '+' : '') + delta + ' ' + it[4] + ' (' + (have || 0) + ' → ' + after + ')');
  }
  try {
    var p = ref.slice(0, 2), type = p === 'RC' ? 'received' : p === 'US' ? 'used' : p === 'DM' ? 'damaged' : null;
    if (type) {
      var m = MV_[type], g = mvList_(ss, type).filter(function (x) { return x.ref === ref; })[0];
      if (!g) return { ok: false, error: 'No entry ' + ref + ' was found.' };
      if (g.status.indexOf('Cancelled') === 0) return { ok: false, error: ref + ' is already cancelled.' };
      var msh = mvSheet_(ss, type);
      g.lines.forEach(function (l) {
        var it = items[String(l.itemId)];
        if (it) move(g.site, it, -m.sign * l.qty);
        marks.push([msh, l.row, 14, ['Cancelled by office', 'Office', now]]);
      });
      desc = m.word + ' ' + ref + ' · ' + g.site + ' · ' + g.date + ' · by ' + g.by + (g.info1 ? ' · ' + g.info1 : '');
    } else if (p === 'TR') {
      var t = trList_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.id === ref; })[0];
      if (!t) return { ok: false, error: 'No transfer ' + ref + ' was found.' };
      if (t.cancelled) return { ok: false, error: ref + ' is already cancelled.' };
      var tsh = trSheet_(ss);
      t.lines.forEach(function (l) {
        if (l.status.indexOf('Cancelled') === 0) return;
        var it = items[String(l.itemId)];
        if (it) {
          if (l.status === 'In transit') {
            move(t.from, it, l.qty);
          } else {
            var rq = l.recQty === '' ? l.qty : l.recQty;
            move(t.to, it, -rq);
            move(t.from, it, r3_(l.qty - (l.shortage === 'Returned to sender' ? l.missing : 0)));
          }
        }
        marks.push([tsh, l.row, 11, ['Cancelled by office']]);
      });
      desc = 'Transfer ' + ref + ' · ' + t.from + ' → ' + t.to + ' · ' + t.date + ' · ' + t.status;
    } else {
      return { ok: false, error: 'Unknown reference "' + ref + '". It must start with RC, US, DM or TR.' };
    }
  } catch (e) {
    return { ok: false, error: e.message };
  }
  if (dry) return { ok: true, summary: desc + '\n\nStock changes:\n' + (notes.length ? notes.join('\n') : 'none') };
  if (add.length) appendLedger_(ss, rows, add);
  marks.forEach(function (k) { k[0].getRange(k[1], k[2], 1, k[3].length).setValues([k[3]]); });
  liveSafe_(ss, rows);
  return { ok: true };
}

/* ---------------- Stock-list PDF for project managers ---------------- */
var LOGO_ = function esc_(x) { return String(x == null ? '' : x).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;'); }

/**
 * The rows of the stock list: only the sites that hold at least one of the chosen items get a column;
 * a site with 0 or no quantity for an item is left blank.
 */
function stockListData_(ss, date, ids, onlySite, req) {
  var t = today_();
  date = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(date)) && String(date) <= t ? String(date) : t;
  var sites = sitesList_(ss);
  if (onlySite && sites.indexOf(onlySite) >= 0) sites = [onlySite];
  var q = stockAsOf_(ledger_(ss), date, null), items = itemsById_(ss), imgs = {};
  ss.getSheetByName('Images').getDataRange().getValues().slice(1).forEach(function (r) { if (r[0] !== '') imgs[r[0]] = String(r[1]); });
  var transit = {};
  if (date === t) trList_(ss).forEach(function (x) {
    if (x.status !== 'In transit' || (sites.length === 1 && x.from !== sites[0] && x.to !== sites[0])) return;
    x.lines.forEach(function (l) { if (l.status === 'In transit') transit[String(l.itemId)] = r3_((transit[String(l.itemId)] || 0) + l.qty); });
  });
  var chosen = (ids || []).map(String).filter(function (id) { return items[id]; });
  var num = function (id, s) { var c = q[id] && q[id][s]; return c && c[0] !== '' ? Number(c[0]) : 0; };
  var iq = idleAsOf_(idleRows_(ss), date, null);
  var idl = function (id, s) { var c = iq[id] && iq[id][s]; return c ? effIdle_(c[0], num(id, s)) : 0; };
  var mq = machAsOf_(machRows_(ss), date, null);
  var mch = function (id, s) { var c = mq[id] && mq[id][s]; return c ? machEff_(c, num(id, s)) : [0, 0]; };
  var used = sites.filter(function (s) { return chosen.some(function (id) { return num(id, s) > 0; }); });
  var showTransit = chosen.some(function (id) { return transit[id] > 0; });
  var rows = chosen.map(function (id) {
    var it = items[id], tot = 0, itot = 0;
    var vals = used.map(function (s) { var v = num(id, s); tot += v > 0 ? v : 0; return v > 0 ? r3_(v) : ''; });
    var idles = used.map(function (s) { var v = idl(id, s); itot += v; return v > 0 ? r3_(v) : ''; });
    var mt = 0, xt = 0;
    var machs = used.map(function (s) {
      var e = mch(id, s); mt += e[0]; xt += e[1];
      return [e[1] > 0 ? e[1] + ' at workshop' : '', e[0] > 0 ? e[0] + ' needs maint.' : ''].filter(String).join(', ');
    });
    mt = r3_(mt); xt = r3_(xt);
    var status = [xt > 0 ? xt + ' at workshop' : '', mt > 0 ? mt + ' needs maintenance' : ''].filter(String).join(' · ');
    return { id: id, name: String(it[1]), cat: String(it[2]), unit: String(it[4]), img: imgs[it[5]] || '',
             vals: vals, idles: idles, machs: machs, status: status, transit: transit[id] > 0 ? transit[id] : '', total: r3_(tot),
             idle: itot > 0 ? r3_(itot) : '', inUse: r3_(Math.max(0, tot - itot - mt - xt)), req: reqOf_(req, id) };
  });
  var showReq = rows.some(function (r) { return r.req !== ''; });
  var showIdle = showReq || rows.some(function (r) { return r.idle !== ''; });   // with Required qty the IDLE column is always shown (balance uses it)
  var showMach = rows.some(function (r) { return r.status !== ''; });
  var now = new Date();
  return {
    date: date, rows: rows, sites: used, allSites: sites.length, showTransit: showTransit, showIdle: showIdle, showMach: showMach, showReq: showReq,
    oneSite: sites.length === 1 ? sites[0] : '',
    when: date === t ? 'Live stock at ' + Utilities.formatDate(now, TZ_, 'dd MMM yyyy, HH:mm')
                     : 'Stock at end of ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(date + 'T12:00:00+04:00'), TZ_, 'dd MMM yyyy'),
    made: Utilities.formatDate(now, TZ_, 'dd MMM yyyy, HH:mm'),
    name: 'Stock list ' + Utilities.formatDate(now, TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd HHmm') + '.pdf'
  };
}

/**
 * Builds a LANDSCAPE PDF stock list of the chosen items with the company logo, via a temporary Google Doc.
 * Saved in your Google Drive folder "WTC Stock PDFs"; also returned so the page can download it.
 */
/** Required qty typed on the ALL SITES screen (only used for this PDF, never saved). */
function reqOf_(req, id) {
  var v = req && req[id] != null ? String(req[id]).trim().replace(',', '.') : '';
  var n = Number(v);
  return v !== '' && isFinite(n) && n >= 0 ? r3_(n) : '';
}
function makeStockPdf(pin, date, ids, onlySite, req) {
  try {
    var ss = ss_();
    ensure_(ss);
    if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
    if (!ids || !ids.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Select at least one item' };
    var d = stockListData_(ss, date, ids, onlySite, req);
    if (!d.rows.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Select at least one item' };
    return pdfOut_(d);
  } catch (e) {
    var m = String(e && e.message || e);
    return { ok: false, error: /authoriz|permission/i.test(m)
      ? 'Google needs your permission first: open the Google Sheet and click WTC Stock > Make a test PDF, then allow access. (' + m + ')'
      : 'Could not create the PDF: ' + m };
  }
}

/**
 * Storekeeper's own PDF: the items in stock at THEIR site (the ones shown on their page), any date up to today.
 */
function makeSitePdf(site, pin, name, date, ids) {
  try {
    var ss = ss_();
    ensure_(ss);
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    var t = today_();
    date = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(date || '')) && String(date) <= t ? String(date) : '';
    var q = stockAsOf_(ledger_(ss), date, site), want = (ids || []).map(String);
    if (!want.length) want = Object.keys(q);
    want = want.filter(function (id) { var c = q[id] && q[id][site]; return c && c[0] !== '' && Number(c[0]) > 0; });
    if (!want.length) return { ok: false, error: 'No items in stock to put in the PDF' };
    var order = itemRows_(ss).map(function (r) { return String(r[0]); });
    want.sort(function (a, b) { return order.indexOf(a) - order.indexOf(b); });
    var d = stockListData_(ss, date, want, site, null);
    d.when += '   ·   Prepared by ' + String(name).trim();
    d.name = 'Stock list ' + site + ' ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd HHmm') + '.pdf';
    var res = pdfOut_(d);
    if (!res.ok && /permission first/.test(res.error)) res.error = 'The office must allow PDFs first (WTC Stock > Make a test PDF). Please tell the office.';
    return res;
  } catch (e) {
    var m = String(e && e.message || e);
    return { ok: false, error: /authoriz|permission/i.test(m) ? 'The office must allow PDFs first (WTC Stock > Make a test PDF). Please tell the office.' : 'Could not create the PDF: ' + m };
  }
}

/* ======================= One PDF per transaction (Received / Used / Damaged / Transfer) =======================
 * The storekeeper can print entries of their own site (a transfer: sending or receiving site); the office (ALL SITES PIN) any entry.
 */
var TX_KIND_ = { RC: 'received', US: 'used', DM: 'damaged', TR: 'transfer' };
var TX_TITLE_ = { received: ['RECEIVED FROM SUPPLIER', '#1B7A3A'], used: ['USED / CONSUMED', '#B26A00'], damaged: ['DAMAGED – WRITE-OFF', '#C42D4E'], transfer: ['TRANSFER BETWEEN SITES', '#1F5FA8'] };
function txImgs_(ss) {
  var im = {}, items = itemsById_(ss), out = {};
  try { ss.getSheetByName('Images').getDataRange().getValues().slice(1).forEach(function (r) { if (r[0] !== '') im[r[0]] = String(r[1]); }); } catch (e) {}
  Object.keys(items).forEach(function (id) { var k = items[id][5]; if (k && im[k]) out[id] = im[k]; });
  return out;
}
function txFmt_(iso) {
  if (!iso) return '';
  var d = new Date(iso);
  return isNaN(d) ? String(iso) : Utilities.formatDate(d, TZ_, 'dd MMM yyyy, HH:mm');
}
function dmPdfPhotos_(g) {
  var cells = [], n = 0;
  g.lines.forEach(function (l) { (l.ph || []).forEach(function (id, k) {
    if (n >= 12) return;
    try { var b = DriveApp.getFileById(id).getBlob(); n++; cells.push('<div style="display:inline-block;width:31%;margin:0 1% 10px;vertical-align:top;text-align:center;break-inside:avoid"><img src="data:' + (b.getContentType() || 'image/jpeg') + ';base64,' + Utilities.base64Encode(b.getBytes()) + '" style="width:100%;height:150px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:6px;border:1px solid #D5D5D5"><div class="s">' + esc_(l.item) + ' · photo ' + (k + 1) + '</div></div>'); } catch (e) {}
  }); });
  return cells.length ? '<div style="font-weight:bold;margin:16px 0 8px">Photos of the damaged items</div><div>' + cells.join('') + '</div>' : '';
}
function makeTxPdf(site, pin, name, ref) {
  try {
    var ss = ss_();
    ensure_(ss);
    site = String(site || '').trim(); ref = String(ref || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    var office = isAll_(site);
    if (office ? !okOffice_(ss, pin) : !checkPin_(ss, site, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
    var kind = TX_KIND_[ref.slice(0, 2)];
    if (!kind) return { ok: false, error: 'Unknown reference ' + ref };
    var g = null;
    if (kind === 'transfer') g = trList_(ss).filter(function (t) { return t.id === ref; })[0];
    else g = mvList_(ss, kind).filter(function (x) { return x.ref === ref; })[0];
    if (!g) return { ok: false, error: ref + ' was not found' };
    if (!office) {
      var mine = kind === 'transfer' ? (g.from === site || g.to === site) : g.site === site;
      if (!mine) return { ok: false, error: ref + ' is not an entry of your site' };
    }
    var pics = txImgs_(ss), T = TX_TITLE_[kind], col = T[1], tr = kind === 'transfer';
    var status = tr ? g.status : g.status, cancelled = /^Cancelled/.test(status);
    var info = [];
    info.push(['Reference', '<b>' + esc_(ref) + '</b>']);
    if (tr) {
      info.push(['From site', esc_(g.from)], ['To site', esc_(g.to)], ['Sent', esc_(txFmt_(g.sent)) + ' · by ' + esc_(g.by)]);
      if (g.rec) info.push(['Received', esc_(txFmt_(g.rec)) + ' · by ' + esc_(g.recBy)]);
      if (g.note) info.push(['Note', esc_(g.note)]);
    } else {
      var m = MV_[kind];
      info.push(['Site', esc_(g.site)], ['Date', esc_(txFmt_(g.time) || g.date)], ['Recorded by', esc_(g.by)]);
      if (g.info1) info.push([esc_(m.i1), esc_(g.info1)]);
      if (g.info2) info.push([esc_(m.i2), esc_(g.info2)]);
    }
    info.push(['Status', '<b style="color:' + (cancelled ? '#B8274A' : /differences|In transit/.test(status) ? '#B26A00' : '#1B7A3A') + '">' + esc_(status) + '</b>' + (g.cancelBy ? ' · ' + esc_(g.cancelBy) : '')]);
    var head = tr ? ['#', '', 'Item', 'Unit', 'Sent', 'Received', 'Difference', 'Line status'] : ['#', '', 'Item', 'Unit', 'Qty', 'Stock before', 'Stock after'];
    var tot = 0, rows = g.lines.map(function (l, i) {
      tot += Number(l.qty) || 0;
      var img = pics[String(l.itemId)] ? '<img src="' + pics[String(l.itemId)] + '" style="width:34px;height:34px;object-fit:contain">' : '';
      var c = ['<td class="c">' + (i + 1) + '</td>', '<td class="c">' + img + '</td>', '<td><b>' + esc_(l.item) + '</b><div class="s">Item no. ' + esc_(l.itemId) + '</div></td>', '<td class="c">' + esc_(l.unit) + '</td>'];
      if (tr) {
        var diff = l.recQty === '' ? '' : r3_(l.recQty - l.qty);
        c.push('<td class="n"><b>' + esc_(l.qty) + '</b></td>', '<td class="n">' + (l.recQty === '' ? '–' : esc_(l.recQty)) + '</td>',
               '<td class="n" style="color:' + (diff < 0 ? '#B8274A' : '#1B1B1B') + '">' + (diff === '' || diff === 0 ? (diff === 0 ? '0' : '–') : (diff > 0 ? '+' : '') + diff) + '</td>',
               '<td>' + esc_(l.status) + (l.shortage ? '<div class="s">Shortage: ' + esc_(l.shortage) + (l.shortBy ? ' · ' + esc_(l.shortBy) : '') + '</div>' : '') + '</td>');
      } else {
        c.push('<td class="n"><b>' + (MV_[kind].sign > 0 ? '+' : '−') + esc_(l.qty) + '</b></td>', '<td class="n">' + (l.before === '' ? '0' : esc_(l.before)) + '</td>', '<td class="n">' + esc_(l.after) + '</td>');
      }
      return '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '>' + c.join('') + '</tr>';
    }).join('');
    var sign = tr ? ['Sent by (' + esc_(g.from) + ')', 'Driver / vehicle', 'Received by (' + esc_(g.to) + ')']
                  : kind === 'received' ? ['Received by (storekeeper)', 'Delivered by (supplier)', 'Checked by'] : ['Storekeeper', 'Site engineer / foreman', 'Approved by'];
    var html = '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><style>@page{size:A4;margin:14mm}*{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}' +
      'body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:10pt;color:#1E2329}.hd td{vertical-align:middle;border:0;padding:0 8px 0 0}' +
      '.band{background:' + col + ';color:#fff;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:6px;margin:12px 0 10px;font-size:14pt;font-weight:bold;display:flex;justify-content:space-between}' +
      'table.inf{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-bottom:12px}table.inf td{border:1px solid #D5D5D5;padding:6px 8px}table.inf td.k{background:#F4F3EF;width:28%;color:#555;font-weight:bold}' +
      'table.t{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}thead{display:table-header-group}tr{break-inside:avoid;page-break-inside:avoid}.t th{background:#1E2329;color:#fff;padding:6px;text-align:left;font-size:9pt}' +
      '.t td{border:1px solid #D5D5D5;padding:5px 6px;vertical-align:middle}.t tr.z td{background:#F7F6F2}.t td.c{text-align:center}.t td.n{text-align:right;white-space:nowrap}.s{color:#777;font-size:8pt}' +
      '.sig{display:flex;gap:18px;margin-top:34px}.sig div{flex:1;border-top:1px solid #1E2329;padding-top:5px;font-size:9pt;color:#555}.x{border:3px solid #B8274A;color:#B8274A;font-weight:bold;font-size:16pt;text-align:center;padding:6px;margin:8px 0;letter-spacing:2px}' +
      '</style></head><body>' +
      '<table class="hd"><tr><td style="width:62px"><img src="' + LOGO_ + '" style="width:54px;height:54px"></td><td><div style="font-size:17pt;font-weight:bold">INVENTORY REPORT</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:10pt;font-weight:bold;color:#C42D4E">WALK THROUGH GENERAL CONTRACTING - SOLE PROPRIETORSHIP LLC</div></td></tr></table>' +
      '<div class="band"><span>' + T[0] + '</span><span>' + esc_(ref) + '</span></div>' + (cancelled ? '<div class="x">' + esc_(status.toUpperCase()) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<table class="inf">' + info.map(function (r) { return '<tr><td class="k">' + r[0] + '</td><td>' + r[1] + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>' +
      '<table class="t"><thead><tr>' + head.map(function (h) { return '<th>' + h + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' + rows +
      '<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:right;font-weight:bold">' + g.lines.length + ' item' + (g.lines.length == 1 ? '' : 's') + ' · total qty</td><td class="n"><b>' + r3_(tot) + '</b></td><td colspan="' + (head.length - 5) + '"></td></tr></tbody></table>' +
      (kind === 'damaged' ? dmPdfPhotos_(g) : '') +
      '<div class="sig">' + sign.map(function (s) { return '<div>' + s + '<br><br>Name / signature / date</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<p style="font-size:8pt;color:#777;margin-top:18px">Printed ' + esc_(Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm')) + ' by ' + esc_(office ? 'Office' : String(name || '').trim()) + ' from the WTC Inventory Report.</p></body></html>';
    var pdf = Utilities.newBlob(html, 'text/html', ref + '.html').getAs('application/pdf').setName(ref + ' ' + (tr ? g.from + ' to ' + g.to : g.site) + '.pdf'), url = '';
    if (office) {
      try {
        var it2 = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Stock PDFs'), folder = it2.hasNext() ? it2.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Stock PDFs');
        url = folder.createFile(pdf).getUrl();
      } catch (e2) { console.error('PDF not saved to Drive: ' + e2); }
    }
    return { ok: true, name: pdf.getName(), url: url, b64: Utilities.base64Encode(pdf.getBytes()), count: g.lines.length };
  } catch (e) {
    var msg = String(e && e.message || e);
    return { ok: false, error: /authoriz|permission/i.test(msg) ? 'The office must allow PDFs first (WTC Stock > Make a test PDF).' : 'Could not create the PDF: ' + msg };
  }
}

/* ======================= Activity report by dates: Received, Used (issued), Damaged and Transfers in one PDF =======================
 * Storekeeper: own site. Office (ALL SITES PIN): one site, or all sites (onlySite = '').
 */
function makeActivityPdf(site, pin, name, from, to, onlySite) {
  try {
    var ss = ss_();
    ensure_(ss);
    site = String(site || '').trim();
    var office = isAll_(site);
    if (office ? !okOffice_(ss, pin) : !checkPin_(ss, site, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
    if (!office && name && !checkPin_(ss, site, pin, name)) return { ok: false, error: 'This PIN is not for ' + String(name).trim() + '. Type your own PIN.' };
    var t = today_(), ok = function (d) { return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(d || '')); };
    from = ok(from) ? String(from) : t.slice(0, 8) + '01'; to = ok(to) ? String(to) : t;
    if (from > to) { var x = from; from = to; to = x; }
    var one = office ? String(onlySite || '').trim() : site, all = office && !one;
    var inSite = function (s) { return all || s === one; };
    var live = function (s) { return String(s || '').indexOf('Cancelled') !== 0; };
    var MN = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
    var f = function (d) { return d ? +d.slice(8, 10) + ' ' + MN[+d.slice(5, 7) - 1] + ' ' + d.slice(0, 4) : ''; };
    var n3 = function (v) { return String(r3_(Number(v) || 0)); };
    var sum = {}, units = {}, add = function (id, item, unit, k, q) { id = String(id); if (!sum[id]) { sum[id] = { item: item, rc: 0, us: 0, dm: 0, ti: 0, to: 0 }; units[id] = unit; } sum[id][k] += Number(q) || 0; };
    var mv = {}, cnt = {};
    ['received', 'used', 'damaged'].forEach(function (k) {
      mv[k] = mvList_(ss, k).filter(function (g) { return live(g.status) && inSite(g.site) && g.date >= from && g.date <= to; });
      mv[k].sort(function (a, b) { return a.time < b.time ? -1 : 1; });
      mv[k].forEach(function (g) { g.lines.forEach(function (l) { add(l.itemId, l.item, l.unit, k === 'received' ? 'rc' : k === 'used' ? 'us' : 'dm', l.qty); }); });
    });
    var trs = trList_(ss).filter(function (x) { return !x.cancelled && x.date >= from && x.date <= to && (all || x.from === one || x.to === one); });
    trs.forEach(function (x) { x.lines.forEach(function (l) {
      if (!live(l.status)) return;
      if (all || x.from === one) add(l.itemId, l.item, l.unit, 'to', l.qty);
      if ((all || x.to === one) && l.recQty !== '') add(l.itemId, l.item, l.unit, 'ti', l.recQty);
    }); });
    var nLines = function (L) { return L.reduce(function (s, g) { return s + g.lines.length; }, 0); };
    var pics = txImgs_(ss), img = function (id) { return pics[String(id)] ? '<img src="' + pics[String(id)] + '" style="width:22px;height:22px;object-fit:contain;vertical-align:middle">' : ''; };
    var COL = { received: '#1B7A3A', used: '#B26A00', damaged: '#C42D4E', transfer: '#1F5FA8', sum: '#1E2329' };
    var sec = function (key, title, head, rows, empty) {
      return '<h2 style="border-left:8px solid ' + COL[key] + ';color:' + COL[key] + '">' + title + '</h2>' + (rows.length
        ? '<table class="t"><thead><tr>' + head.map(function (h) { return '<th style="background:' + COL[key] + '">' + h + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' + rows.join('') + '</tbody></table>'
        : '<p class="none">' + empty + '</p>');
    };
    var mvRows = function (k) {
      var m = MV_[k], out = [], i = 0;
      mv[k].forEach(function (g) { g.lines.forEach(function (l, j) {
        out.push('<tr' + (i++ % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '><td class="nw">' + (j ? '' : f(g.date)) + '</td><td class="nw">' + (j ? '' : '<b>' + esc_(g.ref) + '</b>') + '</td>' + (all ? '<td>' + (j ? '' : esc_(g.site)) + '</td>' : '') +
          '<td>' + img(l.itemId) + ' ' + esc_(l.item) + '</td><td class="n"><b>' + n3(l.qty) + '</b></td><td>' + esc_(l.unit) + '</td><td>' + (j ? '' : esc_(g.info1)) + '</td><td>' + (j ? '' : esc_(g.info2)) + '</td><td>' + (j ? '' : esc_(g.by)) + '</td></tr>');
      }); });
      return out;
    };
    var trRows = [], ti = 0;
    trs.forEach(function (x) { x.lines.forEach(function (l, j) {
      var dir = all ? esc_(x.from) + ' → ' + esc_(x.to) : x.from === one ? 'OUT → ' + esc_(x.to) : 'IN ← ' + esc_(x.from);
      var diff = l.recQty === '' ? '' : r3_(l.recQty - l.qty);
      trRows.push('<tr' + (ti++ % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '><td class="nw">' + (j ? '' : f(x.date)) + '</td><td class="nw">' + (j ? '' : '<b>' + esc_(x.id) + '</b>') + '</td><td class="nw">' + (j ? '' : dir) + '</td>' +
        '<td>' + img(l.itemId) + ' ' + esc_(l.item) + '</td><td class="n"><b>' + n3(l.qty) + '</b></td><td class="n">' + (l.recQty === '' ? '–' : n3(l.recQty)) + '</td><td class="n"' + (diff < 0 ? ' style="color:#B8274A"' : '') + '>' + (diff === '' ? '–' : (diff > 0 ? '+' : '') + diff) + '</td><td>' + esc_(l.unit) + '</td><td>' + esc_(l.status) + '</td><td>' + (j ? '' : esc_(x.by) + (x.recBy ? ' / ' + esc_(x.recBy) : '')) + '</td></tr>');
    }); });
    // stock at the start (end of the day before 'from') and at the end of 'to' (= current qty when 'to' is today)
    var ledg = ledger_(ss), prev = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(Date.parse(from + 'T12:00:00Z') - 864e5), 'UTC', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
    var q0 = stockAsOf_(ledg, prev, all ? null : one), q1 = stockAsOf_(ledg, to, all ? null : one), live2 = sitesList_(ss);
    var qty = function (q, id) { var v = q[id] || {}, n = 0, any = false; Object.keys(v).forEach(function (s2) { if ((all ? live2.indexOf(s2) >= 0 : s2 === one) && v[s2] && v[s2][0] !== '') { n += Number(v[s2][0]) || 0; any = true; } }); return any ? r3_(n) : ''; };
    var order = itemRows_(ss).map(function (r) { return String(r[0]); });
    var ids = Object.keys(sum).sort(function (a, b) { return order.indexOf(a) - order.indexOf(b); });
    var sumRows = ids.map(function (id, i) {
      var v = sum[id], net = r3_(v.rc + v.ti - v.us - v.dm - v.to), c = function (q) { return q ? n3(q) : ''; };
      var o = qty(q0, id), e = qty(q1, id);
      return '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '><td>' + img(id) + ' ' + esc_(v.item) + '</td><td>' + esc_(units[id]) + '</td><td class="n">' + (o === '' ? '0' : o) + '</td><td class="n" style="color:#1B7A3A">' + c(v.rc) + '</td><td class="n" style="color:#1F5FA8">' + c(v.ti) +
        '</td><td class="n" style="color:#B26A00">' + c(v.us) + '</td><td class="n" style="color:#C42D4E">' + c(v.dm) + '</td><td class="n" style="color:#1F5FA8">' + c(v.to) + '</td><td class="n">' + (net > 0 ? '+' : '') + net + '</td><td class="n" style="background:#E3F2E7"><b>' + (e === '' ? '0' : e) + '</b></td></tr>';
    });
    var tiles = [['Received', mv.received.length, nLines(mv.received), COL.received], ['Used / issued', mv.used.length, nLines(mv.used), COL.used], ['Damaged', mv.damaged.length, nLines(mv.damaged), COL.damaged], ['Transfers', trs.length, trs.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + x.lines.length; }, 0), COL.transfer]];
    var who = all ? 'All sites' : one;
    var html = '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><style>@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:11mm}*{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:8.5pt;color:#1E2329}' +
      'table.hd td{border:0;padding:0 8px 0 0;vertical-align:middle}.tiles{display:flex;gap:10px;margin:10px 0 4px}.tiles div{flex:1;border-radius:8px;color:#fff;padding:8px 10px;font-size:9pt}.tiles b{display:block;font-size:17pt}' +
      'h2{font-size:12pt;margin:16px 0 6px;padding-left:8px;break-after:avoid;page-break-after:avoid}table.t{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}thead{display:table-header-group}tr{break-inside:avoid;page-break-inside:avoid}' +
      '.t th{color:#fff;padding:5px;text-align:left;font-size:8pt}.t td{border:1px solid #D5D5D5;padding:3px 5px;vertical-align:middle}.t tr.z td{background:#F6F5F1}.t td.n{text-align:right;white-space:nowrap}.t td.nw{white-space:nowrap}.none{color:#777;font-style:italic;margin:2px 0 0 10px}' +
      '</style></head><body><table class="hd"><tr><td style="width:60px"><img src="' + LOGO_ + '" style="width:50px;height:50px"></td><td><div style="font-size:16pt;font-weight:bold">SITE ACTIVITY REPORT</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:9.5pt;font-weight:bold;color:#C42D4E">WALK THROUGH GENERAL CONTRACTING - SOLE PROPRIETORSHIP LLC</div><div style="color:#555;font-size:10pt"><b>' + esc_(who) + '</b> &middot; ' + f(from) + ' to ' + f(to) + ' &middot; Printed ' +
      esc_(Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm')) + ' by ' + esc_(office ? 'Office' : String(name || '').trim()) + '</div></td></tr></table>' +
      '<div class="tiles">' + tiles.map(function (x) { return '<div style="background:' + x[3] + '"><b>' + x[1] + '</b>' + x[0] + ' · ' + x[2] + ' line' + (x[2] == 1 ? '' : 's') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      sec('sum', 'SUMMARY BY ITEM', ['Item', 'Unit', 'Stock on ' + f(prev), 'Received', 'Transfer in', 'Used', 'Damaged', 'Transfer out', 'Net change', (to === t ? 'Current qty' : 'Stock on ' + f(to))], sumRows, 'No movements in these dates.') +
      sec('received', 'RECEIVED FROM SUPPLIER', ['Date', 'Ref'].concat(all ? ['Site'] : []).concat(['Item', 'Qty', 'Unit', 'Supplier', 'DN / LPO no.', 'By']), mvRows('received'), 'Nothing received.') +
      sec('used', 'USED / ISSUED', ['Date', 'Ref'].concat(all ? ['Site'] : []).concat(['Item', 'Qty', 'Unit', 'Used for (job / area)', 'Note', 'By']), mvRows('used'), 'Nothing used.') +
      sec('damaged', 'DAMAGED', ['Date', 'Ref'].concat(all ? ['Site'] : []).concat(['Item', 'Qty', 'Unit', 'Reason', 'Note', 'By']), mvRows('damaged'), 'Nothing damaged.') +
      sec('transfer', 'TRANSFERS', ['Date', 'Ref', all ? 'From → To' : 'Direction', 'Item', 'Sent', 'Received', 'Difference', 'Unit', 'Status', 'Sent by / received by'], trRows, 'No transfers.') +
      '<p style="font-size:7.5pt;color:#777;margin-top:14px">Entries cancelled by the office are not included. Transfers are listed by the day they were sent; Transfer in counts the quantity received. Net change = received + transfer in − used − damaged − transfer out. ' + (to === t ? 'Current qty' : 'Stock on ' + f(to)) + ' is the stock recorded by the site' + (all ? 's (all sites added together)' : '') + ' at the end of that day; it also includes corrections made in Stock today, so it can differ from the first column + net change.</p></body></html>';
    var nm = 'Activity ' + who + ' ' + from + ' to ' + to + '.pdf';
    var pdf = Utilities.newBlob(html, 'text/html', 'activity.html').getAs('application/pdf').setName(nm), url = '';
    if (office) {
      try {
        var it2 = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Stock PDFs'), folder = it2.hasNext() ? it2.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Stock PDFs');
        url = folder.createFile(pdf).getUrl();
      } catch (e2) { console.error('PDF not saved to Drive: ' + e2); }
    }
    return { ok: true, name: nm, url: url, b64: Utilities.base64Encode(pdf.getBytes()), count: ids.length, entries: tiles.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + x[1]; }, 0) };
  } catch (e) {
    var msg = String(e && e.message || e);
    return { ok: false, error: /authoriz|permission/i.test(msg) ? 'The office must allow PDFs first (WTC Stock > Make a test PDF).' : 'Could not create the PDF: ' + msg };
  }
}

/** Builds the PDF (landscape Google Doc, or the backup layout), saves it to Drive, returns it to the page. */
function pdfOut_(d) {
  try {
    var pdf, how = 'doc';
    try { pdf = pdfFromDoc_(d); }
    catch (e1) {
      console.error('Landscape PDF via Google Doc failed: ' + e1);
      try { PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('LAST_PDF_DOC_ERROR', new Date().toISOString() + ' ' + e1); } catch (x) {}
      if (/authoriz|permission/i.test(String(e1))) throw e1;
      pdf = pdfFromHtml_(d); how = 'html';
    }
    var url = '';
    try {
      var it2 = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Stock PDFs'), folder = it2.hasNext() ? it2.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Stock PDFs');
      url = folder.createFile(pdf).getUrl();
    } catch (e2) { console.error('PDF not saved to Drive: ' + e2); }
    return { ok: true, name: d.name, url: url, b64: Utilities.base64Encode(pdf.getBytes()), count: d.rows.length, sites: d.sites, how: how };
  } catch (e) {
    var m = String(e && e.message || e);
    return { ok: false, error: /authoriz|permission/i.test(m)
      ? 'Google needs your permission first: open the Google Sheet and click WTC Stock > Make a test PDF, then allow access. (' + m + ')'
      : 'Could not create the PDF: ' + m };
  }
}

/** Sheet menu: makes a small test PDF (first 5 items). Running it from the sheet also lets Google ask for permission. */
function testPdf() {
  var ss = ss_(), ui = null;
  try { ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); } catch (e) {}
  ensure_(ss);
  var ids = itemRows_(ss).slice(0, 5).map(function (r) { return String(r[0]); });
  var pin = (siteRows_(ss).filter(function (r) { return isAll_(r[0]); })[0] || [])[1];
  var r = makeStockPdf(String(pin || ''), '', ids, '');
  var why = r.ok && r.how !== 'doc' ? ' Reason the normal layout was not used: ' + (PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('LAST_PDF_DOC_ERROR') || 'unknown') + '.' : '';
  var msg = r.ok ? 'Test PDF made ✓ (' + (r.how === 'doc' ? 'landscape' : 'backup layout') + '). It is in your Google Drive folder "WTC Stock PDFs": ' + r.url + why
                 : r.error + (pin ? '' : ' (Add an ALL SITES row with a PIN on the Sites tab first.)');
  if (ui) ui.alert(msg);
  return msg;
}

/** Highlight colours of the TOTAL, IDLE and REQUIRED columns: [cell background, text, heading background, heading text]. */
var PDF_COLS_ = { 'TOTAL': ['#E3F2E7', '#14592B', '#1B7A3A', '#FFFFFF'], 'IDLE': ['#FFF0DB', '#8F5400', '#B26A00', '#FFFFFF'], 'REQUIRED': ['#E6EFFC', '#1A4F96', '#1F5FA8', '#FFFFFF'] };
/** PDF table columns: sites, then In transit (if any), TOTAL, and IN USE + IDLE when any listed item is idle. */
function pdfHead_(d) {
  return ['#', '', 'Item', 'Category', 'Unit'].concat(d.sites).concat(d.showTransit ? ['In transit'] : [])
    .concat(['TOTAL']).concat(d.showIdle || d.showMach ? ['IN USE'] : []).concat(d.showIdle ? ['IDLE'] : [])
    .concat(d.showMach ? ['MACHINE STATUS'] : []).concat(d.showReq ? ['REQUIRED', 'BALANCE'] : []);
}
/** BALANCE = IDLE − REQUIRED (only idle, unused stock can be given): '−2 short', '+3 extra' or 'OK'. TOTAL is for information only. */
function balTxt_(r) {
  if (r.req === '') return '';
  var b = r3_((r.idle === '' ? 0 : Number(r.idle)) - r.req);
  return b < 0 ? '\u2212' + r3_(-b) + ' short' : b > 0 ? '+' + b + ' extra' : 'OK';
}
function pdfRow_(d, r, i) {
  var sv = r.vals.map(function (v, k) {
    var x = r.idles ? r.idles[k] : '', mm = r.machs ? r.machs[k] : '';
    var br = [x !== '' ? x + ' idle' : '', mm].filter(String).join(', ');
    return String(v) + (v !== '' && br ? ' (' + br + ')' : '');
  });
  return [String(i + 1), '', r.name, r.cat, r.unit].concat(sv).concat(d.showTransit ? [String(r.transit)] : [])
    .concat([String(r.total)]).concat(d.showIdle || d.showMach ? [r.total ? String(r.inUse) : ''] : []).concat(d.showIdle ? [String(r.idle === '' && r.req !== '' ? 0 : r.idle)] : [])
    .concat(d.showMach ? [r.status] : []).concat(d.showReq ? [String(r.req), balTxt_(r)] : []);
}


/**
 * Column widths for the landscape PDF, always fitting the page width (points).
 * Many sites: the other columns get narrower first, then the site columns; site names get a smaller font
 * so they fit in at most two lines and stay centred and lined up.
 */
function pdfLayout_(d, head, inner) {
  var n = d.sites.length, sum = function (a) { return a.reduce(function (x, y) { return x + y; }, 0); };
  var TW = { 'In transit': 50, 'TOTAL': 46, 'IN USE': 46, 'IDLE': 42, 'MACHINE STATUS': 100, 'REQUIRED': 58, 'BALANCE': 66 };
  var TMIN = { 'In transit': 44, 'TOTAL': 34, 'IN USE': 38, 'IDLE': 34, 'MACHINE STATUS': 70, 'REQUIRED': 48, 'BALANCE': 50 };
  var tailCols = head.slice(5 + n), tw = tailCols.map(function (h) { return TW[h] || 50; });
  var words = function (x) { return String(x).split(/\s+/).reduce(function (a, w) { return Math.max(a, w.length); }, 1); };
  // a site column must hold its longest word at 7 pt without breaking it, and should be comfortable when there is room
  var needWord = n ? Math.max.apply(null, d.sites.map(function (x) { return Math.ceil(words(x) * 7 * 0.76 + 10); })) : 0;   // bold capitals
  var ideal = Math.max(needWord, d.showMach ? 78 : d.showIdle ? 60 : 50), floor = Math.max(30, needWord);
  var numW = Math.ceil(String(d.rows.length).length * 9 * 0.6 + 14);   // the # column fits the biggest serial number on one line
  // Category and Unit always wide enough for their longest word (no 'EQUIPM-ENT' or 'NO-S'); sites share what is left
  var catW = Math.min(90, Math.ceil(d.rows.reduce(function (a, r) { return Math.max(a, words(r.cat)); }, 1) * 9 * 0.65 + 14));
  var unitW = Math.min(60, Math.max(40, Math.ceil(d.rows.reduce(function (a, r) { return Math.max(a, words(r.unit)); }, 3) * 9 * 0.7 + 14)));
  var steps = [[numW, 34, 120, Math.max(72, catW), Math.max(38, unitW)], [numW, 26, 100, Math.max(50, catW), unitW], [numW, 22, 90, catW, unitW]], fx = steps[0], headFs = 9;
  var room = function () { return inner - sum(tw) - sum(fx); };
  if (n && room() < n * ideal) { fx = steps[1]; headFs = 8; }
  if (n && room() < n * floor) {                                       // narrow the end columns, never below their own label
    var over = n * floor - room(), free = sum(tw.map(function (w, k) { return w - (TMIN[tailCols[k]] || 40); }));
    var k = free > 0 ? Math.min(1, over / free) : 0;
    tw = tw.map(function (w, x) { var m = TMIN[tailCols[x]] || 40; return Math.round(w - (w - m) * k); });
  }
  if (n && room() < n * floor) { fx = steps[2]; headFs = 7.5; }
  var siteW = n ? Math.max(18, Math.min(100, Math.floor(room() / n))) : 0;
  var spare = room() - siteW * n;
  if (spare > 0) fx = fx.slice(0, 2).concat([fx[2] + Math.min(spare, 300)]).concat(fx.slice(3));   // spare width goes to the item name
  var w = fx.concat(d.sites.map(function () { return siteW; })).concat(tw);
  // one font size for all site names: from 9 pt down to 6.5 pt until every word fits and each name fits in 3 (narrow: 4) lines
  var cw = Math.max(10, siteW - 8), lines = siteW < 50 ? 4 : 3, one = 6.5;
  for (var f = 9; f >= 6.5; f -= 0.5) {
    var per = cw / (f * 0.76);
    if (d.sites.every(function (x) { return words(x) <= per && String(x).length <= per * lines; })) { one = f; break; }
  }
  var cellFs = siteW >= 46 ? 9 : siteW >= 34 ? 8 : 7;
  return { w: w, siteW: siteW, siteFs: d.sites.map(function () { return one; }), headFs: headFs, cellFs: cellFs };
}


/**
 * Splits the PDF rows into pages (row numbers per page) so each page can start with the column header row.
 * Row heights are estimated from the text length and column widths, on the safe side.
 */
function pdfPages_(d, head, all, L, pageH, firstRes, nextRes) {
  var n = d.sites.length;
  var lines = function (t, w, fs) {
    t = String(t == null ? '' : t); if (!t) return 1;
    var per = Math.max(3, Math.floor((w - 10) / (fs * 0.62))), count = 0;   // character width measured on a real PDF
    t.split(/\n/).forEach(function (part) { var len = 0, ln = 1; part.split(/\s+/).forEach(function (wd) { var x = wd.length + (len ? 1 : 0); if (len + x > per && len) { ln++; len = wd.length; } else len += x; while (len > per) { ln++; len -= per; } }); count += ln; });
    return count;
  };
  var hH = 0;
  head.forEach(function (h, k) { var fs = k >= 5 && k < 5 + n ? L.siteFs[k - 5] : L.headFs; hH = Math.max(hH, lines(h, L.w[k], fs) * fs * 1.17); });
  hH += 10;
  var rowH = function (v, i) {
    var h = 0;
    v.forEach(function (t, k) { var fs = k >= 5 && k < 5 + n ? L.cellFs : 9; h = Math.max(h, lines(t, L.w[k], fs) * fs * 1.17); });
    h += 9;                                                             // line height and padding measured on a real PDF
    if (d.rows[i].img) h = Math.max(h, 24);                           // a row with a picture
    return h;
  };
  var first = (pageH - (firstRes == null ? 92 : firstRes)) * 0.97, next = (pageH - (nextRes == null ? 6 : nextRes)) * 0.97, pages = [], cur = [], used = hH, cap = first;
  all.forEach(function (v, i) {
    var h = rowH(v, i);
    if (cur.length && used + h > cap) { pages.push(cur); cur = []; used = hH; cap = next; }
    cur.push(i); used += h;
  });
  if (cur.length) pages.push(cur);
  return pages;
}

/** Landscape A4 stock list built as a temporary Google Doc, returned as a PDF blob. */
function pdfFromDoc_(d) {
  var pdf;

  var doc = DocumentApp.create('WTC stock list (temporary)'), body = doc.getBody();
  try {
    var W = 842, H = 595, M = 28, inner = W - 2 * M;                    // A4 landscape, in points
    body.setPageWidth(W).setPageHeight(H).setMarginTop(M).setMarginBottom(M).setMarginLeft(M).setMarginRight(M);
    var base = {};
    base[DocumentApp.Attribute.FONT_FAMILY] = 'Arial';
    base[DocumentApp.Attribute.FONT_SIZE] = 9;
    base[DocumentApp.Attribute.FOREGROUND_COLOR] = '#1E2329';

    // Everything that must be on EVERY page (logo, title, column headings) goes in the document's page header,
    // which Google Docs repeats on every page by itself. The body holds only the item rows.
    var head = pdfHead_(d), nCols = head.length, firstNum = 5;
    var L = pdfLayout_(d, head, inner);                                // column widths that always fit the page
    var hdr = doc.addHeader();
    var tiny = function (par) { try { par.setSpacingBefore(0).setSpacingAfter(0).setLineSpacing(1); par.editAsText().setFontSize(2); } catch (e) {} return par; };
    tiny(hdr.getChild(0).asParagraph());
    var hd = hdr.appendTable([['', '']]);
    hd.setBorderWidth(0);
    hd.setColumnWidth(0, 52);
    var logo = Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(LOGO_.split(',')[1]), 'image/jpeg', 'logo.jpg');
    hd.getCell(0, 0).getChild(0).asParagraph().appendInlineImage(logo).setWidth(42).setHeight(42);
    var hc = hd.getCell(0, 1);
    hc.getChild(0).asParagraph().setText('STOCK LIST').editAsText().setFontFamily('Arial').setFontSize(16).setBold(true).setForegroundColor('#1E2329');
    hc.appendParagraph('WALK THROUGH GENERAL CONTRACTING - SOLE PROPRIETORSHIP LLC').editAsText()
      .setFontFamily('Arial').setFontSize(9).setBold(true).setForegroundColor('#C42D4E');
    hc.appendParagraph(d.when + '   ·   Sites: ' + (d.oneSite || 'all sites with stock (' + d.sites.length + ' of ' + d.allSites + ')') +
      '   ·   Items: ' + d.rows.length).editAsText().setFontFamily('Arial').setFontSize(8.5).setBold(false).setForegroundColor('#555555');
    for (var hp = 0; hp < hc.getNumChildren(); hp++) try { hc.getChild(hp).asParagraph().setSpacingBefore(0).setSpacingAfter(0); } catch (e) {}
    tiny(hdr.appendParagraph(''));
    var ht = hdr.appendTable([head]);                                   // the column headings
    ht.setAttributes(base);
    ht.setBorderColor('#D5D5D5');
    L.w.forEach(function (w, i) { ht.setColumnWidth(i, w); });
    var hrow = ht.getRow(0);
    for (var k0 = 0; k0 < nCols; k0++) {
      var c0 = hrow.getCell(k0), site0 = k0 >= firstNum && k0 < firstNum + d.sites.length, pad0 = (site0 && L.siteW < 46) || k0 === 0 ? 2 : 4;
      c0.setPaddingTop(4).setPaddingBottom(4).setPaddingLeft(pad0).setPaddingRight(pad0);
      c0.setVerticalAlignment(DocumentApp.VerticalAlignment.CENTER).setBackgroundColor(PDF_COLS_[head[k0]] ? PDF_COLS_[head[k0]][2] : '#1E2329');
      var p0 = c0.getChild(0).asParagraph();
      if (k0 === 0 || k0 >= firstNum) p0.setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
      c0.editAsText().setFontFamily('Arial').setForegroundColor('#FFFFFF').setBold(true).setFontSize(site0 ? L.siteFs[k0 - firstNum] : L.headFs);
    }
    // trailing paragraph Docs keeps after the last table in the header: make it tiny
    try { tiny(hdr.getChild(hdr.getNumChildren() - 1).asParagraph()); } catch (e) {}

    // Item rows (body)
    var all = d.rows.map(function (r, i) { return pdfRow_(d, r, i); });
    var tb = body.appendTable(all);
    tb.setAttributes(base);
    tb.setBorderColor('#D5D5D5');
    L.w.forEach(function (w, i) { tb.setColumnWidth(i, w); });
    for (var r = 0; r < all.length; r++) {
      var row = tb.getRow(r);
      for (var k = 0; k < nCols; k++) {
        var cell = row.getCell(k);
        var isSite = k >= firstNum && k < firstNum + d.sites.length, pad = (isSite && L.siteW < 46) || k === 0 ? 2 : 4;
        cell.setPaddingTop(3).setPaddingBottom(3).setPaddingLeft(pad).setPaddingRight(pad);
        cell.setVerticalAlignment(DocumentApp.VerticalAlignment.CENTER);
        var p = cell.getChild(0).asParagraph();
        if (k === 0 || k >= firstNum) p.setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
        if (r % 2 === 1) cell.setBackgroundColor('#F4F3EF');
        if (k === 2 || head[k] === 'TOTAL') cell.editAsText().setBold(true);
        var tx = cell.editAsText(), str = tx.getText(), at = str.indexOf(' (');
        if (isSite && L.cellFs < 9 && str !== '') tx.setFontSize(L.cellFs);
        if (isSite && at > 0) tx.setForegroundColor(at, str.length - 1, /workshop|maint/.test(str) ? '#B8274A' : '#B26A00').setFontSize(at, str.length - 1, Math.min(8, L.cellFs - 1));
        if (head[k] === 'MACHINE STATUS' && str !== '') { tx.setForegroundColor('#B8274A').setBold(true).setFontSize(8); cell.setBackgroundColor('#F9D6DE'); }
        if (PDF_COLS_[head[k]]) { cell.setBackgroundColor(PDF_COLS_[head[k]][0]); if (str !== '') tx.setForegroundColor(PDF_COLS_[head[k]][1]).setBold(true); }
        if (head[k] === 'BALANCE' && str !== '') { var sh = /short/.test(str); tx.setForegroundColor(sh ? '#B8274A' : '#1b7a3a').setBold(true); if (sh) cell.setBackgroundColor('#F9D6DE'); }
      }
      var img = d.rows[r].img;
      if (img && img.indexOf('base64,') > 0) {
        try {
          var parts = img.split(','), mime = (parts[0].match(/data:([^;]+)/) || [])[1] || 'image/jpeg';
          row.getCell(1).getChild(0).asParagraph().appendInlineImage(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(parts[1]), mime, 'p'))
            .setWidth(28).setHeight(28);
        } catch (e) {}
      }
    }
    body.appendParagraph('Quantities are the latest recorded by each site’s storekeeper in the WTC Stock Report. ' +
      'Only sites holding the listed items are shown; a blank cell means that site has none.' +
      (d.showTransit ? ' In transit = sent between sites, not yet received.' : '') +
      (d.showIdle ? ' IDLE = not being used at the site (shown in brackets under each site, e.g. 3 (1 idle)).' : '') +
      (d.showMach ? ' MACHINE STATUS = machines at a repair workshop or needing maintenance (still counted in the site’s stock).' : '') +
      (d.showIdle || d.showMach ? ' IN USE = TOTAL − IDLE − not working.' : '') +
      (d.showReq ? ' REQUIRED = quantity needed (typed by the office for this list); BALANCE = IDLE − REQUIRED (only idle, unused stock is counted as available; TOTAL is for information).' : '') + '   Generated ' + d.made)
      .editAsText().setFontFamily('Arial').setFontSize(8).setForegroundColor('#666666');
    var first = body.getChild(0);                                     // remove the empty first paragraph a new Doc starts with
    try { if (first.getType() === DocumentApp.ElementType.PARAGRAPH && first.asParagraph().getText() === '' && body.getNumChildren() > 1) first.removeFromParent(); } catch (e) {}
    doc.saveAndClose();
    pdf = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId()).getAs('application/pdf').setName(d.name);
  } finally {
    try { DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId()).setTrashed(true); } catch (e) {}
  }
  return pdf;
}

/** Backup: the same stock list drawn as a web page and converted to PDF (used only if the Google Doc way fails). */
function pdfFromHtml_(d) {
  var cell = function (t, k, r, bold) {
    var hc = PDF_COLS_[head[k]];
    return '<td style="text-align:' + (k === 0 || k >= 5 ? 'center' : 'left') + ';' + (hc ? 'background:' + hc[0] + ';color:' + hc[1] + ';' : '') + (k === 0 ? 'white-space:nowrap;padding-left:2px;padding-right:2px;' : '') + (bold ? 'font-weight:bold;' : '') + (k >= 5 && k < 5 + ns && L.cellFs < 9 ? 'font-size:' + L.cellFs + 'pt;' : '') + '">' + t + '</td>';
  };
  var head = pdfHead_(d), L = pdfLayout_(d, head, 786), ns = d.sites.length;
  var vals = d.rows.map(function (r, i) { return pdfRow_(d, r, i); });
  var rows = d.rows.map(function (r, i) {
    var v = vals[i].map(function (t, k) {
      if (k === 1) return r.img ? '<img src="' + r.img + '" style="width:28px;height:28px">' : '';
      var e = esc_(t), at = k >= 5 && k < 5 + d.sites.length ? e.indexOf(' (') : -1;
      if (head[k] === 'MACHINE STATUS' && e) return '<span style="color:#B8274A;font-size:8pt;font-weight:bold">' + e + '</span>';
      return at > 0 ? e.slice(0, at) + '<span style="color:' + (/workshop|maint/.test(e) ? '#B8274A' : '#B26A00') + ';font-size:8pt">' + e.slice(at) + '</span>' : e;
    });
    v = v.map(function (t, k) { return head[k] === 'BALANCE' && t ? '<span style="color:' + (/short/.test(t) ? '#B8274A' : '#1b7a3a') + '">' + t + '</span>' : head[k] === 'REQUIRED' && t ? '<span style="color:#1f5fa8">' + t + '</span>' : t; });
    return '<tr class="' + (i % 2 ? 'z' : '') + '">' + v.map(function (t, k) { return cell(t, k, i, k === 2 || head[k] === 'TOTAL' || head[k] === 'IDLE' || head[k] === 'REQUIRED' || head[k] === 'BALANCE'); }).join('') + '</tr>';
  });
  // the same heading (logo, company, date / sites line) and column headings on EVERY page
  var top = '<table class="hd"><tr><td style="width:70px"><img src="' + LOGO_ + '" style="width:52px;height:52px"></td><td>' +
    '<div style="font-size:18pt;font-weight:bold">STOCK LIST</div><div style="font-size:10pt;font-weight:bold;color:#C42D4E">WALK THROUGH GENERAL CONTRACTING - SOLE PROPRIETORSHIP LLC</div>' +
    '<div style="color:#555">' + esc_(d.when) + ' &middot; Sites: ' + esc_(d.oneSite || 'all sites with stock (' + d.sites.length + ' of ' + d.allSites + ')') +
    ' &middot; Items: ' + d.rows.length + '</div></td></tr></table>';
  var thead = '<tr>' + head.map(function (h, k) { var site = k >= 5 && k < 5 + ns, hc = PDF_COLS_[h]; return '<th style="width:' + L.w[k] + 'pt;text-align:' + (k >= 5 || k === 0 ? 'center' : 'left') + ';font-size:' + (site ? L.siteFs[k - 5] : L.headFs) + 'pt' + (hc ? ';background:' + hc[0] + ';color:' + hc[1] + ';border-bottom:3px solid ' + hc[2] : '') + '">' + esc_(h) + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr>';
  // ONE table: its <thead> (company heading + column headings) is repeated by the PDF engine at the top of every page,
  // and rows are never cut between pages - no guessing of page breaks, so no half-empty pages.
  var cols = '<colgroup>' + head.map(function (h, k) { return '<col style="width:' + L.w[k] + 'pt">'; }).join('') + '</colgroup>';   // fixed column widths (the first row spans all columns)
  var body = '<table class="t">' + cols + '<thead><tr><th class="top" colspan="' + head.length + '">' + top + '</th></tr>' + thead + '</thead><tbody>' + rows.join('') + '</tbody></table>';
  var html = '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><style>@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:12mm}' +
    '*{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}thead{display:table-header-group}tr{page-break-inside:avoid;break-inside:avoid}body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:9pt;color:#1E2329}table.hd{margin-bottom:8px}.t th.top{background:none;border:0;border-bottom:0;padding:0 0 8px 0;text-align:left;font-weight:normal}.t table.hd td{border:0;padding:0 6px 0 0;background:none;vertical-align:middle}table.t{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;table-layout:fixed}.t th{vertical-align:middle;overflow-wrap:break-word;line-height:1.15}.t td{overflow-wrap:break-word}' +
    '.t th{background:#E3E6EA;color:#1E2329;font-weight:bold;padding:5px;text-align:center;border:1px solid #9AA3AD;border-bottom:2px solid #1E2329}.t td{border:1px solid #D5D5D5;padding:4px}.t tr.z td{background:#F4F3EF}' +
    '</style></head><body>' + body +
    '<p style="font-size:8pt;color:#666">Quantities are the latest recorded by each site’s storekeeper. Only sites holding the listed items are shown; a blank cell means that site has none.' + (d.showIdle ? ' IDLE = not being used at the site.' : '') + (d.showMach ? ' MACHINE STATUS = at a repair workshop or needing maintenance.' : '') + (d.showIdle || d.showMach ? ' IN USE = TOTAL − IDLE − not working.' : '') + (d.showReq ? ' REQUIRED = quantity needed; BALANCE = IDLE − REQUIRED (only idle stock is counted as available).' : '') + ' Generated ' + esc_(d.made) + '</p></body></html>';
  return Utilities.newBlob(html, 'text/html', 'stock.html').getAs('application/pdf').setName(d.name);
}

/* ======================= Rented machines (hired from rental companies) =======================
 * Kept apart from our stock: they are never added to stock totals, the All Sites Stock tab or the stock PDF.
 * "Rentals" tab: one row per rental (a part-return splits a row: the returned part gets its own row).
 * "Rental Types": the machine list for rentals (grows when a site types a new machine).
 * "Rental Companies": rental companies (grows when a site types a new company).
 * Dates are stored as text yyyy-MM-dd.
 */
var RN_TAB_ = 'Rentals', RT_TAB_ = 'Rental Types', RC_TAB_ = 'Rental Companies', RN_COL_ = '#0E7C86';
var RN_HEAD_ = ['Ref', 'Site', 'Machine', 'Size', 'Our item ID', 'Qty', 'Rental company', 'Plate / fleet no.', 'Operator', 'Rate (AED)', 'Per',
                'On-hire', 'Expected return', 'Returned', 'Hours', 'LPO / DN', 'Off-hire note', 'Photo', 'Added by', 'Added time', 'Returned by', 'Status', 'History'];
var RN_N_ = RN_HEAD_.length;
var RT_START_ = [['Mobile crane', '🏗️'], ['Bobcat', '🚜'], ['Excavator', '🦾'], ['JCB / Backhoe', '🚜'], ['Forklift', '🏋️'], ['Telehandler', '🏗️'],
                 ['Scissor lift', '🪜'], ['Manlift / Boom lift', '🧗'], ['Roller', '🛞'], ['Dumper / Tipper', '🚛'], ['Water tanker', '💧'],
                 ['Generator', '⚡'], ['Air compressor', '💨'], ['Concrete pump', '🏭']];
function rnTab_(ss, name, head, rows, textCols) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  if (sh) return sh;
  try { sh = ss.insertSheet(name); } catch (e) { sh = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (sh) return sh; throw e; }
  (textCols || []).forEach(function (c) { sh.getRange(c + ':' + c).setNumberFormat('@'); });
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setValues([head]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground(RN_COL_).setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
  if (rows && rows.length) sh.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, head.length).setValues(rows);
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  try { sh.setTabColor(RN_COL_); } catch (e) {}
  return sh;
}
function rnSheet_(ss) { return rnTab_(ss, RN_TAB_, RN_HEAD_, null, ['H', 'L', 'M', 'N', 'P', 'Q']); }
function rtSheet_(ss) { return rnTab_(ss, RT_TAB_, ['Machine type', 'Icon', 'Added by'], RT_START_.map(function (r) { return [r[0], r[1], '(starter list)']; })); }
function rcSheet_(ss) { return rnTab_(ss, RC_TAB_, ['Company', 'Phone', 'Added by'], null); }
function rnRows_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.rnRows) return MEMO_.rnRows;
  var sh = rnSheet_(ss), n = sh.getLastRow();
  return (MEMO_.rnRows = n > 1 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, RN_N_).getValues() : []);
}
function rnNum_(x) { x = String(x == null ? '' : x).trim().replace(/,/g, ''); return x === '' ? 0 : Number(x); }
function rnDate_(x) { var d = dstr_(x); return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d) ? d : ''; }
function rnObj_(r, i) {
  return { row: i + 2, ref: String(r[0]), site: String(r[1]).trim(), machine: String(r[2]), size: String(r[3] || ''), itemId: String(r[4] || ''),
    qty: Number(r[5]) || 1, company: String(r[6] || ''), plate: String(r[7] || ''), op: String(r[8] || ''), rate: Number(r[9]) || 0, per: String(r[10] || ''),
    on: rnDate_(r[11]), exp: rnDate_(r[12]), ret: rnDate_(r[13]), hours: Number(r[14]) || 0, lpo: String(r[15] || ''), offNote: String(r[16] || ''),
    photo: String(r[17] || ''), by: String(r[18] || ''), retBy: String(r[20] || ''), status: String(r[21] || '') || (rnDate_(r[13]) ? 'Returned' : 'On hire'),
    hist: String(r[22] || '') };
}
function rnList_(ss) { return rnRows_(ss).map(rnObj_).filter(function (o) { return o.ref && o.status !== 'Cancelled'; }); }
function rnDays_(a, b) { return Math.round((Date.parse(b) - Date.parse(a)) / 864e5); }
function rnAdd_(ds, n) { var d = new Date(Date.parse(ds) + n * 864e5); return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
function rnMonthDays_(ds) { var y = +ds.slice(0, 4), m = +ds.slice(5, 7); return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 0)).getUTCDate(); }
/** Estimated rent between two dates (both included). Day: rate x days x qty. Month: rate / days-in-that-month for each day. Hour: rate x hours (counted on the return day). */
function rnCost_(o, from, to) {
  var end = o.ret || to, a = o.on > from ? o.on : from, b = end < to ? end : to;
  if (!o.on || !o.rate) return 0;
  if (o.per === 'Hour') return o.ret && o.ret >= from && o.ret <= to ? o.rate * o.hours * o.qty : 0;
  if (a > b) return 0;
  if (o.per === 'Month') {
    var s = 0, d = a;
    while (d <= b) {
      var mEnd = d.slice(0, 8) + ('0' + rnMonthDays_(d)).slice(-2), e = mEnd < b ? mEnd : b;
      s += o.rate * (rnDays_(d, e) + 1) / rnMonthDays_(d);
      d = rnAdd_(e, 1);
    }
    return Math.round(s * o.qty);
  }
  return o.rate * (rnDays_(a, b) + 1) * o.qty;
}
/** What the page needs for one rental. */
function rnOut_(o, t) {
  var end = o.ret || t, late = !o.ret && o.exp && o.exp < t ? rnDays_(o.exp, t) : 0;
  return { ref: o.ref, site: o.site, machine: o.machine, size: o.size, itemId: o.itemId, qty: o.qty, company: o.company, plate: o.plate, op: o.op,
    rate: o.rate, per: o.per, on: o.on, exp: o.exp, ret: o.ret, hours: o.hours, lpo: o.lpo, offNote: o.offNote, photo: o.photo, by: o.by, retBy: o.retBy,
    status: o.ret ? 'Returned' : 'On hire', days: o.on ? rnDays_(o.on, end) + 1 : 0, late: late, cost: rnCost_(o, o.on || t, end), hist: o.hist };
}
function rnLists_(ss) {
  var types = rtSheet_(ss), cos = rcSheet_(ss), tn = types.getLastRow(), cn = cos.getLastRow();
  return {
    types: tn > 1 ? types.getRange(2, 1, tn - 1, 2).getValues().filter(function (r) { return String(r[0]).trim(); }).map(function (r) { return [String(r[0]).trim(), String(r[1] || '')]; }) : [],
    cos: cn > 1 ? cos.getRange(2, 1, cn - 1, 1).getValues().map(function (r) { return String(r[0]).trim(); }).filter(String) : []
  };
}
/** Adds a new machine type / company to its list (if it is not there yet). Returns the name as it is in the list. */
function rnRemember_(ss, sh, name, extra, by) {
  name = String(name || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
  if (!name) return '';
  var n = sh.getLastRow(), have = n > 1 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, 1).getValues() : [];
  for (var i = 0; i < have.length; i++) if (String(have[i][0]).trim().toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase()) return String(have[i][0]).trim();
  room_(sh, n + 1, 3);
  sh.getRange(n + 1, 1, 1, 3).setValues([[name, extra || '', by]]);
  return name;
}
/** (not used any more: every item can be chosen unless Rent allowed = No) */
function rnItemOk_(it) { return /^asset/i.test(String(it[2] || '').trim()) || isMach_(it); }
function rnPhoto_(b64, ref, site) {
  if (!b64) return '';
  try {
    var m = String(b64).match(/^data:(image\/\w+);base64,(.+)$/);
    if (!m) return '';
    var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Rental Photos'), folder = it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Rental Photos');
    return folder.createFile(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(m[2]), m[1], ref + ' ' + site + ' ' + today_() + '.jpg')).getUrl();
  } catch (e) { console.error('Rental photo not saved: ' + e); return ''; }
}
function rnState_(ss, site) {
  var t = today_(), from = rnAdd_(t, -30), l = rnLists_(ss);
  var list = rnList_(ss).filter(function (o) { return o.site === site && (!o.ret || o.ret >= from); }).map(function (o) { return rnOut_(o, t); });
  return { ok: true, today: t, types: l.types, cos: l.cos, list: list };
}
/** Storekeeper: rented machines at my site (on hire + returned in the last 30 days) + the type and company lists. */
function loadRentals(site, pin, name) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
  return rnState_(ss, site);
}
/** Checks + cleans what the storekeeper typed (new rental or edit). */
function rnClean_(ss, d, isNew) {
  d = d || {};
  var c = {
    machine: String(d.machine || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 80), size: String(d.size || '').trim().slice(0, 40),
    itemId: String(d.itemId || '').trim(), qty: rnNum_(d.qty || 1), company: String(d.company || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 80),
    plate: String(d.plate || '').trim().slice(0, 80), op: d.op === 'With operator' ? 'With operator' : d.op === 'Without operator' ? 'Without operator' : '',
    rate: rnNum_(d.rate), per: ['Hour', 'Day', 'Month'].indexOf(d.per) >= 0 ? d.per : 'Day', on: rnDate_(d.on), exp: rnDate_(d.exp),
    lpo: String(d.lpo || '').trim().slice(0, 100)
  };
  if (c.itemId) {
    var it = itemsById_(ss)[c.itemId];
    if (!it) return { error: 'Item not found' };
    if (isNew && (itemFlags_(ss)[c.itemId] || [])[2] === false) return { error: it[1] + ' cannot be chosen as rented (Items tab: Rent allowed = No)' };
    c.machine = String(it[1]);
  }
  if (isNew && !c.machine) return { error: 'Choose the machine (or type its name under Other)' };
  if (!isFinite(c.qty) || c.qty < 1 || c.qty !== Math.floor(c.qty)) return { error: 'How many: type a whole number (1 or more)' };
  if (!c.company) return { error: 'Choose or type the rental company' };
  if (!c.op) return { error: 'Choose With operator or Without operator' };
  if (!isFinite(c.rate) || c.rate < 0) return { error: 'Rate: type a number (or leave it empty)' };
  if (!c.on) return { error: 'Choose the on-hire date (the day it arrived)' };
  if (c.on > today_()) return { error: 'The on-hire date cannot be in the future' };
  if (c.exp && c.exp < c.on) return { error: 'Expected return cannot be before the on-hire date' };
  return { c: c };
}
/** Storekeeper adds a rented machine. d = {machine, size, itemId, qty, company, plate, op, rate, per, on, exp, lpo, photo, newType} */
function saveRental(site, pin, name, d) {
  MEMO_.t0 = Date.now();
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    name = String(name).trim();
    var k = rnClean_(ss, d, true);
    if (k.error) return { ok: false, error: k.error };
    var c = k.c, sh = rnSheet_(ss);
    if (!c.itemId) c.machine = rnRemember_(ss, rtSheet_(ss), c.machine, '🛠️', name + ' · ' + site + ' · ' + today_());
    c.company = rnRemember_(ss, rcSheet_(ss), c.company, '', name + ' · ' + site + ' · ' + today_());
    var ref = 'RN-' + ('000' + (maxRef_(sh) + 1)).slice(-4), n = sh.getLastRow();
    var photo = rnPhoto_(d && d.photo, ref, site);
    room_(sh, n + 1, RN_N_);
    sh.getRange(n + 1, 1, 1, RN_N_).setValues([[ref, site, c.machine, c.size, c.itemId, c.qty, c.company, c.plate, c.op, c.rate || '', c.per,
      c.on, c.exp, '', '', c.lpo, '', photo, name, new Date(), '', 'On hire', today_() + ' added by ' + name]]);
    MEMO_.rnRows = null;
    mark_(ss, 'Rented machine', site, name);
    var res = rnState_(ss, site);
    res.msg = ref + ' · ' + (c.qty > 1 ? c.qty + ' × ' : '') + c.machine + ' from ' + c.company;
    return res;
  });
}
/**
 * Storekeeper adds several rented machines that came from the same rental company in one go.
 * common = {company, on, exp, lpo, photo}; lines = [{machine | itemId, qty, size, plate, op, rate, per}]. Each line gets its own RN number.
 */
function saveRentals(site, pin, name, common, lines) {
  MEMO_.t0 = Date.now();
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    name = String(name).trim();
    common = common || {};
    lines = (lines || []).slice(0, 30);
    if (!lines.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Add at least one machine' };
    var ok = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      var d = {}, l = lines[i] || {};
      ['machine', 'itemId', 'qty', 'size', 'plate', 'op', 'rate', 'per'].forEach(function (k) { d[k] = l[k]; });
      ['company', 'on', 'exp', 'lpo'].forEach(function (k) { d[k] = common[k]; });
      var k = rnClean_(ss, d, true);
      if (k.error) return { ok: false, error: (lines.length > 1 ? 'Machine ' + (i + 1) + (d.machine ? ' (' + d.machine + ')' : '') + ': ' : '') + k.error };
      ok.push(k.c);
    }
    var sh = rnSheet_(ss), by = name + ' · ' + site + ' · ' + today_();
    var company = rnRemember_(ss, rcSheet_(ss), ok[0].company, '', by), n0 = maxRef_(sh), first = 'RN-' + ('000' + (n0 + 1)).slice(-4);
    var photo = rnPhoto_(common.photo, first, site), rows = [];
    ok.forEach(function (c, i) {
      if (!c.itemId) c.machine = rnRemember_(ss, rtSheet_(ss), c.machine, '🛠️', by);
      rows.push(['RN-' + ('000' + (n0 + 1 + i)).slice(-4), site, c.machine, c.size, c.itemId, c.qty, company, c.plate, c.op, c.rate || '', c.per,
        c.on, c.exp, '', '', c.lpo, '', photo, name, new Date(), '', 'On hire', today_() + ' added by ' + name + (ok.length > 1 ? ' (one delivery of ' + ok.length + ')' : '')]);
    });
    var n = sh.getLastRow();
    room_(sh, n + rows.length, RN_N_);
    sh.getRange(n + 1, 1, rows.length, RN_N_).setValues(rows);
    MEMO_.rnRows = null;
    mark_(ss, 'Rented machines', site, name);
    var res = rnState_(ss, site);
    res.msg = (rows.length > 1 ? rows.length + ' machines · ' + rows[0][0] + ' to ' + rows[rows.length - 1][0] : rows[0][0] + ' · ' + (ok[0].qty > 1 ? ok[0].qty + ' × ' : '') + ok[0].machine) + ' from ' + company;
    return res;
  });
}
/** The on-hire row of a rental at my site. */
function rnFind_(ss, site, ref) {
  var l = rnList_(ss).filter(function (o) { return o.ref === String(ref) && !o.ret; });
  if (!l.length) return { error: 'This rental is not on hire any more. Refresh the page.' };
  if (l[0].site !== site) return { error: 'This rental is at ' + l[0].site + ', not at your site.' };
  return { o: l[0] };
}
/** Storekeeper corrects a rental that is still on hire (not the machine itself). */
function editRental(site, pin, name, ref, d) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    name = String(name).trim();
    var f = rnFind_(ss, site, ref);
    if (f.error) return { ok: false, error: f.error };
    var o = f.o;
    d = d || {}; d.itemId = o.itemId; d.machine = o.machine;
    var k = rnClean_(ss, d, false);
    if (k.error) return { ok: false, error: k.error };
    var c = k.c;
    c.company = rnRemember_(ss, rcSheet_(ss), c.company, '', name + ' · ' + site + ' · ' + today_());
    var ch = [];
    [['size', 'size'], ['qty', 'qty'], ['company', 'company'], ['plate', 'plate'], ['op', 'operator'], ['rate', 'rate'], ['per', 'per'], ['on', 'on-hire'], ['exp', 'expected return'], ['lpo', 'LPO']].forEach(function (p) {
      if (String(o[p[0]] || '') !== String(c[p[0]] || '')) ch.push(p[1] + ' ' + (o[p[0]] || '–') + ' → ' + (c[p[0]] || '–'));
    });
    var photo = d.photo ? rnPhoto_(d.photo, o.ref, site) : '';
    if (photo) ch.push('new photo');
    if (!ch.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Nothing changed' };
    var sh = rnSheet_(ss);
    sh.getRange(o.row, 4, 1, 13).setValues([[c.size, o.itemId, c.qty, c.company, c.plate, c.op, c.rate || '', c.per, c.on, c.exp, '', '', c.lpo]]);
    if (photo) sh.getRange(o.row, 18).setValue(photo);
    sh.getRange(o.row, 23).setValue((o.hist ? o.hist + '\n' : '') + today_() + ' edited by ' + name + ': ' + ch.join(', '));
    MEMO_.rnRows = null;
    mark_(ss, 'Rented machine edited', site, name);
    var res = rnState_(ss, site);
    res.msg = o.ref + ' updated';
    return res;
  });
}
/** Storekeeper: the machine (or some of them) went back to the rental company. */
function returnRental(site, pin, name, ref, qty, date, hours, note) {
  MEMO_.t0 = Date.now();
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    name = String(name).trim();
    var f = rnFind_(ss, site, ref);
    if (f.error) return { ok: false, error: f.error };
    var o = f.o, k = rnNum_(qty || o.qty), dt = rnDate_(date), h = rnNum_(hours), t = today_();
    if (!isFinite(k) || k < 1 || k !== Math.floor(k) || k > o.qty) return { ok: false, error: 'How many went back: 1 to ' + o.qty };
    if (!dt) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose the return (off-hire) date' };
    if (dt > t) return { ok: false, error: 'The return date cannot be in the future' };
    if (dt < o.on) return { ok: false, error: 'The return date cannot be before the on-hire date (' + o.on + ')' };
    if (!isFinite(h) || h < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Hours: type a number' };
    note = String(note || '').trim().slice(0, 100);
    var sh = rnSheet_(ss), line = dt + ' returned' + (k < o.qty ? ' ' + k + ' of ' + o.qty : '') + ' by ' + name;
    if (k === o.qty) {
      sh.getRange(o.row, 14, 1, 4).setValues([[dt, h || '', o.lpo, note]]);
      sh.getRange(o.row, 21, 1, 3).setValues([[name, 'Returned', (o.hist ? o.hist + '\n' : '') + line]]);
    } else {
      var r = rnRows_(ss)[o.row - 2].slice(), n = sh.getLastRow();
      r[5] = k; r[13] = dt; r[14] = h || ''; r[16] = note; r[20] = name; r[21] = 'Returned'; r[22] = (o.hist ? o.hist + '\n' : '') + line;
      room_(sh, n + 1, RN_N_);
      sh.getRange(n + 1, 1, 1, RN_N_).setValues([r]);
      sh.getRange(o.row, 6).setValue(o.qty - k);
      sh.getRange(o.row, 23).setValue((o.hist ? o.hist + '\n' : '') + line + ' · ' + (o.qty - k) + ' still on site');
    }
    MEMO_.rnRows = null;
    mark_(ss, 'Rented machine returned', site, name);
    var done = rnOut_(rnObj_([o.ref, site, o.machine, '', o.itemId, k, o.company, '', '', o.rate, o.per, o.on, o.exp, dt, h], 0), t);
    var res = rnState_(ss, site);
    res.msg = (k > 1 ? k + ' × ' : '') + o.machine + ' returned · ' + done.days + ' days' + (done.cost ? ' · about AED ' + Math.round(done.cost).toLocaleString('en-US') : '');
    return res;
  });
}
/** Storekeeper: a rented machine goes from my site to another site (it stays on hire). */
function moveRental(site, pin, name, ref, toSite) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    name = String(name).trim();
    toSite = String(toSite || '').trim();
    if (sitesList_(ss).indexOf(toSite) < 0 || toSite === site) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose the site it is going to' };
    var f = rnFind_(ss, site, ref);
    if (f.error) return { ok: false, error: f.error };
    var o = f.o, sh = rnSheet_(ss);
    sh.getRange(o.row, 2).setValue(toSite);
    sh.getRange(o.row, 23).setValue((o.hist ? o.hist + '\n' : '') + today_() + ' moved ' + site + ' → ' + toSite + ' by ' + name);
    MEMO_.rnRows = null;
    mark_(ss, 'Rented machine moved', site, name);
    var res = rnState_(ss, site);
    res.msg = o.machine + ' moved to ' + toSite;
    return res;
  });
}
/** On hire at the end of a date: { itemId: { site: qty } } – for the "+ rented" line in the office stock list. */
function rnByItem_(ss, date) {
  var out = {};
  try {
    rnList_(ss).forEach(function (o) {
      if (!o.itemId || !o.on || o.on > date || (o.ret && o.ret <= date)) return;
      out[o.itemId] = out[o.itemId] || {};
      out[o.itemId][o.site] = (out[o.itemId][o.site] || 0) + o.qty;
    });
  } catch (e) { console.error('rentals: ' + e); }
  return out;
}
/** Office (ALL SITES): every rental on hire + returned in the last 120 days. */
function loadRentalsOffice(pin) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var t = today_(), from = rnAdd_(t, -120), m0 = t.slice(0, 8) + '01', l = rnLists_(ss), month = 0;
  var all = rnList_(ss);
  all.forEach(function (o) { month += rnCost_(o, m0, t); });
  return { ok: true, today: t, sites: sitesList_(ss), cos: l.cos, types: l.types, monthCost: Math.round(month),
    list: all.filter(function (o) { return !o.ret || o.ret >= from; }).map(function (o) { return rnOut_(o, t); }) };
}
/** Office: monthly rental report PDF. month = 'yyyy-MM', site = '' for all sites. */
function makeRentalPdf(pin, month, site) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var t = today_();
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(String(month))) month = t.slice(0, 7);
  var from = month + '-01', to = month + '-' + ('0' + rnMonthDays_(from)).slice(-2), upto = to < t ? to : t;
  var MN = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'];
  var label = MN[+month.slice(5) - 1] + ' ' + month.slice(0, 4);
  var rows = rnList_(ss).filter(function (o) { return o.on && o.on <= to && (!o.ret || o.ret >= from) && (!site || o.site === site); });
  if (!rows.length) return { ok: false, error: 'No rented machines in ' + label + (site ? ' at ' + site : '') + '.' };
  rows.sort(function (a, b) { return a.company < b.company ? -1 : a.company > b.company ? 1 : a.on < b.on ? -1 : a.on > b.on ? 1 : 0; });
  var f = function (d) { return d ? +d.slice(8) + ' ' + MN[+d.slice(5, 7) - 1].slice(0, 3) + (d.slice(0, 7) !== month ? ' ' + d.slice(2, 4) : '') : '—'; };
  var n = function (x) { return Math.round(x).toLocaleString('en-US'); };
  var tot = 0, byCo = {}, sites = {};
  var body = rows.map(function (o, i) {
    var a = o.on > from ? o.on : from, e = o.ret || upto, b = e < upto ? e : upto, d = a <= b ? rnDays_(a, b) + 1 : 0, c = rnCost_(o, from, upto);
    tot += c; byCo[o.company] = (byCo[o.company] || 0) + c; sites[o.site] = 1;
    var late = !o.ret && o.exp && o.exp < t;
    return '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '><td class="nw">' + esc_(o.ref) + '</td><td class="nw">' + esc_(o.site) + '</td><td><b>' + esc_(o.machine) + '</b>' + (o.size ? ' ' + esc_(o.size) : '') + (o.itemId ? ' <span style="color:#666">(our item ' + esc_(o.itemId) + ')</span>' : '') + '</td><td class="n">' + o.qty +
      '</td><td>' + esc_(o.company) + '</td><td>' + esc_(o.plate) + '</td><td>' + (o.op === 'With operator' ? 'Yes' : 'No') + '</td><td class="nw">' + f(o.on) + '</td><td class="nw">' + (o.ret ? f(o.ret) : '<span style="color:' + (late ? '#B8274A' : '#555') + '">' + (late ? 'late · ' : '') + 'on site</span>') +
      '</td><td class="n">' + (o.per === 'Hour' ? (o.hours ? o.hours + ' h' : '–') : d) + '</td><td class="n">' + (o.rate ? n(o.rate) + ' / ' + o.per.toLowerCase() : '–') + '</td><td class="n amt">' + (c ? n(c) : '–') + '</td><td>' + esc_(o.lpo) + '</td></tr>';
  }).join('');
  var coLine = Object.keys(byCo).sort().map(function (k) { return esc_(k) + ' <b>' + n(byCo[k]) + '</b>'; }).join(' &middot; ');
  var head = ['Ref', 'Site', 'Machine', 'Qty', 'Rental company', 'Plate / fleet', 'Operator', 'On-hire', 'Returned', 'Days in month', 'Rate (AED)', 'Amount (AED)', 'LPO / DN'];
  var top = '<table class="hd"><tr><td style="width:64px"><img src="' + LOGO_ + '" style="width:50px;height:50px"></td><td><div style="font-size:17pt;font-weight:bold">RENTED MACHINES · ' + esc_(label.toUpperCase()) + '</div>' +
    '<div style="font-size:10pt;font-weight:bold;color:#C42D4E">WALK THROUGH GENERAL CONTRACTING - SOLE PROPRIETORSHIP LLC</div><div style="color:#555">Sites: ' + esc_(site || Object.keys(sites).sort().join(', ')) +
    ' &middot; Rentals: ' + rows.length + ' &middot; Days counted inside ' + esc_(label) + (upto < to ? ' up to ' + f(upto) : '') + '</div></td></tr></table>';
  var html = '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><style>@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:12mm}*{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}thead{display:table-header-group}tr{break-inside:avoid;page-break-inside:avoid}' +
    'body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:8.5pt;color:#1E2329}table.t{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}.t th{background:#0E7C86;color:#fff;padding:5px;text-align:left;border:1px solid #0B636B}.t th.top{background:none;border:0;padding:0 0 8px;color:#1E2329;font-weight:normal}' +
    '.t td{border:1px solid #D5D5D5;padding:4px}.t tr.z td{background:#F4F3EF}.t td.n{text-align:right;white-space:nowrap}.t td.amt,.t tr.z td.amt{background:#E3F3F4;font-weight:bold}.t td.nw{white-space:nowrap}table.hd td{border:0;padding:0 6px 0 0;vertical-align:middle}.sum td{border-top:2px solid #1E2329;font-weight:bold;background:#fff}</style></head><body>' +
    '<table class="t"><thead><tr><th class="top" colspan="' + head.length + '">' + top + '</th></tr><tr>' + head.map(function (h) { return '<th>' + h + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' + body +
    '<tr class="sum"><td colspan="11" style="text-align:right">' + coLine + ' &nbsp; TOTAL</td><td class="n amt">' + n(tot) + '</td><td></td></tr></tbody></table>' +
    '<p style="font-size:7.5pt;color:#666">Amounts are estimates from the rates the sites entered: day rate × days × qty; month rate ÷ days in the month × days; hour rate × hours (counted in the month of return). Compare with the rental companies’ invoices. Generated ' + esc_(Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm')) + '</p></body></html>';
  try {
    var pdf = Utilities.newBlob(html, 'text/html', 'rentals.html').getAs('application/pdf').setName('Rented_machines_' + month + (site ? '_' + site.replace(/\W+/g, '_') : '') + '.pdf'), url = '';
    try {
      var it2 = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Stock PDFs'), folder = it2.hasNext() ? it2.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Stock PDFs');
      url = folder.createFile(pdf).getUrl();
    } catch (e2) { console.error('PDF not saved to Drive: ' + e2); }
    return { ok: true, name: pdf.getName(), url: url, b64: Utilities.base64Encode(pdf.getBytes()), count: rows.length, total: Math.round(tot) };
  } catch (e) {
    var m = String(e && e.message || e);
    return { ok: false, error: /authoriz|permission/i.test(m) ? 'Google needs your permission first: open the Google Sheet and click WTC Stock > Make a test PDF, then allow access.' : 'Could not create the PDF: ' + m };
  }
}

/* ======================= Site services: waste skip changed · water tank filled · septic tank emptied · diesel / petrol bought =======================
 * Works on the existing items (found by name): WASTE SKIP (SUPPLIER), every WATER TANK…, every GRP SEPTIC TANK…, DIESEL, PETROL.
 * Skips / tanks: only counts how many times the supplier came – the stock (Total) is not changed.
 * Fuel: litres + AED; "Into our fuel tank / cans" also adds the litres to stock (as a Received entry, RC-…); "Straight into machines / vehicles" does not.
 * Storekeepers cannot cancel (same as other entries): the office uses WTC Stock > Cancel a transaction with the SV-… number.
 */
var SV_TAB_ = 'Site Services';
var SV_HEAD_ = ['Ref', 'Date', 'Time', 'Site', 'Service', 'Item ID', 'Item', 'How many', 'Unit', 'Amount (AED)', 'Where it went', 'Into what',
                'Supplier / station', 'Ticket / receipt no.', 'Recorded by', 'Status', 'Linked entry', 'Cancelled by', 'Cancelled at'];
var SV_N_ = SV_HEAD_.length;
var SV_KIND_ = {
  skip: { word: 'Skip changed', unit: 'skips', col: '#4E7D2B', ic: '♻️', title: 'Waste skips changed', verb: 'Changed' },
  water: { word: 'Water filled', unit: 'tanks', col: '#1F6FB2', ic: '💧', title: 'Water tanks filled', verb: 'Filled' },
  septic: { word: 'Septic emptied', unit: 'tanks', col: '#8A5A12', ic: '🚛', title: 'GRP septic tanks emptied', verb: 'Emptied' },
  fuel: { word: 'Fuel bought', unit: 'litres', col: '#B5480F', ic: '⛽', title: 'Diesel and petrol bought', verb: 'Bought' }
};
/** Which service an item has (by its name), or ''. The page uses the same rule. */
function svKind_(name) {
  var n = String(name || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if (/^WASTE SKIP/.test(n)) return 'skip';
  if (/^WATER TANK/.test(n)) return 'water';
  if (/^GRP SEPTIC TANK/.test(n)) return 'septic';
  if (n === 'DIESEL' || n === 'PETROL') return 'fuel';
  return '';
}
function svSheet_(ss) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(SV_TAB_);
  if (sh) return sh;
  try { sh = ss.insertSheet(SV_TAB_); } catch (e) { sh = ss.getSheetByName(SV_TAB_); if (sh) return sh; throw e; }
