  sh.getRange('B:B').setNumberFormat('@'); sh.getRange('N:N').setNumberFormat('@');
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, SV_N_).setValues([SV_HEAD_]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#1E2329').setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  try { sh.setTabColor('#4E7D2B'); } catch (e) {}
  return sh;
}
function svList_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.svList) return MEMO_.svList;
  var sh = svSheet_(ss), n = sh.getLastRow(), out = [];
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, SV_N_).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    if (!r[0]) return;
    out.push({ row: i + 2, ref: String(r[0]), date: dstr_(r[1]), time: r[2] instanceof Date ? ('0' + r[2].getHours()).slice(-2) + ':' + ('0' + r[2].getMinutes()).slice(-2) : String(r[2] || '').replace(/^'/, ''), site: String(r[3]).trim(), service: String(r[4]), itemId: String(r[5]),
      item: String(r[6]), qty: Number(r[7]) || 0, unit: String(r[8] || ''), aed: Number(r[9]) || 0, where: String(r[10] || ''), into: String(r[11] || ''),
      supplier: String(r[12] || ''), ticket: String(r[13] || ''), by: String(r[14] || ''), status: String(r[15] || 'OK'), link: String(r[16] || ''),
      kind: svKind_(r[6]) });
  });
  return (MEMO_.svList = out);
}
function svOk_(e) { return !/^Cancelled/.test(e.status); }
/** This month's totals for one site: { itemId: [how many, AED, last date, entries] } – shown on the item in Stock today. */
function svSite_(ss, site, date) {
  var m = String(date || today_()).slice(0, 7), out = {};
  try {
    svList_(ss).forEach(function (e) {
      if (e.site !== site || !svOk_(e) || e.date.slice(0, 7) !== m || e.date > date) return;
      var v = out[e.itemId] || (out[e.itemId] = [0, 0, '', 0]);
      v[0] = r3_(v[0] + e.qty); v[1] = Math.round((v[1] + e.aed) * 100) / 100; if (e.date > v[2]) v[2] = e.date; v[3]++;
    });
  } catch (e) { console.error('services: ' + e); }
  return out;
}
/** Office: this month's totals { itemId: { site: [how many, AED] } }. */
function svAll_(ss, date) {
  var m = String(date || today_()).slice(0, 7), out = {};
  try {
    svList_(ss).forEach(function (e) {
      if (!svOk_(e) || e.date.slice(0, 7) !== m || e.date > date) return;
      var a = out[e.itemId] || (out[e.itemId] = {}), v = a[e.site] || (a[e.site] = [0, 0]);
      v[0] = r3_(v[0] + e.qty); v[1] = Math.round((v[1] + e.aed) * 100) / 100;
    });
  } catch (e) { console.error('services: ' + e); }
  return out;
}
/** Storekeeper saves one service entry. d = {itemId, qty, date, ticket, amount, supplier, where ('tank' | 'direct'), into} */
function saveService(site, pin, name, d) {
  MEMO_.t0 = Date.now();
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  return withLock_(function () {
    var err = trCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (err) return { ok: false, error: err };
    name = String(name).trim(); d = d || {};
    var it = itemsById_(ss)[String(d.itemId)];
    if (!it) return { ok: false, error: 'Item not found. Refresh the page.' };
    var kind = svKind_(it[1]), K = SV_KIND_[kind];
    if (!K) return { ok: false, error: it[1] + ' has no service button' };
    var q = Number(String(d.qty == null ? '' : d.qty).replace(/,/g, '').trim()), t = today_(), date = dstr_(d.date) || t;
    if (!isFinite(q) || q <= 0) return { ok: false, error: kind === 'fuel' ? 'Type how many litres were bought' : 'Choose how many (1 or more)' };
    if (kind !== 'fuel' && q !== Math.floor(q)) return { ok: false, error: 'How many: a whole number' };
    q = r3_(q);
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date) || date > t) return { ok: false, error: 'The date cannot be in the future' };
    var ticket = String(d.ticket || '').trim().slice(0, 60), supplier = String(d.supplier || '').trim().slice(0, 80), into = String(d.into || '').trim().slice(0, 80);
    var aed = String(d.amount == null ? '' : d.amount).replace(/,/g, '').trim(), where = '';
    aed = aed === '' ? 0 : Number(aed);
    if (!isFinite(aed) || aed < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Amount: type a number (or leave it empty)' };
    if (kind === 'fuel') {
      if (d.where !== 'tank' && d.where !== 'direct') return { ok: false, error: 'Choose where the fuel went' };
      where = d.where === 'tank' ? 'Into fuel tank / cans' : 'Straight into machines / vehicles';
    } else { aed = 0; supplier = supplier || ''; into = ''; }
    var sh = svSheet_(ss), ref = 'SV-' + ('000' + (maxRef_(sh) + 1)).slice(-4), now = new Date(), link = '', after = null;
    if (kind === 'fuel' && d.where === 'tank') {   // the litres go into stock: a normal Received entry
      var rows = ledger_(ss), msh = mvSheet_(ss, 'received'), m = MV_.received;
      link = m.prefix + '-' + ('000' + (maxRef_(msh) + 1)).slice(-4);
      var have = haveOf_(rows, site, it[0]); after = r3_((have || 0) + q);
      var info1 = supplier || 'Fuel bought', info2 = ticket ? ticket + ' · ' + ref : ref;
      appendLedger_(ss, rows, [[now, t, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], after, have === null ? '' : have, name, m.word + ' ' + link + ' from ' + info1 + ' (' + ref + ')']]);
      room_(msh, msh.getLastRow() + 1, 16);
      msh.getRange(msh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, 16).setValues([[link, now, t, site, it[0], it[1], it[4], q, have === null ? '' : have, after, name, info1, info2, 'Recorded', '', '']]);
      liveSafe_(ss, rows);
    }
    room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + 1, SV_N_);
    sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, SV_N_).setValues([[ref, date, "'" + Utilities.formatDate(now, TZ_, 'HH:mm'), site, K.word, it[0], it[1], q, kind === 'fuel' ? 'litres' : K.unit,
      aed || '', where, into, supplier, ticket, name, 'OK', link, '', '']]);
    MEMO_.svList = null;
    mark_(ss, K.word + ' ' + ref, site, name);
    var msg = kind === 'fuel' ? q + ' L ' + String(it[1]).toLowerCase() + (aed ? ' · AED ' + aed.toFixed(2) : '') + ' · ' + (link ? 'into fuel tank (stock ' + after + ' L)' : 'straight into ' + (into || 'machines / vehicles'))
                              : q + ' ' + (q == 1 ? K.unit.replace(/s$/, '') : K.unit) + ' ' + K.verb.toLowerCase() + ' · ' + it[1];
    return withState_(ss, site, { ok: true, ref: ref, link: link, msg: msg + ' · ' + site + (ticket ? ' · ' + ticket : ''), kind: kind }, null);
  });
}
/** Storekeeper: the entries of one item at my site (this month and last month). */
function serviceHistory(site, pin, itemId) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  if (!checkPin_(ss, site, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
  var t = today_(), from = new Date(Date.parse(t.slice(0, 8) + '01') - 20 * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 7) + '-01';
  var l = svList_(ss).filter(function (e) { return e.site === site && e.itemId === String(itemId) && e.date >= from; });
  l.sort(function (a, b) { return a.date + a.time < b.date + b.time ? 1 : -1; });
  return { ok: true, today: t, list: l.map(function (e) { return { ref: e.ref, date: e.date, time: e.time, qty: e.qty, unit: e.unit, aed: e.aed, where: e.where, into: e.into, supplier: e.supplier, ticket: e.ticket, by: e.by, status: e.status, link: e.link }; }) };
}
/** Office cancel of an SV-… entry (also cancels the linked Received entry of fuel put into the tank). */
function svCancel_(ss, ref, dry) {
  var e = svList_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.ref === ref; })[0];
  if (!e) return { ok: false, error: 'No entry ' + ref + ' was found.' };
  if (!svOk_(e)) return { ok: false, error: ref + ' is already cancelled.' };
  var desc = e.service + ' ' + ref + ' · ' + e.site + ' · ' + e.date + ' · ' + e.qty + ' ' + e.unit + ' · ' + e.item + ' · by ' + e.by;
  var linked = null;
  if (e.link) { linked = officeCancel_(ss, e.link, true); if (!linked.ok && !/already cancelled/.test(linked.error)) return linked; }
  if (dry) return { ok: true, summary: desc + (linked && linked.ok ? '\n\nThe fuel put into the tank is taken back from stock too (' + e.link + '):\n' + linked.summary : '\n\nThe stock does not change.') };
  if (linked && linked.ok) { var r = officeCancel_(ss, e.link, false); if (!r.ok) return r; }
  svSheet_(ss).getRange(e.row, 16, 1, 4).setValues([['Cancelled by office', e.link, 'Office', new Date()]]);
  MEMO_.svList = null;
  return { ok: true };
}
/** Office: monthly Site services PDF (all four services). month = 'yyyy-MM', site = '' for all sites. */
function makeServicesPdf(pin, month, site) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var t = today_();
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(String(month))) month = t.slice(0, 7);
  var MN = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'];
  var label = MN[+month.slice(5) - 1] + ' ' + month.slice(0, 4);
  var all = svList_(ss).filter(function (e) { return svOk_(e) && e.date.slice(0, 7) === month && (!site || e.site === site); });
  if (!all.length) return { ok: false, error: 'No site services recorded in ' + label + (site ? ' at ' + site : '') + '.' };
  var end = month + '-31' < t ? month + '-31' : t, rows = ledger_(ss), qn = stockAsOf_(rows, end > t ? t : end, '');
  var n = function (x, dp) { return Number(x).toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: dp || 0, maximumFractionDigits: dp || 0 }); };
  var f = function (d) { return +d.slice(8) + ' ' + MN[+d.slice(5, 7) - 1].slice(0, 3); };
  var css = '@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:12mm}*{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:9pt;color:#1E2329}' +
    'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-bottom:6px}thead{display:table-header-group}tr{break-inside:avoid;page-break-inside:avoid}th{color:#fff;padding:5px 6px;text-align:left}td{border:1px solid #D5D5D5;padding:4px 6px}tr.z td{background:#F4F3EF}td.n,th.n{text-align:right;white-space:nowrap}td.n{font-weight:bold}tr.s td{background:#fff;font-weight:bold;border-top:2px solid #1E2329}' +
    'h2{font-size:12pt;margin:14px 0 4px}.hd td{border:0;padding:0 6px 0 0;vertical-align:middle}.note{font-size:7.5pt;color:#666}';
  var html = '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><style>' + css + '</style></head><body><table class="hd"><tr><td style="width:62px"><img src="' + LOGO_ + '" style="width:50px;height:50px"></td><td><div style="font-size:17pt;font-weight:bold">SITE SERVICES · ' + esc_(label.toUpperCase()) + '</div>' +
    '<div style="font-size:10pt;font-weight:bold;color:#C42D4E">WALK THROUGH GENERAL CONTRACTING - SOLE PROPRIETORSHIP LLC</div><div style="color:#555">' + esc_(site || 'All sites') + ' · waste skips changed · water tanks filled · septic tanks emptied · diesel and petrol bought</div></td></tr></table>';
  ['skip', 'water', 'septic', 'fuel'].forEach(function (k) {
    var K = SV_KIND_[k], es = all.filter(function (e) { return e.kind === k; });
    if (!es.length) return;
    html += '<h2 style="color:' + K.col + '">' + K.ic + ' ' + K.title + '</h2>';
    var g = {}, keys = [];
    es.forEach(function (e) { var key = e.site + '|' + e.itemId; if (!g[key]) { g[key] = { site: e.site, itemId: e.itemId, item: e.item, q: 0, aed: 0, last: '', tank: 0, direct: 0 }; keys.push(key); } var x = g[key]; x.q = r3_(x.q + e.qty); x.aed += e.aed; if (e.date > x.last) x.last = e.date; if (e.link) x.tank = r3_(x.tank + e.qty); else x.direct = r3_(x.direct + e.qty); });
    keys.sort();
    var th = '<th style="background:' + K.col + '">';
    if (k === 'fuel') {
      html += '<table><thead><tr>' + th + 'Site</th>' + th + 'Fuel</th><th class="n" style="background:' + K.col + '">Litres bought</th><th class="n" style="background:' + K.col + '">Into tank / cans</th><th class="n" style="background:' + K.col + '">Straight into machines</th><th class="n" style="background:' + K.col + '">Amount AED</th><th class="n" style="background:' + K.col + '">AED / litre</th>' + th + 'Last</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
      var tq = 0, ta = 0;
      keys.forEach(function (key, i) { var x = g[key]; tq += x.q; ta += x.aed; html += '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '><td>' + esc_(x.site) + '</td><td>' + esc_(x.item) + '</td><td class="n">' + n(x.q) + '</td><td class="n">' + n(x.tank) + '</td><td class="n">' + n(x.direct) + '</td><td class="n">' + (x.aed ? n(x.aed, 2) : '–') + '</td><td class="n">' + (x.aed ? n(x.aed / x.q, 2) : '–') + '</td><td>' + f(x.last) + '</td></tr>'; });
      html += '<tr class="s"><td colspan="2">Total</td><td class="n">' + n(tq) + '</td><td></td><td></td><td class="n">' + n(ta, 2) + '</td><td></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table>';
    } else {
      html += '<table><thead><tr>' + th + 'Site</th>' + th + 'Item</th><th class="n" style="background:' + K.col + '">On site</th><th class="n" style="background:' + K.col + '">' + K.verb + ' in ' + esc_(MN[+month.slice(5) - 1].slice(0, 3)) + '</th>' + th + 'Last</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
      var tt = 0;
      keys.forEach(function (key, i) { var x = g[key], on = qn[x.itemId] && qn[x.itemId][x.site] ? qn[x.itemId][x.site][0] : ''; tt += x.q; html += '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '><td>' + esc_(x.site) + '</td><td>' + esc_(x.item) + '</td><td class="n">' + (on === '' ? '–' : on) + '</td><td class="n">' + n(x.q) + '</td><td>' + f(x.last) + '</td></tr>'; });
      html += '<tr class="s"><td colspan="3">Total</td><td class="n">' + n(tt) + '</td><td></td></tr></tbody></table>';
    }
  });
  all.sort(function (a, b) { return (a.date + a.time + a.ref) < (b.date + b.time + b.ref) ? -1 : 1; });
  html += '<h2 style="page-break-before:always">Every entry</h2><table><thead><tr>' + ['Ref', 'Date', 'Site', 'Service', 'Item', 'How many', 'AED', 'Supplier / station', 'Ticket / receipt', 'Where / into', 'Recorded by'].map(function (h, i) { return '<th style="background:#1E2329"' + (i === 5 || i === 6 ? ' class="n"' : '') + '>' + h + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' +
    all.map(function (e, i) { return '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '><td>' + esc_(e.ref) + '</td><td style="white-space:nowrap">' + f(e.date) + ' ' + esc_(e.time) + '</td><td>' + esc_(e.site) + '</td><td>' + esc_(SV_KIND_[e.kind] ? SV_KIND_[e.kind].ic + ' ' : '') + esc_(e.service) + '</td><td>' + esc_(e.item) + '</td><td class="n">' + n(e.qty) + ' ' + esc_(e.unit === 'litres' ? 'L' : '') + '</td><td class="n">' + (e.aed ? n(e.aed, 2) : '') + '</td><td>' + esc_(e.supplier) + '</td><td>' + esc_(e.ticket) + '</td><td>' + esc_([e.where, e.into].filter(String).join(' · ')) + (e.link ? ' (' + esc_(e.link) + ')' : '') + '</td><td>' + esc_(e.by) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</tbody></table>' +
    '<p class="note">Compare each total with that supplier’s invoice. Skips and tanks: counts of supplier visits – the stock is not changed by them. Fuel “into tank / cans” was also added to stock as a Received entry. Generated ' + esc_(Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm')) + '</p></body></html>';
  try {
    var pdf = Utilities.newBlob(html, 'text/html', 'services.html').getAs('application/pdf').setName('Site_services_' + month + (site ? '_' + site.replace(/\W+/g, '_') : '') + '.pdf'), url = '';
    try { var it2 = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Stock PDFs'), folder = it2.hasNext() ? it2.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Stock PDFs'); url = folder.createFile(pdf).getUrl(); } catch (e2) { console.error('PDF not saved to Drive: ' + e2); }
    return { ok: true, name: pdf.getName(), url: url, b64: Utilities.base64Encode(pdf.getBytes()), count: all.length };
  } catch (e) {
    var msg = String(e && e.message || e);
    return { ok: false, error: /authoriz|permission/i.test(msg) ? 'Google needs your permission first: open the Google Sheet and click WTC Stock > Make a test PDF, then allow access.' : 'Could not create the PDF: ' + msg };
  }
}

/* ======================= Excess materials stores (Main Musaffah Store · Al-Faya Yard) =======================
 * Materials that come back from finished sites (new or used). There is no fixed item list: every material
 * is a CARD with its own code (EX-0001), details and up to 5 photos.
 * Tabs (made by themselves):
 *   Excess Stores – Store | PIN | Storekeeper (one row per storekeeper, own PIN – like the Sites tab)
 *   Excess Items  – one row per card      Excess Moves – every in and out      Excess Photos – links to the photos
 * The quantity of a card in each store is always worked out from its moves (cancelled moves are left out).
 */
var XS_TAB_ = 'Excess Stores', XI_TAB_ = 'Excess Items', XM_TAB_ = 'Excess Moves', XP_TAB_ = 'Excess Photos', X_COL_ = '#5A3E8F';
var XS_START_ = ['Main Musaffah Store', 'Al-Faya Yard'];
var XI_HEAD_ = ['Code', 'Category', 'Name', 'Brand', 'Model / code', 'Size', 'Colour / type', 'Unit', 'Condition', 'First store', 'Zone / rack',
  'In store now', 'Note', 'Created', 'Created by', 'Status', 'Thumb'];
var XI_N_ = XI_HEAD_.length;
var XM_HEAD_ = ['Ref', 'Date', 'Time', 'Code', 'Material', 'Move', 'Qty', 'Unit', 'From', 'To', 'Note no.', 'Taken / brought by', 'Recorded by',
  'Note', 'Status', 'Cancelled by', 'Cancelled at'];
var XM_N_ = XM_HEAD_.length;
var XP_HEAD_ = ['Code', 'No.', 'What', 'File ID', 'Link', 'Added', 'By'];
var X_CATS_ = ['Tiles & stone', 'Sanitary ware', 'Lighting', 'Electrical', 'Plumbing', 'Doors & ironmongery', 'Kitchen', 'Gypsum, ceiling & finishes', 'Paint & painting tools',
  'Chemicals, sealants & waterproofing', 'Machinery & power tools', 'Hand tools', 'Cutting discs, bits & blades', 'Fixings & fasteners', 'Safety & PPE', 'Site consumables',
  'Wood, scaffolding & formwork', 'Blocks & foundation', 'Office, camp & furniture', 'Site materials'];
var X_UNITS_ = ['pcs', 'box', 'm²', 'metre', 'roll', 'set', 'pair', 'packet', 'bundle', 'slab', 'bag', 'carton', 'can', 'kg', 'litre'];
var X_CONDS_ = ['New', 'Used – good', 'Used – damaged'];
var X_MAXPH_ = 5;

function xTab_(ss, name, head, textCols, start, note) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  if (sh) return sh;
  try { sh = ss.insertSheet(name); } catch (e) { sh = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (sh) return sh; throw e; }
  (textCols || []).forEach(function (c) { sh.getRange(c + ':' + c).setNumberFormat('@'); });
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setValues([head]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground(X_COL_).setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
  if (start && start.length) sh.getRange(2, 1, start.length, start[0].length).setValues(start);
  if (note) try { sh.getRange(1, 1).setNote(note); } catch (e) {}
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  try { sh.setTabColor(X_COL_); } catch (e) {}
  return sh;
}
function xsSheet_(ss) {
  var sh = xTab_(ss, XS_TAB_, ['Store', 'PIN', 'Storekeeper', XS_LINK_HEAD_], ['B'], XS_START_.map(function (s) { return [s, '', '', '']; }),
    'Excess materials stores. One row per storekeeper with his own PIN (like the Sites tab). Add more rows for more storekeepers or stores.');
  if (!MEMO_.xsHead) {
    MEMO_.xsHead = 1;
    try {
      room_(sh, 1, 4);
      if (String(sh.getRange(1, 4).getValue()).trim() === '') sh.getRange(1, 4).setValue(XS_LINK_HEAD_).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground(X_COL_).setFontColor('#FFFFFF')
        .setNote('Optional. If the same storekeeper also runs a site, type that site name here exactly as in the Sites tab (e.g. MUSAFFAH STORE). He then signs in once with his site PIN and sees both: Site stock and Excess materials. The store is no longer listed on its own on the sign-in page.');
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  return sh;
}
var XS_LINK_HEAD_ = 'Same login as site';
/** {excess store: site} for stores run with the same login as a site (column D of Excess Stores). */
function xLinks_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.xLinks) return MEMO_.xLinks;
  var o = {}, sites = {}; sitesList_(ss).forEach(function (s) { sites[s.toLowerCase()] = s; });
  xsRows_(ss).forEach(function (r) { var st = String(r[0]).trim(), l = String(r[3] == null ? '' : r[3]).trim().toLowerCase(); if (st && l && sites[l]) o[st] = sites[l]; });
  return (MEMO_.xLinks = o);
}
/** Excess stores that open together with this site. */
function xLinkedOf_(ss, site) { var L = xLinks_(ss); return Object.keys(L).filter(function (st) { return L[st] === site; }); }
function xiSheet_(ss) { return xTab_(ss, XI_TAB_, XI_HEAD_, ['A', 'E', 'F', 'G', 'K', 'M', 'N'], null, 'One row per material card. The quantity (In store now) is worked out from Excess Moves – do not type it.'); }
function xmSheet_(ss) { return xTab_(ss, XM_TAB_, XM_HEAD_, ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'K', 'N'], null, 'Every move of the excess materials. A wrong move is cancelled by the office: WTC Stock > Cancel a transaction, with its EXM number.'); }
function xpSheet_(ss) { return xTab_(ss, XP_TAB_, XP_HEAD_, ['A', 'D', 'F'], null, 'Photos are saved in the Google Drive folder “WTC Excess Photos”.'); }

function xsRows_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.xs) return MEMO_.xs;
  var sh = xsSheet_(ss), n = sh.getLastRow();
  return (MEMO_.xs = n > 1 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, Math.min(4, sh.getMaxColumns())).getValues().filter(function (r) { return String(r[0]).trim(); }) : []);
}
function xStores_(ss) {
  var out = [];
  xsRows_(ss).forEach(function (r) { var s = String(r[0]).trim(); if (s && out.indexOf(s) < 0 && !isAll_(s)) out.push(s); });
  return out;
}
function xNames_(ss) {
  var o = {};
  xsRows_(ss).forEach(function (r) {
    var s = String(r[0]).trim(), n = String(r[2] == null ? '' : r[2]).trim();
    if (!o[s]) o[s] = [];
    if (n && o[s].indexOf(n) < 0) o[s].push(n);
  });
  return o;
}
/** Store names are matched loosely: “AL FAYA YARD”, “Al-Faya Yard” and “Main Al-Faya Yard” are the same store. */
function xKey_(n) { return String(n || '').toLowerCase().replace(/\b(main|store|yard|the)\b/g, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''); }
function xCanon_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.xCanon) return MEMO_.xCanon;
  var m = {};
  xStores_(ss).forEach(function (st) { m[xKey_(st)] = st; });
  return (MEMO_.xCanon = m);
}
function xFix_(ss, n) {
  var s = String(n == null ? '' : n).trim();
  if (s && xStores_(ss).indexOf(s) < 0 && siteNames_(ss)[s]) return n;   // a real site (e.g. MUSAFFAH STORE) is never read as an excess store
  var k = xKey_(n), c = xCanon_(ss); return k && c[k] ? c[k] : n;
}
function xIs_(ss, site) { return xStores_(ss).indexOf(String(site || '').trim()) >= 0; }
/** '' when the store, PIN and name are right; otherwise the message for the page. */
function xCheck_(ss, store, pin, name) {
  store = String(store || '').trim();
  var p = String(pin || '').trim(), nm = String(name || '').trim();
  var rows = xsRows_(ss).filter(function (r) { return xKey_(r[0]) === xKey_(store); });
  if (!rows.length) return 'Unknown store';
  var lk = xLinks_(ss)[xFix_(ss, store)];
  if (lk && p && nm && checkPin_(ss, lk, p, nm)) return '';   // same login as the site
  if (!p || !rows.some(function (r) { return String(r[1] == null ? '' : r[1]).trim() === p; })) return 'Wrong store or PIN';
  if (!rows.some(function (r) { return String(r[2] == null ? '' : r[2]).trim(); })) return 'No storekeeper names are set for this store yet. Please ask the office.';
  if (!nm) return 'Choose your name from the list first';
  if (!rows.some(function (r) { return String(r[1] == null ? '' : r[1]).trim() === p && String(r[2] == null ? '' : r[2]).trim() === nm; }))
    return 'This PIN is not for ' + nm + '. Type your own PIN.';
  return '';
}
/** Who may look: the office (ALL SITES PIN, view only) or a storekeeper of an excess store. */
function xView_(ss, site, pin, name) {
  if (isAll_(site)) return okOffice_(ss, pin) ? { office: true } : { error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var e = xCheck_(ss, site, pin, name);
  return e ? { error: e } : { office: false, store: xFix_(ss, String(site).trim()) };
}

function xTime_(v) {
  if (v instanceof Date) return ('0' + v.getHours()).slice(-2) + ':' + ('0' + v.getMinutes()).slice(-2);
  return String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/^'/, '');
}
function xItems_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.xItems) return MEMO_.xItems;
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(XI_TAB_), n = sh ? sh.getLastRow() : 0, out = [];
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, XI_N_).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    if (!String(r[0]).trim()) return;
    var s = function (k) { return String(r[k] == null ? '' : r[k]).trim(); };
    out.push({ row: i + 2, code: s(0), cat: s(1), name: s(2), brand: s(3), model: s(4), size: s(5), colour: s(6), unit: s(7), cond: s(8),
      store: xFix_(ss, s(9)), loc: s(10), note: s(12), created: dstr_(r[13]), by: s(14), status: s(15) || 'OK', thumb: /^data:image\//.test(s(16)) ? s(16) : '' });
  });
  return (MEMO_.xItems = out);
}
function xMoves_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.xMoves) return MEMO_.xMoves;
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(XM_TAB_), n = sh ? sh.getLastRow() : 0, out = [];
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, XM_N_).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    if (!String(r[0]).trim()) return;
    var s = function (k) { return String(r[k] == null ? '' : r[k]).trim(); };
    out.push({ row: i + 2, ref: s(0), date: dstr_(r[1]), time: xTime_(r[2]), code: s(3), item: s(4), move: s(5), qty: Number(r[6]) || 0, unit: s(7),
      from: xFix_(ss, s(8)), to: xFix_(ss, s(9)), noteNo: s(10), by2: s(11), rec: s(12), note: s(13), status: s(14) || 'OK' });
  });
  return (MEMO_.xMoves = out);
}
function xOk_(m) { return !/^Cancelled/.test(m.status); }
/** { code: { store: qty } } from the moves. A Count move holds the difference (+ or −). */
function xBal_(moves) {
  var b = {};
  moves.forEach(function (m) {
    if (!xOk_(m)) return;
    var o = b[m.code] || (b[m.code] = {}), q = m.qty;
    var add = function (st, d) { if (st) o[st] = r3_((o[st] || 0) + d); };
    if (m.move === 'Received') add(m.to, q);
    else if (m.move === 'Issued' || m.move === 'Used' || m.move === 'Damaged') add(m.from, -q);
    else if (m.move === 'Moved') { add(m.from, -q); add(m.to, q); }
    else if (m.move === 'Count') add(m.from, q);
  });
  return b;
}
function xPhotoMap_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.xPh) return MEMO_.xPh;
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(XP_TAB_), n = sh ? sh.getLastRow() : 0, o = {};
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, 7).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
    var c = String(r[0]).trim(), id = String(r[3]).trim();
    if (!c || !id) return;
    (o[c] || (o[c] = [])).push({ no: Number(r[1]) || 0, what: String(r[2] || ''), id: id });
  });
  return (MEMO_.xPh = o);
}
function xSum_(o) { var t = 0; Object.keys(o || {}).forEach(function (k) { t += o[k]; }); return r3_(t); }
/** Everything the store page and the office tab need: all cards with quantities per store, where they came from and went to. */
function xState_(ss, full, only) {
  var items = xItems_(ss), moves = xMoves_(ss), bal = xBal_(moves), ph = xPhotoMap_(ss), src = {}, dst = {}, gone = {}, t = today_(), mon = 0;
  var put = function (o, code, k, q) { var x = o[code] || (o[code] = {}); x[k] = r3_((x[k] || 0) + q); };
  var mine = {};   // cards this store has held (a store sees only its own materials)
  if (only) moves.forEach(function (m) {
    var held = m.move === 'Received' ? m.to === only : m.move === 'Moved' ? (m.from === only || m.to === only) : m.from === only;
    if (held) mine[m.code] = 1;
  });
  moves.forEach(function (m) {
    if (!xOk_(m)) return;
    if (only && !mine[m.code]) return;
    if (m.date.slice(0, 7) === t.slice(0, 7)) mon++;
    if (m.move === 'Received') put(src, m.code, m.from, m.qty);
    else if (m.move === 'Issued') put(dst, m.code, m.to, m.qty);
    else if (m.move === 'Used' || m.move === 'Damaged') put(gone, m.code, m.move, m.qty);
  });
  var cats = X_CATS_.slice();
  var cards = items.filter(function (it) { return it.status !== 'Cancelled' && (!only || mine[it.code] || it.store === only); }).map(function (it) {
    if (it.cat && cats.indexOf(it.cat) < 0) cats.push(it.cat);
    var b = bal[it.code] || {};
    Object.keys(b).forEach(function (k) { if (!b[k]) delete b[k]; });
    return { code: it.code, cat: it.cat, name: it.name, brand: it.brand, model: it.model, size: it.size, colour: it.colour, unit: it.unit, cond: it.cond,
      store: it.store, loc: it.loc, note: it.note, created: it.created, by: it.by, th: !!it.thumb, thumb: full ? it.thumb : '', bal: b, total: xSum_(b),
      nph: (ph[it.code] || []).length, src: src[it.code] || {}, dst: dst[it.code] || {}, gone: gone[it.code] || {} };
  });
  cards.sort(function (a, b) { return a.code < b.code ? -1 : 1; });
  return { ok: true, today: t, stores: xStores_(ss), sites: sitesList_(ss), cats: cats, units: X_UNITS_, conds: X_CONDS_, cards: cards, month: mon };
}
/** Writes the worked-out quantity into “In store now” so the sheet is easy to read. */
function xSync_(ss, code) {
  MEMO_.xMoves = null; MEMO_.xItems = null;
  var it = xItems_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.code === code; })[0];
  if (!it) return;
  var b = xBal_(xMoves_(ss))[code] || {}, txt = Object.keys(b).filter(function (k) { return b[k]; }).map(function (k) { return k + ': ' + b[k]; }).join(' · ');
  xiSheet_(ss).getRange(it.row, 12).setValue(xSum_(b) + ' ' + it.unit + (txt ? ' (' + txt + ')' : ''));
}
function xRef_(sh, pre) { return pre + ('000' + (maxRef_(sh) + 1)).slice(-4); }
function xNum_(x) { x = String(x == null ? '' : x).replace(/,/g, '').trim(); return x === '' ? NaN : Number(x); }
function xTxt_(x, n) { return String(x == null ? '' : x).replace(/[\r\n\t]+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, n || 120); }
function xDate_(d) { d = String(d || '').trim(); var t = today_(); return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d) && d <= t ? d : t; }
function xAddMove_(ss, m) {
  var sh = xmSheet_(ss), ref = xRef_(sh, 'EXM-'), now = new Date();
  room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + 1, XM_N_);
  sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, XM_N_).setValues([[ref, m.date, Utilities.formatDate(now, TZ_, 'HH:mm'), m.code, m.item, m.move, m.qty, m.unit,
    m.from || '', m.to || '', m.noteNo || '', m.by2 || '', m.rec || '', m.note || '', 'OK', '', '']]);
  MEMO_.xMoves = null;
  return ref;
}
/** Saves photos (data URIs from the phone) in Drive and lists them in Excess Photos. Returns how many were saved. */
function xSavePhotos_(ss, code, photos, by) {
  photos = (photos || []).filter(function (p) { return p && /^data:image\/\w+;base64,/.test(p.b64 || ''); });
  if (!photos.length) return 0;
  var have = (xPhotoMap_(ss)[code] || []).length, room = Math.max(0, X_MAXPH_ - have), n = 0;
  if (!room) return 0;
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Excess Photos'), folder = it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Excess Photos');
  var sh = xpSheet_(ss), rows = [];
  photos.slice(0, room).forEach(function (p) {
    try {
      var m = String(p.b64).match(/^data:(image\/\w+);base64,(.+)$/), no = have + rows.length + 1, what = xTxt_(p.what || 'Photo', 30);
      var f = folder.createFile(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(m[2]), m[1], code + ' ' + no + ' ' + what + '.jpg'));
      rows.push([code, no, what, f.getId(), f.getUrl(), today_(), by]);
    } catch (e) { console.error('Excess photo not saved: ' + e); }
  });
  if (rows.length) { room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + rows.length, 7); sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rows.length, 7).setValues(rows); n = rows.length; }
  MEMO_.xPh = null;
  return n;
}
function xCardFields_(c) {
  c = c || {};
  var f = { cat: xTxt_(c.cat, 40), name: xTxt_(c.name, 120), brand: xTxt_(c.brand, 60), model: xTxt_(c.model, 60), size: xTxt_(c.size, 40),
    colour: xTxt_(c.colour, 60), unit: xTxt_(c.unit, 20), cond: xTxt_(c.cond, 30), loc: xTxt_(c.loc, 60), note: xTxt_(c.note, 300) };
  if (!f.cat) return { error: 'Choose the category.' };
  if (!f.name) return { error: 'Type the name of the material.' };
  if (!f.unit) return { error: 'Choose the unit.' };
  if (X_CONDS_.indexOf(f.cond) < 0) return { error: 'Choose the condition.' };
  return f;
}

/* ---------------- functions the page calls ---------------- */
/** Excess store storekeeper: everything for the store page. */
function loadExcess(store, pin, name) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  var e = xCheck_(ss, store, pin, name);
  if (e) return { ok: false, error: e };
  var r = xState_(ss, false, xFix_(ss, store)); r.store = String(store).trim();
  return r;
}
/** Office (ALL SITES): all excess materials, view only. */
function loadExcessOffice(pin) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  return xState_(ss);
}
/** Small pictures for the gallery, a few at a time (codes = list of card codes). */
function xThumbs(site, pin, name, codes) {
  var ss = ss_(), v = xView_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (v.error) return { ok: false, error: v.error };
  var want = {}, out = {};
  (codes || []).slice(0, 120).forEach(function (c) { want[String(c)] = 1; });
  xItems_(ss).forEach(function (it) { if (want[it.code]) out[it.code] = it.thumb || ''; });
  return { ok: true, thumbs: out };
}
function xThumbOf_(ss, code) { var o = {}; xItems_(ss).forEach(function (it) { if (it.code === code) o[code] = it.thumb || ''; }); return o; }
/** One card: its photos (list) and full history. */
/** Issued materials between two dates (store: only its own issues; office: both stores), newest first. */
function xIssued(site, pin, name, from, to) {
  var ss = ss_(), v = xView_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (v.error) return { ok: false, error: v.error };
  var t = today_();
  from = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(from || '') ? from : t.slice(0, 8) + '01';
  to = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(to || '') ? to : t;
  if (from > to) { var x = from; from = to; to = x; }
  var it = {}, sites = {};
  xItems_(ss).forEach(function (i) { it[i.code] = i; });
  var list = xMoves_(ss).filter(function (m) { return xOk_(m) && m.move === 'Issued' && m.date >= from && m.date <= to && (v.office || m.from === v.store); })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return a.date === b.date ? (a.ref < b.ref ? 1 : -1) : (a.date < b.date ? 1 : -1); })
    .map(function (m) { var i = it[m.code] || {}; sites[m.to] = 1;
      return { ref: m.ref, date: m.date, time: m.time, code: m.code, item: m.item, cat: i.cat || '', size: [i.size, i.colour].filter(String).join(' · '), qty: m.qty, unit: m.unit,
        from: m.from, to: m.to, noteNo: m.noteNo, by2: m.by2, rec: m.rec, note: m.note }; });
  return { ok: true, office: !!v.office, from: from, to: to, list: list, sites: Object.keys(sites).sort() };
}
function xCard(site, pin, name, code) {
  var ss = ss_(), v = xView_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (v.error) return { ok: false, error: v.error };
  code = String(code || '').trim();
  var moves = xMoves_(ss).filter(function (m) { return m.code === code; }).reverse();
  return { ok: true, code: code, photos: (xPhotoMap_(ss)[code] || []).map(function (p) { return { no: p.no, what: p.what, id: p.id }; }), moves: moves };
}
/** One photo from Drive (only files listed in Excess Photos). */
function xPhoto(site, pin, name, id) {
  var ss = ss_(), v = xView_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (v.error) return { ok: false, error: v.error };
  var ok = false, m = xPhotoMap_(ss);
  Object.keys(m).forEach(function (c) { m[c].forEach(function (p) { if (p.id === id) ok = true; }); });
  if (!ok) return { ok: false, error: 'Photo not found' };
  try {
    var b = DriveApp.getFileById(id).getBlob();
    return { ok: true, id: id, src: 'data:' + (b.getContentType() || 'image/jpeg') + ';base64,' + Utilities.base64Encode(b.getBytes()) };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: 'Could not open the photo: ' + e }; }
}
/**
 * Material came back from a site. d = { from, date, noteNo, by2, qty, code (add to an existing card) | card {…new card…}, photos [{b64, what}], thumb }
 */
function xReceive(store, pin, name, d) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  var e = xCheck_(ss, store, pin, name);
  if (e) return { ok: false, error: e };
  store = String(store).trim(); name = String(name).trim(); d = d || {};
  var qty = xNum_(d.qty), from = xTxt_(d.from, 80), date = xDate_(d.date);
  if (!from) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose the site it came from.' };
  if (!(qty > 0)) return { ok: false, error: 'Type how many came back.' };
  var f = null;
  if (!d.code) { f = xCardFields_(d.card); if (f.error) return { ok: false, error: f.error }; }
  var res = withLock_(function () {
    MEMO_.xItems = null; MEMO_.xMoves = null;
    var code = String(d.code || '').trim(), it;
    if (code) {
      it = xItems_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.code === code && x.status !== 'Cancelled'; })[0];
      if (!it) return { ok: false, error: 'Card ' + code + ' was not found.' };
      if (!it.thumb && /^data:image\/\w+;base64,/.test(d.thumb || '') && String(d.thumb).length < 45000) xiSheet_(ss).getRange(it.row, 17).setValue(String(d.thumb));
    } else {
      var sh = xiSheet_(ss); code = xRef_(sh, 'EX-');
      var thumb = /^data:image\/\w+;base64,/.test(d.thumb || '') && String(d.thumb).length < 45000 ? String(d.thumb) : '';
      room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + 1, XI_N_);
      sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, XI_N_).setValues([[code, f.cat, f.name, f.brand, f.model, f.size, f.colour, f.unit, f.cond, store, f.loc,
        '', f.note, date, name, 'OK', thumb]]);
      MEMO_.xItems = null;
      it = { code: code, name: f.name, unit: f.unit };
    }
    var ref = xAddMove_(ss, { date: date, code: code, item: it.name, move: 'Received', qty: qty, unit: it.unit, from: from, to: store,
      noteNo: xTxt_(d.noteNo, 40), by2: xTxt_(d.by2, 60), rec: name });
    return { ok: true, code: code, ref: ref, isNew: !d.code, item: it.name, unit: it.unit };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return res;
  try { res.photos = xSavePhotos_(ss, res.code, d.photos, name); } catch (x) { console.error(x); res.photos = 0; }
  xSync_(ss, res.code);
  res.thumbs = xThumbOf_(ss, res.code);
  res.msg = (res.isNew ? 'New card ' : 'Added to ') + res.code + ' · ' + qty + ' ' + res.unit + ' ' + res.item + ' from ' + from + ' (' + res.ref + ')';
  res.state = xState_(ss, false, xFix_(ss, store)); res.state.store = store;
  return res;
}
/**
 * Issue to a site / move to the other store / used at store / damaged / count check.
 * d = { type: issue|move|used|damaged|count, qty (count: the quantity counted now), to, noteNo, by2, note, date, photo }
 */
function xMove(store, pin, name, code, d) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  var e = xCheck_(ss, store, pin, name);
  if (e) return { ok: false, error: e };
  store = String(store).trim(); name = String(name).trim(); code = String(code || '').trim(); d = d || {};
  var T = { issue: 'Issued', move: 'Moved', used: 'Used', damaged: 'Damaged', count: 'Count' }[d.type];
  if (!T) return { ok: false, error: 'Unknown action' };
  var qty = xNum_(d.qty), to = xTxt_(d.to, 80), note = xTxt_(d.note, 200), date = xDate_(d.date);
  if (T === 'Count' ? !(qty >= 0) : !(qty > 0)) return { ok: false, error: T === 'Count' ? 'Type the quantity you counted.' : 'Type how many.' };
  if (T === 'Issued' && sitesList_(ss).indexOf(to) < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose the site.' };
  if (T === 'Moved' && (xStores_(ss).indexOf(to) < 0 || to === store)) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose the other store.' };
  if ((T === 'Used' || T === 'Damaged' || T === 'Count') && !note) return { ok: false, error: T === 'Used' ? 'Type what it was used for.' : 'Type the reason.' };
  var res = withLock_(function () {
    MEMO_.xItems = null; MEMO_.xMoves = null;
    var it = xItems_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.code === code && x.status !== 'Cancelled'; })[0];
    if (!it) return { ok: false, error: 'Card ' + code + ' was not found.' };
    var have = (xBal_(xMoves_(ss))[code] || {})[store] || 0, q = qty;
    if (T === 'Count') { q = r3_(qty - have); if (!q) return { ok: false, error: 'The count is the same as now (' + have + ' ' + it.unit + '). Nothing to change.' }; }
    else if (qty > have) return { ok: false, error: 'Only ' + have + ' ' + it.unit + ' of ' + code + ' in ' + store + '.' };
    var ref = xAddMove_(ss, { date: date, code: code, item: it.name, move: T, qty: q, unit: it.unit, from: store, to: T === 'Issued' || T === 'Moved' ? to : '',
      noteNo: xTxt_(d.noteNo, 40), by2: xTxt_(d.by2, 60), rec: name, note: note });
    return { ok: true, ref: ref, item: it.name, unit: it.unit, have: have, after: r3_(T === 'Count' ? qty : have - qty), q: q };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return res;
  if (d.photo) try { xSavePhotos_(ss, code, [{ b64: d.photo, what: T === 'Damaged' ? 'Damage' : T === 'Issued' ? 'Handover' : 'Photo' }], name); } catch (x) { console.error(x); }
  xSync_(ss, code);
  var say = { Issued: 'Issued to ' + to, Moved: 'Moved to ' + to, Used: 'Used at store', Damaged: 'Damaged / thrown', Count: 'Count corrected (' + (res.q > 0 ? '+' : '') + res.q + ')' }[T];
  res.msg = say + ' · ' + (T === 'Count' ? '' : qty + ' ' + res.unit + ' · ') + res.item + ' · ' + code + ' · ' + res.have + ' → ' + res.after + ' ' + res.unit + ' left in ' + store + ' (' + res.ref + ')';
  res.state = xState_(ss, false, xFix_(ss, store)); res.state.store = store;
  return res;
}
/** Change the details / place of a card and add photos. f = { cat, name, brand, model, size, colour, unit, cond, loc, note } */
function xEdit(store, pin, name, code, f, photos, thumb) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  var e = xCheck_(ss, store, pin, name);
  if (e) return { ok: false, error: e };
  code = String(code || '').trim();
  var c = xCardFields_(f);
  if (c.error) return { ok: false, error: c.error };
  var res = withLock_(function () {
    MEMO_.xItems = null;
    var it = xItems_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.code === code && x.status !== 'Cancelled'; })[0];
    if (!it) return { ok: false, error: 'Card ' + code + ' was not found.' };
    var sh = xiSheet_(ss);
    sh.getRange(it.row, 2, 1, 8).setValues([[c.cat, c.name, c.brand, c.model, c.size, c.colour, c.unit, c.cond]]);
    sh.getRange(it.row, 11).setValue(c.loc); sh.getRange(it.row, 13).setValue(c.note);
    if (!it.thumb && /^data:image\/\w+;base64,/.test(thumb || '') && String(thumb).length < 45000) sh.getRange(it.row, 17).setValue(thumb);
    MEMO_.xItems = null;
    return { ok: true };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return res;
  var n = 0;
  try { n = xSavePhotos_(ss, code, photos, String(name).trim()); } catch (x) { console.error(x); }
  xSync_(ss, code);
  res.thumbs = xThumbOf_(ss, code);
  res.msg = code + ' · ' + c.name + ' – details saved' + (n ? ' · ' + n + ' photo' + (n == 1 ? '' : 's') + ' added' : '');
  res.state = xState_(ss, false, xFix_(ss, store)); res.state.store = String(store).trim();
  return res;
}
/**
 * Sheet menu: after photo files named like “EX-0012 1 Item.jpg” are uploaded to the Drive folder “WTC Excess Photos”
 * (or a folder inside it), this lists them in Excess Photos so they show on the material cards. Safe to run again.
 */
function linkExcessPhotos() {
  var ss = ss_(), ui = null;
  try { ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); } catch (e) {}
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Excess Photos');
  if (!it.hasNext()) { if (ui) ui.alert('There is no Google Drive folder called “WTC Excess Photos” yet. Upload the photos folder first.'); return; }
  var have = {}, codes = {}, sh = xpSheet_(ss), rows = [], seen = 0;
  Object.keys(xPhotoMap_(ss)).forEach(function (c) { xPhotoMap_(ss)[c].forEach(function (p) { have[p.id] = 1; have[c + '|' + p.no] = 1; }); });
  xItems_(ss).forEach(function (x) { codes[x.code] = 1; });
  var walk = function (f, depth) {
    var files = f.getFiles();
    while (files.hasNext()) {
      var file = files.next(), m = file.getName().match(/^(EX-\d{4,})\s+(\d+)\s*(.*?)\.(jpe?g|png|webp)$/i);
      if (!m) continue;
      seen++;
      if (have[file.getId()] || have[m[1] + '|' + m[2]] || !codes[m[1]]) continue;
      have[m[1] + '|' + m[2]] = 1;
      rows.push([m[1], Number(m[2]), m[3] || 'Photo', file.getId(), file.getUrl(), today_(), 'Upload']);
    }
    if (depth < 3) { var subs = f.getFolders(); while (subs.hasNext()) walk(subs.next(), depth + 1); }
  };
  while (it.hasNext()) walk(it.next(), 0);
  if (rows.length) { room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + rows.length, 7); sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rows.length, 7).setValues(rows); }
  MEMO_.xPh = null;
  var msg = 'Photo files found: ' + seen + '. New photos linked to material cards: ' + rows.length + '.';
  if (ui) ui.alert(msg); return msg;
}
/** Office cancel (WTC Stock > Cancel a transaction) of an EXM move. */
function xCancel_(ss, ref, dry) {
  var m = xMoves_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.ref === ref; })[0];
  if (!m) return { ok: false, error: 'No entry ' + ref + ' was found.' };
  if (!xOk_(m)) return { ok: false, error: ref + ' is already cancelled.' };
  var rest = xMoves_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.ref !== ref; }), b = xBal_(rest)[m.code] || {};
  var neg = Object.keys(b).filter(function (k) { return b[k] < 0; });
  if (neg.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Cannot cancel ' + ref + ': ' + neg.map(function (k) { return k + ' would have ' + b[k] + ' ' + m.unit; }).join(', ') +
    ' of ' + m.code + '. Cancel the later moves of this material first.' };
  var desc = 'Excess ' + m.move + ' ' + ref + ' · ' + m.code + ' ' + m.item + ' · ' + m.qty + ' ' + m.unit + ' · ' + m.date + (m.from ? ' · from ' + m.from : '') + (m.to ? ' · to ' + m.to : '') + ' · by ' + m.rec;
  if (dry) return { ok: true, summary: desc + '\n\nAfter cancelling: ' + (Object.keys(b).map(function (k) { return k + ' ' + b[k]; }).join(' · ') || '0') + ' ' + m.unit + ' of ' + m.code + '.' };
  xmSheet_(ss).getRange(m.row, 15, 1, 3).setValues([['Cancelled by office', 'Office', new Date()]]);
  xSync_(ss, m.code);
  return { ok: true };
}
/** Site page: excess materials issued to this site in the last 60 days (read only). */
function xIn_(ss, site) {
  if (!ss.getSheetByName(XM_TAB_)) return [];
  var from = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(Date.now() - 60 * 864e5), TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd'), th = {};
  var ms = xMoves_(ss).filter(function (m) { return xOk_(m) && m.move === 'Issued' && m.to === site && m.date >= from; });
  if (!ms.length) return [];
  xItems_(ss).forEach(function (it) { th[it.code] = it; });
  return ms.reverse().map(function (m) {
    var it = th[m.code] || {};
    return { ref: m.ref, date: m.date, code: m.code, item: m.item, size: [it.size, it.colour].filter(String).join(' · '), cond: it.cond || '', qty: m.qty, unit: m.unit,
      from: m.from, noteNo: m.noteNo, by2: m.by2, thumb: it.thumb || '' };
  });
}
/**
 * PDFs: kind = 'stock' (list with photos), 'moves' (in / out between dates), 'card' (one card with photos and history), 'label' (label to stick on the box).
 * opt = { store, from, to, code }. The office and the excess store storekeepers can make them.
 */
function makeExcessPdf(site, pin, name, kind, opt) {
  var ss = ss_(), v = xView_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (v.error) return { ok: false, error: v.error };
  opt = opt || {};
  var S = xState_(ss, true, v.office ? '' : v.store), stores = v.office ? xStores_(ss) : [v.store], t = today_(), st = v.office ? String(opt.store || '').trim() : v.store;
  var MN = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
  var f = function (d) { return d ? +d.slice(8, 10) + ' ' + MN[+d.slice(5, 7) - 1] + ' ' + d.slice(0, 4) : ''; };
  var n = function (x) { return Number(x).toLocaleString('en-US', { maximumFractionDigits: 3 }); };
  var lst = function (o) { return Object.keys(o || {}).map(function (k) { return esc_(k) + ' ' + n(o[k]); }).join(' · ') || '–'; };
  var det = function (c) { return [c.brand, c.model, c.size, c.colour].filter(String).map(esc_).join(' · '); };
  var TH = {}; xItems_(ss).forEach(function (it) { if (/^data:image\//.test(it.thumb || '')) TH[it.code] = it.thumb; });
  var ti = function (code, sz) { return TH[code] ? '<img src="' + TH[code] + '" style="width:' + sz + 'px;height:' + sz + 'px;border-radius:3px">' : ''; };
  var css = '@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:12mm}*{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:9pt;color:#1E2329}' +
    'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-bottom:6px}thead{display:table-header-group}tr{break-inside:avoid;page-break-inside:avoid}th{background:' + X_COL_ + ';color:#fff;padding:5px 6px;text-align:left}td{border:1px solid #D5D5D5;padding:4px 6px;vertical-align:middle}tr.z td{background:#F6F4FA}td.n,th.n{text-align:right;white-space:nowrap}td.n{font-weight:bold}' +
    '.hd td{border:0;padding:0 6px 0 0;vertical-align:middle}.note{font-size:7.5pt;color:#666}img.t{width:64px;height:64px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:4px}h2{font-size:12pt;margin:14px 0 4px}';
  var head = function (title, sub) {
    return '<table class="hd"><tr><td style="width:62px"><img src="' + LOGO_ + '" style="width:50px;height:50px"></td><td><div style="font-size:17pt;font-weight:bold">' + esc_(title) + '</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:10pt;font-weight:bold;color:#C42D4E">WALK THROUGH GENERAL CONTRACTING - SOLE PROPRIETORSHIP LLC</div><div style="color:#555">' + esc_(sub) + '</div></td></tr></table>';
  };
  var html = '', fname = '', count = 0, by = v.office ? 'Office' : String(name || '').trim();
  if (kind === 'stock') {
    var cs = S.cards.filter(function (c) { return st ? (c.bal[st] || 0) > 0 : c.total > 0; });
    if (opt.cat) cs = cs.filter(function (c) { return c.cat === opt.cat; });
    cs.sort(function (a, b) { return (a.cat + a.name).localeCompare(b.cat + b.name); });
    if (!cs.length) return { ok: false, error: 'No excess materials in stock' + (st ? ' in ' + st : '') + '.' };
    var noPh = cs.length > 2000;
    if (noPh) cs.forEach(function (c) { c.thumb = ''; });
    html = head('EXCESS MATERIALS · STOCK LIST WITH PHOTOS', (st || stores.join(' + ')) + (opt.cat ? ' · ' + opt.cat : ' · all categories') + ' · as on ' + f(t) + ' · ' + cs.length + ' materials') +
      '<table><thead><tr><th>Photo</th><th>Code</th><th>Category</th><th>Material</th><th>Condition</th>' + (st ? '<th class="n">In store</th>' : stores.map(function (s) { return '<th class="n">' + esc_(s) + '</th>'; }).join('')) +
      '<th>Kept at</th><th>Came from</th><th>Issued to</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
      cs.map(function (c, i) {
        return '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '><td style="width:70px">' + (c.thumb || TH[c.code] ? '<img class="t" src="' + (c.thumb || TH[c.code]) + '">' : '') + '</td><td><b>' + esc_(c.code) + '</b></td><td>' + esc_(c.cat) + '</td><td><b>' + esc_(c.name) + '</b><br><span style="color:#666">' + det(c) + '</span></td><td>' + esc_(c.cond) + '</td>' +
          (st ? '<td class="n">' + n(c.bal[st] || 0) + ' ' + esc_(c.unit) + '</td>' : stores.map(function (s) { return '<td class="n">' + (c.bal[s] ? n(c.bal[s]) + ' ' + esc_(c.unit) : '–') + '</td>'; }).join('')) +
          '<td>' + esc_(c.loc) + '</td><td>' + lst(c.src) + '</td><td>' + lst(c.dst) + '</td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table>' + (noPh ? '<p class="note">More than 2000 materials – photos are left out to keep the PDF small. Choose a category to get the photos.</p>' : '');
    fname = 'Excess_stock_' + t + (st ? '_' + st.replace(/\W+/g, '_') : '') + '.pdf'; count = cs.length;
  } else if (kind === 'moves') {
    var from = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(opt.from || '') ? opt.from : t.slice(0, 8) + '01', to = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(opt.to || '') ? opt.to : t;
    var ms = xMoves_(ss).filter(function (m) { return m.date >= from && m.date <= to && (!st || m.from === st || m.to === st); });
    if (!ms.length) return { ok: false, error: 'No excess moves between ' + f(from) + ' and ' + f(to) + (st ? ' for ' + st : '') + '.' };
    var tot = {};
    ms.forEach(function (m) { if (xOk_(m)) tot[m.move] = (tot[m.move] || 0) + 1; });
    html = head('EXCESS MATERIALS · IN AND OUT', (st || stores.join(' + ')) + ' · ' + f(from) + ' – ' + f(to) + ' · ' + Object.keys(tot).map(function (k) { return tot[k] + ' ' + k.toLowerCase(); }).join(' · ')) +
      '<table><thead><tr>' + ['Photo', 'Ref', 'Date', 'Code', 'Material', 'Move', 'Qty', 'From', 'To', 'Note no.', 'Taken / brought by', 'Recorded by', 'Note / status'].map(function (h, i) { return '<th' + (i === 6 ? ' class="n"' : '') + '>' + h + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' +
      ms.map(function (m, i) {
        var x = !xOk_(m);
        return '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + (x ? ' style="color:#999;text-decoration:line-through"' : '') + '><td style="width:46px">' + ti(m.code, 40) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.ref) + '</td><td style="white-space:nowrap">' + f(m.date) + ' ' + esc_(m.time) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.code) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.item) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.move) + '</td><td class="n">' + n(m.qty) + ' ' + esc_(m.unit) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.from) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.to) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.noteNo) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.by2) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.rec) + '</td><td>' + esc_([m.note, x ? m.status : ''].filter(String).join(' · ')) + '</td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table>';
    fname = 'Excess_moves_' + from + '_to_' + to + '.pdf'; count = ms.length;
  } else if (kind === 'issued') {
    var I = xIssued(site, pin, name, opt.from, opt.to), site2 = String(opt.site || '').trim();
    var il = (I.list || []).filter(function (m) { return (!st || m.from === st) && (!site2 || m.to === site2); }).reverse();
    if (!il.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Nothing was issued' + (site2 ? ' to ' + site2 : '') + ' between ' + f(I.from) + ' and ' + f(I.to) + '.' };
    var bySite = {}, byDate = {}, dates = [], two = !st && stores.length > 1;
    il.forEach(function (m) { var o = bySite[m.to] || (bySite[m.to] = { n: 0, codes: {}, days: {} }); o.n++; o.codes[m.code] = 1; o.days[m.date] = 1;
      if (!byDate[m.date]) { byDate[m.date] = []; dates.push(m.date); } byDate[m.date].push(m); });
    var cols = ['Photo', 'Ref', 'Code', 'Material', 'Qty'].concat(two ? ['From store'] : []).concat(['To site', 'MR / request no.', 'Taken by', 'Recorded by']);
    html = head('EXCESS MATERIALS · ISSUED', (st || stores.join(' + ')) + ' · ' + f(I.from) + ' – ' + f(I.to) + (site2 ? ' · to ' + site2 : ' · all sites') + ' · ' + il.length + ' issues') +
      '<h2>Totals by site</h2><table style="width:auto;min-width:50%"><thead><tr><th>Site</th><th class="n">Issues</th><th class="n">Materials</th><th class="n">Days</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
      Object.keys(bySite).sort().map(function (k, i) { var o = bySite[k]; return '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '><td><b>' + esc_(k) + '</b></td><td class="n">' + o.n + '</td><td class="n">' + Object.keys(o.codes).length + '</td><td class="n">' + Object.keys(o.days).length + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') +
      '</tbody></table><h2>Day by day</h2><table><thead><tr>' + cols.map(function (h) { return '<th' + (h === 'Qty' ? ' class="n"' : '') + '>' + h + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' +
      dates.map(function (dt) {
        return '<tr><td colspan="' + cols.length + '" style="background:#EFE9F8;font-weight:bold">' + f(dt) + ' · ' + byDate[dt].length + ' issue' + (byDate[dt].length == 1 ? '' : 's') + '</td></tr>' +
          byDate[dt].map(function (m) { return '<tr><td style="width:52px">' + ti(m.code, 46) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.ref) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.code) + '</td><td><b>' + esc_(m.item) + '</b>' + (m.size ? '<br><span style="color:#666">' + esc_(m.size) + '</span>' : '') + '</td><td class="n">' + n(m.qty) + ' ' + esc_(m.unit) + '</td>' +
            (two ? '<td>' + esc_(m.from) + '</td>' : '') + '<td>' + esc_(m.to) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.noteNo) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.by2) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.rec) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table>';
    fname = 'Excess_issued_' + I.from + '_to_' + I.to + (site2 ? '_' + site2.replace(/\W+/g, '_') : '') + '.pdf'; count = il.length;
  } else if (kind === 'card' || kind === 'label') {
    var c = S.cards.filter(function (x) { return x.code === String(opt.code || '').trim(); })[0];
    if (!c) return { ok: false, error: 'Card not found.' };
    if (kind === 'label') {
      var one = '<div style="border:2px dashed #888;border-radius:10px;padding:14px 18px;margin:0 0 14mm;width:150mm;page-break-inside:avoid">' +
        '<div style="font-size:40pt;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:2px">' + esc_(c.code) + '</div><div style="font-size:15pt;font-weight:bold">' + esc_(c.name) + '</div>' +
        '<div style="font-size:12pt">' + det(c) + '</div><div style="font-size:12pt;margin-top:4px"><b>' + esc_(c.cond.toUpperCase()) + '</b> · unit: ' + esc_(c.unit) + (c.loc ? ' · kept at ' + esc_(c.loc) : '') + '</div>' +
        '<div style="font-size:10pt;color:#555;margin-top:4px">From ' + lst(c.src) + ' · ' + f(c.created) + ' · Walk Through General Contracting – excess materials</div></div>';
      html = '<style>@page{size:A4 portrait;margin:14mm}body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;color:#111}</style>' + one + one;
      fname = 'Label_' + c.code + '.pdf'; count = 1;
    } else {
      var ph = (xPhotoMap_(ss)[c.code] || []).slice(0, 3), pics = '';
      ph.forEach(function (p) { try { var b = DriveApp.getFileById(p.id).getBlob(); pics += '<img src="data:image/jpeg;base64,' + Utilities.base64Encode(b.getBytes()) + '" style="height:150px;max-width:260px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:6px;margin-right:8px">'; } catch (e) {} });
      if (!pics && c.thumb) pics = '<img src="' + c.thumb + '" style="height:120px;border-radius:6px">';
      var ms2 = xMoves_(ss).filter(function (m) { return m.code === c.code; });
      html = head('MATERIAL CARD ' + c.code, c.cat + ' · ' + c.name) + '<div style="margin:6px 0 10px">' + pics + '</div>' +
        '<table style="width:auto;min-width:60%"><tbody>' + [['Material', esc_(c.name)], ['Brand · model', esc_([c.brand, c.model].filter(String).join(' · ') || '–')], ['Size · colour', esc_([c.size, c.colour].filter(String).join(' · ') || '–')],
          ['Condition', esc_(c.cond)], ['In store now', stores.map(function (s) { return esc_(s) + ' <b>' + n(c.bal[s] || 0) + ' ' + esc_(c.unit) + '</b>'; }).join(' · ')], ['Kept at', esc_(c.loc || '–')],
          ['Came from', lst(c.src)], ['Issued to', lst(c.dst)], ['Used · damaged', lst(c.gone)], ['Note', esc_(c.note || '–')], ['Card made', f(c.created) + ' by ' + esc_(c.by)]].map(function (r) { return '<tr><td style="color:#666;width:120px">' + r[0] + '</td><td>' + r[1] + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</tbody></table>' +
        '<h2>History</h2><table><thead><tr>' + ['Ref', 'Date', 'Move', 'Qty', 'From', 'To', 'Note no.', 'Taken / brought by', 'Recorded by', 'Note / status'].map(function (h, i) { return '<th' + (i === 3 ? ' class="n"' : '') + '>' + h + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' +
        ms2.map(function (m, i) { var x = !xOk_(m); return '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + (x ? ' style="color:#999;text-decoration:line-through"' : '') + '><td>' + esc_(m.ref) + '</td><td>' + f(m.date) + ' ' + esc_(m.time) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.move) + '</td><td class="n">' + n(m.qty) + ' ' + esc_(m.unit) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.from) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.to) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.noteNo) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.by2) + '</td><td>' + esc_(m.rec) + '</td><td>' + esc_([m.note, x ? m.status : ''].filter(String).join(' · ')) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</tbody></table>';
      fname = 'Card_' + c.code + '.pdf'; count = 1;
    }
  } else return { ok: false, error: 'Unknown report' };
  if (kind !== 'label') html = '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><style>' + css + '</style></head><body>' + html + '<p class="note">Generated ' + esc_(Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm')) + ' by ' + esc_(by) + '.</p></body></html>';
  else html = '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"></head><body>' + html + '</body></html>';
  try {
    var pdf = Utilities.newBlob(html, 'text/html', 'excess.html').getAs('application/pdf').setName(fname), url = '';
    try { var it2 = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Stock PDFs'), folder = it2.hasNext() ? it2.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Stock PDFs'); url = folder.createFile(pdf).getUrl(); } catch (e2) { console.error('PDF not saved to Drive: ' + e2); }
    return { ok: true, name: pdf.getName(), url: url, b64: Utilities.base64Encode(pdf.getBytes()), count: count };
  } catch (e) {
    var msg = String(e && e.message || e);
    return { ok: false, error: /authoriz|permission/i.test(msg) ? 'Google needs your permission first: open the Google Sheet and click WTC Stock > Make a test PDF, then allow access.' : 'Could not create the PDF: ' + msg };
  }
}

/* ======================= Notices & tasks · weekly reminders · assistant · dashboard · other site items =======================
 * Tabs (made by themselves): Notices, Notice Replies, Assistant Log, Site Items, Site Item Moves, Dashboard.
 * A daily timer (WTC Stock › Turn on daily reminders & assistant) runs wtcDaily() at 08:00:
 *   Monday – weekly-check task to every site · Wednesday/Thursday – reminder to sites not done · every day – the assistant.
 */
var NT_TAB_ = 'Notices', NR_TAB_ = 'Notice Replies', AL_TAB_ = 'Assistant Log', SI_TAB_ = 'Site Items', SM_TAB_ = 'Site Item Moves';
var NT_HEAD_ = ['ID', 'Created', 'From', 'Type', 'Title', 'Details', 'Send to', 'Due', 'Priority', 'Attachment', 'Source', 'Status', 'Key', 'Closed at'];
var NR_HEAD_ = ['ID', 'Site', 'Seen at', 'Seen by', 'Acknowledged at', 'Acknowledged by', 'Done at', 'Done by', 'Note', 'Photo'];
var SI_HEAD_ = ['Code', 'Site', 'Category', 'Name', 'Size / colour', 'Unit', 'Condition', 'Whose', 'Where on site', 'Qty now', 'Note', 'Photo IDs',
  'Created', 'Created by', 'Last checked', 'Status', 'Thumb'];
var SM_HEAD_ = ['Ref', 'Date', 'Time', 'Code', 'Site', 'Item', 'Move', 'Qty', 'Unit', 'Qty after', 'To', 'Note', 'By', 'Status', 'Cancelled by', 'Cancelled at'];
var N_COL_ = '#E07A00', AS_COL_ = '#00897B', SI_COL_ = '#2E7D32';
var AS_RULES_ = { weekly: true, signin: true, overdue: true, rental: true, damaged: true, email: true };
var HELPER_TABS_ = ['Stock', 'Changes', 'Changes - weekly history', 'Idle Log', 'Repair Log', 'Logins', 'Login Summary', 'Images', 'Excess Photos',
  'Notice Replies', 'Assistant Log', 'Site Item Moves', 'Rental Types', 'Rental Companies'];

function nTab_(ss, name, head, textCols, col, note) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  if (sh) return sh;
  try { sh = ss.insertSheet(name); } catch (e) { sh = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (sh) return sh; throw e; }
  (textCols || []).forEach(function (c) { sh.getRange(c + ':' + c).setNumberFormat('@'); });
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setValues([head]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground(col).setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
  if (note) try { sh.getRange(1, 1).setNote(note); } catch (e) {}
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  try { sh.setTabColor(col); } catch (e) {}
  return sh;
}
function ntSheet_(ss) { return nTab_(ss, NT_TAB_, NT_HEAD_, ['A', 'H', 'M'], N_COL_, 'Announcements and tasks for the storekeepers. Made from the page (Office › Notices) or by the assistant.'); }
function nrSheet_(ss) { return nTab_(ss, NR_TAB_, NR_HEAD_, ['A'], N_COL_, 'Who has seen, acknowledged and finished each notice.'); }
function alSheet_(ss) { return nTab_(ss, AL_TAB_, ['Run', 'Site', 'Kind', 'Finding', 'Action'], [], AS_COL_, 'What the assistant found each time it ran.'); }
function siSheet_(ss) { return nTab_(ss, SI_TAB_, SI_HEAD_, ['A', 'E', 'I', 'L', 'M', 'O'], SI_COL_, 'Items that are at a site but not in the main item list. Added by the storekeepers with photos.'); }
function smSheet_(ss) { return nTab_(ss, SM_TAB_, SM_HEAD_, ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'], SI_COL_, 'Every change of the other site items. A wrong one is cancelled by the office: WTC Stock › Cancel a transaction, with its SIM number.'); }
function nTxt_(x, n) { return String(x == null ? '' : x).replace(/\r/g, '').trim().slice(0, n || 200); }
function nIso_(x) { var d = new Date(x); return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? String(x || '').slice(0, 16) : Utilities.formatDate(d, TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm'); }
function nNow_() { return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm'); }
function nDow_(ds) { var p = String(ds).split('-'); return new Date(Date.UTC(+p[0], +p[1] - 1, +p[2])).getUTCDay(); }  // 0 Sun … 6 Sat
function nAddDays_(ds, n) { var p = String(ds).split('-'), d = new Date(Date.UTC(+p[0], +p[1] - 1, +p[2])); d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + n); return fmtUTC_(d); }
/** Who is asking: the office (ALL SITES PIN), an excess store storekeeper, or a site storekeeper. */
function nWho_(ss, site, pin, name) {
  site = String(site || '').trim(); name = String(name || '').trim();
  if (isAll_(site)) return okOffice_(ss, pin) ? { office: true, name: 'Office' } : { error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  if (!siteNames_(ss)[site] && xIs_(ss, site)) { var e = xCheck_(ss, site, pin, name); return e ? { error: e } : { site: xFix_(ss, site), store: true, name: name }; }
  if (!checkPin_(ss, site, pin, name)) return { error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
  return { site: site, store: false, name: name };
}
function nTargets_(ss) { var x = []; try { x = xStores_(ss); } catch (e) {} return { sites: sitesList_(ss), stores: x }; }
/** “Send to” is stored as text: ALL · SITES · STORES · or names joined with “ | ”. */
function nRecipients_(ss, to) {
  var T = nTargets_(ss), t = String(to || '').trim();
  if (t === 'ALL') return T.sites.concat(T.stores);
  if (t === 'SITES') return T.sites.slice();
  if (t === 'STORES') return T.stores.slice();
  return t.split('|').map(function (s) { return s.trim(); }).filter(String);
}
function nToText_(to) { var t = String(to || ''); return t === 'ALL' ? 'All storekeepers' : t === 'SITES' ? 'All sites' : t === 'STORES' ? 'Excess stores' : t.split('|').map(function (s) { return s.trim(); }).join(', '); }

/* ---------------- notices ---------------- */
function ntList_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.nt) return MEMO_.nt;
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(NT_TAB_), n = sh ? sh.getLastRow() : 0, out = [];
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, NT_HEAD_.length).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    if (!String(r[0]).trim()) return;
    var s = function (k) { return String(r[k] == null ? '' : r[k]).trim(); };
    out.push({ row: i + 2, id: s(0), created: r[1] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(r[1], TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm') : s(1), from: s(2), type: s(3) || 'Announcement',
      title: s(4), details: s(5), to: s(6), due: dstr_(r[7]), priority: s(8) || 'Normal', attach: s(9), source: s(10) || 'Office', status: s(11) || 'Open', key: s(12) });
  });
  return (MEMO_.nt = out);
}
function nrMap_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.nr) return MEMO_.nr;
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(NR_TAB_), n = sh ? sh.getLastRow() : 0, m = {};
  var t = function (v) { return v instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(v, TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm') : String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); };
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, NR_HEAD_.length).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    var id = String(r[0]).trim(), st = String(r[1]).trim();
    if (!id) return;
    (m[id] || (m[id] = {}))[st] = { row: i + 2, seen: t(r[2]), seenBy: t(r[3]), ack: t(r[4]), ackBy: t(r[5]), done: t(r[6]), doneBy: t(r[7]), note: t(r[8]), photo: t(r[9]) };
  });
  return (MEMO_.nr = m);
}
/** Adds a notice. o = { type, title, details, to, due, priority, attach, source, from, key }. Returns the ID, or '' when the key already exists. */
function ntAdd_(ss, o) {
  if (o.key && ntList_(ss).some(function (n) { return n.key === o.key; })) return '';
  var sh = ntSheet_(ss), id = 'NT-' + ('000' + (maxRef_(sh) + 1)).slice(-4);
  room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + 1, NT_HEAD_.length);
  sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, NT_HEAD_.length).setValues([[id, nNow_(), o.from || 'Office', o.type || 'Announcement', nTxt_(o.title, 150), nTxt_(o.details, 2000),
    o.to || 'ALL', o.due || '', o.priority || 'Normal', o.attach || '', o.source || 'Office', 'Open', o.key || '', '']]);
  MEMO_.nt = null;
  return id;
}
function nWeekDone_(ss) { if (!MEMO_.nwk) MEMO_.nwk = checks_(ss, mondayOf_(today_())); return MEMO_.nwk; }
/** State of one notice for one site: 'new' (needs a tap), 'progress' (task acknowledged, not done) or 'done'. */
function nState_(ss, n, site) {
  var r = (nrMap_(ss)[n.id] || {})[site] || {};
  if (n.source === 'Weekly' && n.type === 'Task' && n.key === 'WEEKLY|' + mondayOf_(today_()) && nWeekDone_(ss)[site]) return { st: 'done', r: r, auto: 'Weekly check confirmed ' + nIso_(nWeekDone_(ss)[site][0]) };
  if (n.type === 'Task') return { st: r.done ? 'done' : r.ack ? 'progress' : 'new', r: r };
  return { st: r.ack ? 'done' : 'new', r: r };
}
function nOut_(ss, n, site) {
  var s = nState_(ss, n, site), t = today_();
  return { id: n.id, created: n.created, from: n.from, type: n.type, title: n.title, details: n.details, due: n.due, priority: n.priority, attach: n.attach, source: n.source,
    st: s.st, seen: s.r.seen || '', ack: s.r.ack || '', ackBy: s.r.ackBy || '', done: s.r.done || '', doneBy: s.r.doneBy || '', note: s.r.note || '', photo: s.r.photo || '',
    auto: s.auto || '', late: !!(n.due && n.due < t && s.st !== 'done') };
}
function nForSite_(ss, site) {
  var t = today_(), from = nAddDays_(t, -45);
  return ntList_(ss).filter(function (n) { return n.status === 'Open' && nRecipients_(ss, n.to).indexOf(site) >= 0; })
    .map(function (n) { return nOut_(ss, n, site); })
    .filter(function (o) { return o.st !== 'done' || String(o.done || o.ack || o.created).slice(0, 10) >= from; })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return a.created < b.created ? 1 : -1; });
}
/** Storekeeper: my notices (site page and excess store page). */
function loadNotices(site, pin, name) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = nWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error) return { ok: false, error: w.error };
  if (w.office) return { ok: true, list: [], n: 0 };
  var l = nForSite_(ss, w.site);
  if (!w.store) { var seen = {}, more = 0; l.forEach(function (o) { seen[o.id] = 1; }); xLinkedOf_(ss, w.site).forEach(function (st) { nForSite_(ss, st).forEach(function (o) { if (!seen[o.id]) { seen[o.id] = 1; o.forStore = st; l.push(o); more++; } }); }); if (more) l.sort(function (a, b) { return a.created < b.created ? 1 : a.created > b.created ? -1 : 0; }); }
  return { ok: true, list: l, n: l.filter(function (o) { return o.st === 'new'; }).length, p: l.filter(function (o) { return o.st === 'progress'; }).length, today: today_() };
}
/** Storekeeper: act = seen · ack · done (note, photo for done). */
function nAct(site, pin, name, id, act, note, photo) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = nWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error || w.office) return { ok: false, error: w.error || 'Only storekeepers answer notices.' };
  var n = ntList_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0];
  var rcp = n ? nRecipients_(ss, n.to) : [], tgt = [w.site].concat(w.store ? [] : xLinkedOf_(ss, w.site)).filter(function (s) { return rcp.indexOf(s) >= 0; });
  if (!n || !tgt.length) return { ok: false, error: 'This notice was not found.' };
  if (act === 'done' && n.type !== 'Task') act = 'ack';
  var link = '';
  if (act === 'done' && /^data:image\/\w+;base64,/.test(photo || '')) {
    try {
      var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Notice Photos'), folder = it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Notice Photos');
      var m = String(photo).match(/^data:(image\/\w+);base64,(.+)$/);
      link = folder.createFile(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(m[2]), m[1], id + ' ' + w.site + '.jpg')).getUrl();
    } catch (e) { console.error('notice photo: ' + e); }
  }
  withLock_(function () { tgt.forEach(function (who) {
    MEMO_.nr = null;
    var sh = nrSheet_(ss), r = (nrMap_(ss)[id] || {})[who], now = nNow_();
    var row = r ? sh.getRange(r.row, 1, 1, NR_HEAD_.length).getValues()[0] : [id, who, '', '', '', '', '', '', '', ''];
    if (!row[2]) { row[2] = now; row[3] = w.name; }
    if ((act === 'ack' || act === 'done') && !row[4]) { row[4] = now; row[5] = w.name; }
    if (act === 'done') { row[6] = now; row[7] = w.name; row[8] = nTxt_(note, 500); if (link) row[9] = link; }
    if (r) sh.getRange(r.row, 1, 1, NR_HEAD_.length).setValues([row]);
    else { room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + 1, NR_HEAD_.length); sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, NR_HEAD_.length).setValues([row]); }
    MEMO_.nr = null;
  }); });
  if (act !== 'seen') try { mark_(ss, act === 'done' ? 'Task done' : 'Notice acknowledged', w.site, w.name); } catch (e) {}
  var res = loadNotices(site, pin, name);
  res.msg = act === 'done' ? 'Task completed – the office can see it now.' : act === 'ack' ? 'Thank you – the office can see that you read it.' : '';
  return res;
}
/** Office: every notice with the answer of each site. */
function loadNoticesOffice(pin) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var t = today_(), from = nAddDays_(t, -60), open = 0, late = 0, doneMonth = 0;
  var list = ntList_(ss).filter(function (n) { return n.status === 'Open' || String(n.created).slice(0, 10) >= from; }).map(function (n) {
    var rec = nRecipients_(ss, n.to), ans = rec.map(function (s) { var o = nOut_(ss, n, s); return { site: s, st: o.st, seen: o.seen, ack: o.ack, ackBy: o.ackBy, done: o.done, doneBy: o.doneBy, note: o.note, photo: o.photo, auto: o.auto }; });
    var fin = ans.filter(function (a) { return a.st === 'done'; }).length;
    if (n.status === 'Open' && fin < ans.length) { open++; if (n.due && n.due < t) late++; }
    ans.forEach(function (a) { if (a.st === 'done' && String(a.done || a.ack).slice(0, 7) === t.slice(0, 7)) doneMonth++; });
    return { id: n.id, created: n.created, from: n.from, type: n.type, title: n.title, details: n.details, to: nToText_(n.to), due: n.due, priority: n.priority, attach: n.attach,
      source: n.source, status: n.status, ans: ans, fin: fin, late: !!(n.due && n.due < t && fin < ans.length && n.status === 'Open') };
  }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.status === 'Open') !== (b.status === 'Open') ? (a.status === 'Open' ? -1 : 1) : (a.created < b.created ? 1 : -1); });
  var T = nTargets_(ss);
  return { ok: true, today: t, list: list, open: open, late: late, doneMonth: doneMonth, sites: T.sites, stores: T.stores };
}
/** Office: send a notice. d = { type: Announcement|Task, title, details, to: 'ALL'|'SITES'|'STORES'|[names], due, priority, attach: {b64, name} } */
function nSend(pin, d) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  d = d || {};
  var title = nTxt_(d.title, 150), to = Array.isArray(d.to) ? d.to.join(' | ') : String(d.to || '');
  if (!title) return { ok: false, error: 'Type the title.' };
  if (!to) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose who receives it.' };
  if (!nRecipients_(ss, to).length) return { ok: false, error: 'Nobody to send to.' };
  var link = '';
  if (d.attach && /^data:[\w\/.+-]+;base64,/.test(d.attach.b64 || '')) {
    try {
      var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Notice Files'), folder = it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Notice Files');
      var m = String(d.attach.b64).match(/^data:([\w\/.+-]+);base64,(.+)$/);
      var f = folder.createFile(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(m[2]), m[1], nTxt_(d.attach.name, 80) || 'file'));
      try { f.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW); } catch (e) {}
      link = f.getUrl();
    } catch (e) { console.error('notice file: ' + e); }
  }
  var id = withLock_(function () {
    return ntAdd_(ss, { type: d.type === 'Task' ? 'Task' : 'Announcement', title: title, details: d.details, to: to, due: /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d.due || '') ? d.due : '',
      priority: ['Low', 'Normal', 'High'].indexOf(d.priority) >= 0 ? d.priority : 'Normal', attach: link, from: nTxt_(d.from, 40) || 'Office', source: d.source || 'Office' });
  });
  var r = loadNoticesOffice(pin);
  r.msg = id + ' sent to ' + nRecipients_(ss, to).length + ' storekeeper page' + (nRecipients_(ss, to).length == 1 ? '' : 's') + '.';
  return r;
}
/** Office: remind the sites that have not answered (a new reminder notice), or close a notice. */
function nRemind(pin, id) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var n = ntList_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0];
  if (!n) return { ok: false, error: 'Notice not found.' };
  var miss = nRecipients_(ss, n.to).filter(function (s) { return nState_(ss, n, s).st !== 'done'; });
  if (!miss.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Everybody has already answered.' };
  var rid = withLock_(function () {
    return ntAdd_(ss, { type: 'Announcement', title: 'Reminder: ' + n.title, details: (n.type === 'Task' ? 'Please finish this task and mark it done' : 'Please read this and acknowledge') + ' – ' + n.id + (n.due ? ', due ' + n.due : '') + '.',
      to: miss.join(' | '), priority: 'High', from: 'Office', source: 'Reminder', key: 'REM|' + n.id + '|' + today_() });
  });
  var r = loadNoticesOffice(pin);
  r.msg = rid ? 'Reminder sent to ' + miss.join(', ') + '.' : 'A reminder was already sent today.';
  return r;
}
function nClose(pin, id) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var n = ntList_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0];
  if (!n) return { ok: false, error: 'Notice not found.' };
  ntSheet_(ss).getRange(n.row, 12, 1, 3).setValues([['Closed', n.key, nNow_()]]);
  MEMO_.nt = null;
  var r = loadNoticesOffice(pin); r.msg = n.id + ' closed – the storekeepers do not see it any more.';
  return r;
}

/* ---------------- weekly check reminders + daily timer ---------------- */
function nWeekly_(ss, force) {
  var t = today_(), wk = mondayOf_(t), dow = nDow_(t), done = nWeekDone_(ss), out = [];
  if (dow === 1 || force) {
    var id = ntAdd_(ss, { type: 'Task', title: 'Weekly stock check · week of ' + wk, details: 'Go through every item in Stock today (and your other site items), correct any difference and tap “Confirm weekly check”. Please finish by Thursday.',
      to: 'SITES', due: nAddDays_(wk, 3), priority: 'High', from: 'Weekly reminder', source: 'Weekly', key: 'WEEKLY|' + wk });
    if (id) out.push('Weekly check task ' + id + ' sent to all sites.');
  }
  if (dow === 3 || dow === 4) {
    var miss = sitesList_(ss).filter(function (s) { return !done[s]; });
    if (miss.length) {
      var rid = ntAdd_(ss, { type: 'Announcement', title: (dow === 4 ? 'Last day: ' : '2 days left: ') + 'weekly stock check · week of ' + wk, details: 'Your weekly stock check is not confirmed yet. Please finish it ' + (dow === 4 ? 'today.' : 'by Thursday.'),
        to: miss.join(' | '), priority: 'High', from: 'Weekly reminder', source: 'Reminder', key: 'WEEKLYR|' + wk + '|' + dow });
      if (rid) out.push('Weekly check reminder sent to ' + miss.length + ' sites.');
    }
  }
  return out;
}
/** Weekly check status of every site for the office. */
function nWeekTable_(ss) {
  var wk = mondayOf_(today_()), done = nWeekDone_(ss), rem = {};
  ntList_(ss).forEach(function (n) {
    if (n.key.indexOf('WEEKLY|' + wk) === 0 || n.key.indexOf('WEEKLYR|' + wk) === 0) {
      var day = n.key.indexOf('WEEKLYR') === 0 ? (n.key.slice(-1) === '3' ? 'Wed' : 'Thu') : 'Mon';
      nRecipients_(ss, n.to).forEach(function (s) { (rem[s] || (rem[s] = [])).push(day); });
    }
  });
  return { week: wk, rows: sitesList_(ss).map(function (s) { var d = done[s]; return { site: s, done: d ? nIso_(d[0]) : '', by: d ? d[1] : '', rem: (rem[s] || []).join(' · ') }; }) };
}
/** Runs every day at 08:00 (after “Turn on daily reminders & assistant”). */
function wtcDaily() {
  var ss = ss_(), log = [];
  try { ensure_(ss); } catch (e) {}
  try { log = log.concat(withLock_(function () { return nWeekly_(ss, false); })); } catch (e) { console.error('weekly: ' + e); }
  try { if (ss.getSheetByName(PI_TAB_)) pSync_(ss, false); } catch (e) { console.error('PPE file: ' + e); }
  try { log.push(asRun_(ss, true, true).summary); } catch (e) { console.error('assistant: ' + e); }
  try { var dsh = ss.getSheetByName('Dashboard'); if (dsh) dbKpis_(ss, dsh); } catch (e) { console.error('dashboard: ' + e); }
  console.log(log.join('\n'));
}
/** Sheet menu: turns the 08:00 timer on (safe to run again). */
function installDaily() {
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (t) { if (t.getHandlerFunction() === 'wtcDaily') ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); });
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('wtcDaily').timeBased().everyDays(1).atHour(8).inTimezone(TZ_).create();
