  var msg = 'Done. Every day at about 08:00 the system will: run the assistant and e-mail you a short report; on Monday send the weekly-check task to every site; on Wednesday and Thursday remind the sites that have not confirmed.';
  try { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(msg); } catch (e) {}
  return msg;
}
function runAssistantMenu() { var r = asRun_(ss_(), true, false); try { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(r.summary); } catch (e) {} return r.summary; }
function weeklyNowMenu() { var ss = ss_(), m = withLock_(function () { return nWeekly_(ss, true); }); var s = m.join('\n') || 'The weekly-check task for this week was already sent.'; try { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(s); } catch (e) {} return s; }

/* ---------------- assistant ---------------- */
function asRules_() {
  var r = {}; Object.keys(AS_RULES_).forEach(function (k) { r[k] = AS_RULES_[k]; });
  try { var s = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('WTC_ASSIST'); if (s) { var o = JSON.parse(s); Object.keys(r).forEach(function (k) { if (k in o) r[k] = !!o[k]; }); } } catch (e) {}
  return r;
}
/** Looks at every site / store. send = also give the tasks (otherwise only shows), mail = e-mail the office. */
function asRun_(ss, send, mail) {
  var R = asRules_(), t = today_(), dow = nDow_(t), wk = mondayOf_(t), T = nTargets_(ss), all = T.sites.concat(T.stores), done = nWeekDone_(ss);
  var last = {}, sh = ss.getSheetByName('Logins');
  if (sh && sh.getLastRow() > 1) sh.getRange(2, 2, sh.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
    var d = r[0] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(r[0], TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm') : String(r[0] || ''), s = String(r[1] || '').trim();
    if (d && (!last[s] || d > last[s])) last[s] = d;
  });
  try { var XL = xLinks_(ss); Object.keys(XL).forEach(function (st) { var s = XL[st]; if (last[s] && (!last[st] || last[s] > last[st])) last[st] = last[s]; }); } catch (e) {}
  var open = {}, lateT = {};
  ntList_(ss).forEach(function (n) {
    if (n.status !== 'Open' || n.type !== 'Task') return;
    nRecipients_(ss, n.to).forEach(function (s) { if (nState_(ss, n, s).st !== 'done') { open[s] = (open[s] || 0) + 1; if (n.due && n.due < t) (lateT[s] || (lateT[s] = [])).push(n); } });
  });
  var rentLate = {};
  try { rnList_(ss).forEach(function (o) { if (o.status === 'On hire' && o.exp && o.exp < t) (rentLate[o.site] || (rentLate[o.site] = [])).push(o); }); } catch (e) {}
  var dmg = {};
  try {
    var ds = ss.getSheetByName('Damaged');
    if (ds && ds.getLastRow() > 1) ds.getRange(2, 3, ds.getLastRow() - 1, 12).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
      var d = dstr_(r[0]), s = String(r[1]).trim(), q = Number(r[5]) || 0, st = String(r[11] || '');
      if (!d || /Cancel/i.test(st)) return;
      var o = dmg[s] || (dmg[s] = { now: 0, past: 0 });
      if (d >= wk) o.now += q; else if (d >= nAddDays_(wk, -56)) o.past += q;
    });
  } catch (e) {}
  var find = [], rows = [], sent = 0;
  var add = function (s, kind, text, task) {
    var f = { site: s, kind: kind, text: text, sent: false, suggest: !task };
    if (task && send) { var id = ntAdd_(ss, task); if (id) { f.sent = true; sent++; } else f.sent = 'before'; }
    else if (task) f.sent = ntList_(ss).some(function (n) { return n.key === task.key; }) ? 'before' : false;
    find.push(f);
  };
  all.forEach(function (s) {
    var isSite = T.sites.indexOf(s) >= 0, score = 100, ls = last[s] || '', days = ls ? Math.round((Date.parse(t) - Date.parse(ls.slice(0, 10))) / 864e5) : 99;
    if (R.signin && days >= 3) { score -= 15; add(s, 'signin', (ls ? 'No sign-in for ' + days + ' days' : 'Never signed in'), send ? { type: 'Announcement', title: 'Please sign in to the stock page', details: 'The assistant sees no sign-in from ' + s + (ls ? ' since ' + ls.slice(0, 10) : '') + '. Please sign in and update your stock.', to: s, priority: 'Normal', from: 'Assistant', source: 'Assistant', key: 'AS|signin|' + s + '|' + t } : null); }
    if (isSite && R.weekly && !done[s] && dow >= 4) { score -= 25; add(s, 'weekly', 'Weekly stock check not confirmed (week of ' + wk + ')', { type: 'Task', title: 'Weekly stock check is late', details: 'The weekly stock check for the week of ' + wk + ' is not confirmed yet. Please finish it today.', to: s, due: t, priority: 'High', from: 'Assistant', source: 'Assistant', key: 'AS|weekly|' + wk + '|' + s }); }
    else if (isSite && !done[s] && dow >= 1 && dow <= 3) score -= 5;
    if (R.overdue && lateT[s]) { score -= Math.min(30, 10 * lateT[s].length); lateT[s].forEach(function (n) { add(s, 'overdue', 'Task late: ' + n.title + ' (due ' + n.due + ')', { type: 'Announcement', title: 'Reminder: ' + n.title, details: 'This task was due on ' + n.due + '. Please finish it and mark it done (' + n.id + ').', to: s, priority: 'High', from: 'Assistant', source: 'Assistant', key: 'AS|late|' + n.id + '|' + s + '|' + t }); }); }
    if (R.rental && rentLate[s]) rentLate[s].forEach(function (o) { score -= 5; add(s, 'rental', 'Rented ' + o.machine + (o.company ? ' (' + o.company + ')' : '') + ' is ' + rnDays_(o.exp, t) + ' days past the expected return ' + o.exp, { type: 'Task', title: 'Return or extend: ' + o.machine, details: 'The rented ' + o.machine + (o.plate ? ' ' + o.plate : '') + ' from ' + o.company + ' was expected back on ' + o.exp + '. Return it, or change the expected return date (' + o.ref + ').', to: s, due: nAddDays_(t, 1), priority: 'High', from: 'Assistant', source: 'Assistant', key: 'AS|rent|' + o.ref + '|' + o.exp }); });
    var dm = dmg[s];
    if (R.damaged && dm && dm.now >= 3 && dm.now > 3 * (dm.past / 8 || 0.34)) { score -= 10; add(s, 'damaged', dm.now + ' items written off as damaged this week (about ' + Math.round(dm.past / 8 * 10) / 10 + ' a week before)', { type: 'Task', title: 'Many damaged items this week – please send photos', details: dm.now + ' items were written off as damaged this week. Please reply with photos of the damaged items and the reason.', to: s, due: nAddDays_(t, 2), priority: 'Normal', from: 'Assistant', source: 'Assistant', key: 'AS|dmg|' + wk + '|' + s }); }
    rows.push({ site: s, store: !isSite, last: ls, days: days, weekly: isSite ? (done[s] ? 'done' : 'no') : '', open: open[s] || 0, late: (lateT[s] || []).length, score: Math.max(0, score) });
  });
  var need = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.score < 80; }).length;
  var summary = 'Assistant: ' + all.length + ' pages checked · ' + need + ' need attention · ' + (send ? sent + ' new tasks / reminders sent' : find.filter(function (f) { return !f.sent && !f.suggest; }).length + ' tasks to send');
  if (send) try {
    var ash = alSheet_(ss), now = nNow_(), out = find.map(function (f) { return [now, f.site, f.kind, f.text, f.sent === true ? 'Sent to storekeeper' : f.sent ? 'Already sent before' : 'For the office']; });
    if (out.length) { room_(ash, ash.getLastRow() + out.length, 5); ash.getRange(ash.getLastRow() + 1, 1, out.length, 5).setValues(out); }
  } catch (e) { console.error('assistant log: ' + e); }
  if (mail && R.email) try { asMail_(ss, rows, find, sent); } catch (e) { console.error('assistant mail: ' + e); }
  return { rows: rows, find: find, summary: summary, need: need, sent: sent };
}
function asMail_(ss, rows, find, sent) {
  var to = ''; try { to = Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail(); } catch (e) {}
  if (!to) try { to = ss.getOwner().getEmail(); } catch (e) {}
  if (!to) return;
  var wt = nWeekTable_(ss), wdone = wt.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.done; }).length, need = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.score < 80; });
  var url = ''; try { url = ScriptApp.getService().getUrl(); } catch (e) {}
  var box = function (n, t, c) { return '<td style="background:#F4F3EF;border-radius:10px;padding:10px;text-align:center;width:25%"><b style="font-size:22px;color:' + c + '">' + n + '</b><br><span style="font-size:12px;color:#555">' + t + '</span></td>'; };
  var html = '<div style="font-family:Arial;font-size:14px;color:#222;max-width:640px"><h2 style="margin:0 0 4px">WTC Assistant – morning report</h2><div style="color:#555;margin-bottom:12px">' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'EEE dd MMM yyyy HH:mm') + '</div>' +
    '<table style="width:100%;border-spacing:6px"><tr>' + box(rows.length, 'pages checked', AS_COL_) + box(need.length, 'need attention', '#B3261E') + box(sent, 'tasks given', '#2563A8') + box(wdone + '/' + wt.rows.length, 'weekly checks done', '#1b7a3a') + '</tr></table>' +
    '<h3 style="margin:14px 0 4px">Needs your attention</h3><ul>' + (need.length ? need.map(function (r) { return '<li><b>' + esc_(r.site) + '</b> – score ' + r.score + ': ' + find.filter(function (f) { return f.site === r.site; }).map(function (f) { return esc_(f.text); }).join('; ') + '</li>'; }).join('') : '<li>Nothing – all good.</li>') + '</ul>' +
    '<h3 style="margin:14px 0 4px">Everything the assistant found</h3><ul>' + (find.length ? find.map(function (f) { return '<li>' + esc_(f.site) + ' – ' + esc_(f.text) + (f.sent === true ? ' <i>(task sent)</i>' : '') + '</li>'; }).join('') : '<li>Nothing.</li>') + '</ul>' +
    (url ? '<p><a href="' + url + '" style="background:' + AS_COL_ + ';color:#fff;padding:9px 16px;border-radius:8px;text-decoration:none;font-weight:bold">Open the stock page</a></p>' : '') + '</div>';
  MailApp.sendEmail({ to: to, subject: 'WTC Assistant – ' + need.length + ' need attention · ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'dd MMM'), htmlBody: html });
}
/** Office: the assistant tab (scores, what it finds now, weekly checks, rules). */
function assistantOffice(pin) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var r = asRun_(ss, false, false), lastRun = '', ash = ss.getSheetByName(AL_TAB_);
  if (ash && ash.getLastRow() > 1) { var v = ash.getRange(ash.getLastRow(), 1).getValue(); lastRun = v instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(v, TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm') : String(v); }
  var on = false; try { on = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().some(function (t) { return t.getHandlerFunction() === 'wtcDaily'; }); } catch (e) {}
  return { ok: true, today: today_(), rows: r.rows, find: r.find, need: r.need, rules: asRules_(), lastRun: lastRun, timer: on, week: nWeekTable_(ss),
    tasksToday: ntList_(ss).filter(function (n) { return n.source === 'Assistant' && String(n.created).slice(0, 10) === today_(); }).length };
}
function assistantRun(pin) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var s = withLock_(function () { return asRun_(ss, true, false).summary; });
  MEMO_.nt = null; MEMO_.nr = null;
  var r = assistantOffice(pin); r.msg = s; return r;
}
function assistantRules(pin, rules) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var o = {}; Object.keys(AS_RULES_).forEach(function (k) { o[k] = !!(rules || {})[k]; });
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('WTC_ASSIST', JSON.stringify(o));
  var r = assistantOffice(pin); r.msg = 'Assistant rules saved.'; return r;
}

/* ---------------- dashboard tab in the sheet (locked buttons, fits a laptop screen) ----------------
 * Menu WTC Stock › Build / refresh dashboard makes the first tab “Dashboard”: live numbers, 20 buttons that open each tab,
 * 5 quick actions and “Needs attention today”. The buttons are pictures INSIDE the cells, so they cannot be dragged or moved;
 * one click on a button selects its cell and onSelectionChange() below does the job. The tab is protected (warning if
 * someone types on it). Numbers refresh every morning and with 🔄. (Works in Google Sheets on a computer, not the phone app.)
 */

var DB_GROUPS_ = [['📦  DAILY STOCK', [['allsites', 'All Sites Stock', '📊 All Sites Stock', '#C42D4E'], ['received', 'Received', '➕ Received', '#1b7a3a'], ['used', 'Used', '➖ Used', '#B26A00'], ['damaged', 'Damaged', '✖ Damaged', '#B3261E'], ['transfers', 'Transfers', '⇄ Transfers', '#2563A8']]],
  ['🏗  SITE WORK', [['rentals', 'Rentals', '🚜 Rentals', '#B5480F'], ['services', 'Site Services', '🚛 Site Services', '#4E7D2B'], ['checks', 'Checks', '📋 Weekly Checks', '#7A5C00'], ['siteitems', 'Site Items', '📍 Other Site Items', '#2E7D32'], ['repairs', 'Machinery Repairs', '🔧 Machinery Repairs', '#B8274A']]],
  ['♻  STORES · TEAM', [['exitems', 'Excess Items', '🖼 Excess Items', '#5A3E8F'], ['exmoves', 'Excess Moves', '🔁 Excess Moves', '#5A3E8F'], ['notices', 'Notices', '🔔 Notices & Tasks', '#E07A00'], ['assistant', 'Assistant Log', '🤖 Assistant Log', '#00897B'], ['logins', 'Login Summary', '👥 Logins', '#6B4FA0']]],
  ['⚙  SETTINGS', [['items', 'Items', '🧾 Items', '#455A64'], ['sites', 'Sites', '👷 Sites · PINs', '#455A64'], ['exstores', 'Excess Stores', '🏬 Excess Stores', '#455A64'], ['idle', 'Idle Items', '💤 Idle Items', '#8F5400'], ['pdfs', '#PDF', '📄 PDF folder', '#455A64']]]];
var DB_ACTS_ = [['a_refresh', '#REFRESH', '🔄 Refresh numbers', '#1E2329'], ['a_hide', '#HIDE', '🙈 Hide helper tabs', '#1E2329'], ['a_show', '#SHOW', '👁 Show all tabs', '#1E2329'],
  ['a_assist', '#ASSIST', '🤖 Run assistant now', '#00897B'], ['a_page', '#PAGE', '🌐 Open the stock page', '#C42D4E']];
var DB_BG_ = '#FBF4F6', DB_BTN_ROWS_ = [8, 10, 12, 14, 16], DB_ACT_ROW_ = 19, DB_LINK_ROW_ = 20, DB_AT_ROW_ = 23, DB_AT_N_ = 10;
/** Which cell (top-left of a button) does what. */
function dbHits_() {
  var h = {};
  DB_GROUPS_.forEach(function (g, gi) { g[1].forEach(function (b, bi) { h[DB_BTN_ROWS_[bi] + ',' + (1 + gi * 4)] = b[1]; }); });
  DB_ACTS_.forEach(function (a, i) { h[DB_ACT_ROW_ + ',' + (1 + i * 3)] = a[1]; });
  return h;
}
/** Puts a button picture inside a (merged) cell; if Google cannot show the picture, a coloured text button is used instead. */
function dbPic_(rg, key, text, col) {
  rg.merge();
  try {
    var ci = SpreadsheetApp.newCellImage().setSourceUrl('data:image/png;base64,' + DB_IMG_[key]).setAltTextTitle(text).build();
    rg.getCell(1, 1).setValue(ci);
  } catch (e) {
    rg.setValue(text + '  ›').setBackground(col).setFontColor('#FFFFFF').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(11).setHorizontalAlignment('left')
      .setBorder(true, true, true, true, false, false, DB_BG_, SpreadsheetApp.BorderStyle.SOLID_THICK);
  }
}
/** One click on a dashboard button (simple trigger – runs by itself when a cell is selected). */
function onSelectionChange(e) {
  var ss = null;
  try {
    if (!e || !e.range) return;
    var r = e.range, sh = r.getSheet();
    if (sh.getName() !== 'Dashboard' || r.getNumRows() > 1 || r.getNumColumns() > 4) return;
    var a = dbHits_()[r.getRow() + ',' + r.getColumn()];
    if (!a) return;
    ss = e.source || sh.getParent(); SS_OVR_ = ss;
    try { sh.getRange('A1').activate(); } catch (x) {}   // so the same button works again next time
    dbDo_(ss, sh, a);
  } catch (err) {
    try { ss.toast(String(err.message || err), 'Dashboard', 6); } catch (x) {}
  } finally { SS_OVR_ = null; }
}
function dbSay_(ss, m) { try { ss.toast(m, 'Dashboard', 6); } catch (e) {} }
function dbDo_(ss, sh, a) {
  if (a === '#REFRESH') { dbKpis_(ss, sh); return dbSay_(ss, 'Numbers updated.'); }
  if (a === '#HIDE') { var n = hideTabs_(ss); return dbSay_(ss, n + ' helper tabs hidden. They keep working. “Show all tabs” brings them back.'); }
  if (a === '#SHOW') { var m = showTabs_(ss); return dbSay_(ss, m + ' tabs shown again.'); }
  if (a === '#ASSIST') {
    dbSay_(ss, 'The assistant is checking all pages…');
    var res = asRun_(ss, true, false); dbKpis_(ss, sh); return dbSay_(ss, res.summary);
  }
  if (a === '#PDF' || a === '#PAGE') {
    var L = {}; try { L = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('WTC_DBLINKS') || '{}'); } catch (e) {}
    var url = a === '#PDF' ? L.pdf : L.page, label = a === '#PDF' ? '📄 Open the PDF folder (WTC Stock PDFs)' : '🌐 Open the stock page';
    var c = sh.getRange(DB_LINK_ROW_, 1);
    if (!url) { c.setValue(a === '#PDF' ? 'No PDF folder yet – it is made with the first PDF. Then click WTC Stock › Build / refresh dashboard.' : 'Run WTC Stock › Build / refresh dashboard once, then click this button again.').setBackground('#FFF4C2'); return; }
    c.setRichTextValue(SpreadsheetApp.newRichTextValue().setText('👉  ' + label + '  –  click here').setLinkUrl(url).build());
    c.setBackground('#FFF4C2').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(11);
    return dbSay_(ss, 'Click the yellow link under the quick actions.');
  }
  dbGo_(ss, a);
}
/** Opens a tab (shows it first if it was hidden). */
function dbGo_(ss, tab) {
  var t = ss.getSheetByName(tab);
  if (!t) return dbSay_(ss, 'The tab “' + tab + '” is not there yet. It is made by itself with the first entry of that kind.');
  if (t.isSheetHidden()) t.showSheet();
  t.activate();
  try { t.getRange(1, 1).activate(); } catch (e) {}
}
function hideTabs_(ss) {
  var n = 0;
  HELPER_TABS_.forEach(function (name) { var s = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (s && !s.isSheetHidden() && ss.getSheets().filter(function (x) { return !x.isSheetHidden(); }).length > 1) { s.hideSheet(); n++; } });
  return n;
}
function showTabs_(ss) { var n = 0; ss.getSheets().forEach(function (s) { if (s.isSheetHidden()) { s.showSheet(); n++; } }); return n; }
function dbLinks_(ss) {
  var L = {};
  try { var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Stock PDFs'); if (it.hasNext()) L.pdf = it.next().getUrl(); } catch (e) {}
  try { L.page = ScriptApp.getService().getUrl(); } catch (e) {}
  try { PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('WTC_DBLINKS', JSON.stringify(L)); } catch (e) {}
}

/** Builds the whole Dashboard tab (layout + buttons), then the numbers. Returns the sheet. */
function dbBuild_(ss) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Dashboard');
  if (!sh) sh = ss.insertSheet('Dashboard', 0);
  try { ss.setActiveSheet(sh); ss.moveActiveSheet(1); } catch (e) {}
  try { sh.getImages().forEach(function (im) { im.remove(); }); } catch (e) {}   // old floating buttons (v23.1)
  try { sh.getProtections(SpreadsheetApp.ProtectionType.SHEET).forEach(function (p) { p.remove(); }); } catch (e) {}
  sh.clear();
  try { sh.getRange(1, 1, sh.getMaxRows(), sh.getMaxColumns()).breakApart(); } catch (e) {}
  if (sh.getMaxColumns() < 17) sh.insertColumnsAfter(sh.getMaxColumns(), 17 - sh.getMaxColumns());
  if (sh.getMaxRows() < 40) sh.insertRowsAfter(sh.getMaxRows(), 40 - sh.getMaxRows());
  try { sh.setHiddenGridlines(true); } catch (e) {}
  try { sh.setTabColor('#C42D4E'); } catch (e) {}
  try { sh.setFrozenRows(0); sh.setFrozenColumns(0); } catch (e) {}
  sh.setColumnWidths(1, 16, 72); sh.setColumnWidth(17, 20);
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 40, 17).setBackground(DB_BG_).setFontFamily('Arial').setVerticalAlignment('middle').setFontColor('#1E2329').setFontSize(10);
  var H = { 1: 40, 2: 20, 3: 6, 4: 30, 5: 18, 6: 8, 7: 20, 9: 4, 11: 4, 13: 4, 15: 4, 17: 8, 18: 20, 19: 34, 20: 22, 21: 8, 22: 22 };
  DB_BTN_ROWS_.forEach(function (r) { H[r] = 44; });
  for (var i = 0; i < DB_AT_N_; i++) H[DB_AT_ROW_ + i] = 22;
  H[DB_AT_ROW_ + DB_AT_N_] = 20;
  Object.keys(H).forEach(function (r) { sh.setRowHeight(Number(r), H[r]); });
  // header
  try { sh.getRange('A1:A2').merge(); sh.getRange('A1').setValue(SpreadsheetApp.newCellImage().setSourceUrl(LOGO_).setAltTextTitle('WTC').build()); } catch (e) {}
  sh.getRange('B1:L1').merge().setValue('WTC STOCK · DASHBOARD').setFontSize(20).setFontWeight('bold');
  sh.getRange('B2:P2').merge().setFontColor('#C42D4E').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9);
  for (var k = 0; k < 8; k++) { sh.getRange(4, 1 + k * 2, 1, 2).merge(); sh.getRange(5, 1 + k * 2, 1, 2).merge(); }
  // 20 buttons
  DB_GROUPS_.forEach(function (g, gi) {
    var c = 1 + gi * 4;
    sh.getRange(7, c, 1, 4).merge().setValue(g[0]).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setFontColor('#555555');
    g[1].forEach(function (b, bi) { dbPic_(sh.getRange(DB_BTN_ROWS_[bi], c, 1, 4), b[0], b[2], b[3]); });
  });
  // quick actions
  sh.getRange(18, 1, 1, 16).merge().setValue('⚡  QUICK ACTIONS').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setFontColor('#555555');
  DB_ACTS_.forEach(function (a, i) { dbPic_(sh.getRange(DB_ACT_ROW_, 1 + i * 3, 1, 3), a[0], a[2], a[3]); });
  sh.getRange(DB_LINK_ROW_, 1, 1, 16).merge();
  // needs attention
  sh.getRange(22, 1, 1, 3).merge().setValue('🔎 NEEDS ATTENTION'); sh.getRange(22, 4, 1, 10).merge().setValue('What the assistant found today'); sh.getRange(22, 14, 1, 3).merge().setValue('Status');
  sh.getRange(22, 1, 1, 16).setBackground('#1E2329').setFontColor('#FFFFFF').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9);
  for (var r = DB_AT_ROW_; r < DB_AT_ROW_ + DB_AT_N_; r++) { sh.getRange(r, 1, 1, 3).merge(); sh.getRange(r, 4, 1, 10).merge(); sh.getRange(r, 14, 1, 3).merge(); }
  sh.getRange(DB_AT_ROW_ + DB_AT_N_, 1, 1, 16).merge().setValue('One click on a button opens it · come back with the “Dashboard” tab (always first) · buttons are locked in their cells · works on a computer; on the phone use the stock page.').setFontColor('#777777').setFontSize(8);
  dbLinks_(ss);
  dbKpis_(ss, sh);
  try { var pr = sh.protect().setDescription('WTC Dashboard – made by the script. Use WTC Stock › Build / refresh dashboard.'); pr.setWarningOnly(true); } catch (e) {}
  try { sh.getRange('A1').activate(); } catch (e) {}
  return sh;
}
/** Writes the live numbers, the time and “Needs attention today”. */
function dbKpis_(ss, sh) {
  var t = today_(), k = {};
  var cnt = function (name, col, test) { var s = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (!s || s.getLastRow() < 2) return 0; return s.getRange(2, col, s.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().filter(function (r) { return test(r[0]); }).length; };
  var isT = function (v) { return dstr_(v).slice(0, 10) === t; };
  try { k.sites = sitesList_(ss).length + nTargets_(ss).stores.length; } catch (e) { k.sites = 0; }
  try { k.today = cnt('Received', 3, isT) + cnt('Used', 3, isT) + cnt('Damaged', 3, isT) + cnt('Transfers', 2, isT) + cnt('Excess Moves', 2, isT) + cnt('Site Services', 2, isT) + cnt('Site Item Moves', 2, isT); } catch (e) { k.today = 0; }
  try { var on = loadNoticesOffice_(ss); k.open = on.open; k.late = on.late; } catch (e) { k.open = 0; k.late = 0; }
  try { var wt = nWeekTable_(ss); k.week = wt.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.done; }).length + ' / ' + wt.rows.length; } catch (e) { k.week = '–'; }
  try { k.rent = rnList_(ss).filter(function (o) { return o.status === 'On hire'; }).length; } catch (e) { k.rent = 0; }
  try { k.ex = ss.getSheetByName('Excess Items') ? xState_(ss).cards.filter(function (c) { return c.total > 0; }).length : 0; } catch (e) { k.ex = 0; }
  try { k.si = siItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.status === 'OK'; }).length; } catch (e) { k.si = 0; }
  var A = null; try { A = asRun_(ss, false, false); } catch (e) { console.error('dashboard assistant: ' + e); }
  k.need = A ? A.need : 0;
  var K = [[k.sites, 'sites & stores', '#C42D4E'], [k.today, 'entries today', '#2563A8'], [k.open + (k.late ? '  (' + k.late + ' late)' : ''), 'open notices & tasks', N_COL_],
    [k.week, 'weekly checks done', '#1b7a3a'], [k.rent, 'rented machines on hire', '#B5480F'], [k.ex, 'excess materials in stock', '#5A3E8F'], [k.si, 'other site items', SI_COL_], [k.need, 'need attention today', '#B3261E']];
  K.forEach(function (x, i) {
    var c = 1 + i * 2;
    sh.getRange(4, c).setValue(x[0]); sh.getRange(5, c).setValue(x[1]);
    sh.getRange(4, c, 1, 2).setFontSize(18).setFontWeight('bold').setFontColor(x[2]).setBackground('#FFFFFF').setHorizontalAlignment('left');
    sh.getRange(5, c, 1, 2).setFontSize(8).setFontColor('#555555').setBackground('#FFFFFF').setFontWeight('normal');
    sh.getRange(4, c, 2, 2).setBorder(true, true, true, true, false, false, DB_BG_, SpreadsheetApp.BorderStyle.SOLID_THICK);
    sh.getRange(4, c, 1, 2).setBorder(true, null, null, null, null, null, x[2], SpreadsheetApp.BorderStyle.SOLID_THICK);
  });
  sh.getRange('B2').setValue('WALK THROUGH GENERAL CONTRACTING · updated ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'EEE dd MMM yyyy HH:mm') + ' · numbers refresh every morning and with 🔄 Refresh numbers');
  sh.getRange(DB_LINK_ROW_, 1).setValue('').setBackground(DB_BG_);
  var rows = [], IC = { signin: '😴', weekly: '📋', overdue: '⏰', rental: '🚜', damaged: '⚠️' };
  if (A) A.find.forEach(function (f) { rows.push([f.site, (IC[f.kind] || '•') + '  ' + f.text, f.sent === true ? '✔ Task sent today' : f.sent ? '✔ Task already sent' : f.suggest ? 'For the office' : 'Task goes out at 08:00']); });
  var more = rows.length > DB_AT_N_ ? rows.length - DB_AT_N_ + 1 : 0;
  if (more) { rows = rows.slice(0, DB_AT_N_ - 1); rows.push(['', '… and ' + more + ' more – open the 🤖 Assistant tab on the stock page', '']); }
  for (var r = DB_AT_ROW_; r < DB_AT_ROW_ + DB_AT_N_; r++) {
    var v = rows[r - DB_AT_ROW_] || (r === DB_AT_ROW_ && !rows.length ? ['', '👍  Nothing needs attention – all good.', ''] : ['', '', '']);
    sh.getRange(r, 1).setValue(v[0]); sh.getRange(r, 4).setValue(v[1]); sh.getRange(r, 14).setValue(v[2]);
    sh.getRange(r, 1, 1, 16).setBackground(v[1] ? (r % 2 ? '#FFFFFF' : '#F6F1F3') : DB_BG_).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('normal').setFontColor('#1E2329');
    if (v[0]) sh.getRange(r, 1).setFontWeight('bold');
    if (v[2]) sh.getRange(r, 14).setFontColor(/^✔/.test(v[2]) ? '#1b7a3a' : '#B3261E');
  }
  return sh;
}
function loadNoticesOffice_(ss) {
  var t = today_(), open = 0, late = 0;
  ntList_(ss).forEach(function (n) {
    if (n.status !== 'Open') return;
    var rec = nRecipients_(ss, n.to), fin = rec.filter(function (s) { return nState_(ss, n, s).st === 'done'; }).length;
    if (fin < rec.length) { open++; if (n.due && n.due < t) late++; }
  });
  return { open: open, late: late };
}
function buildDashboard() { var ss = ss_(); var sh = dbBuild_(ss); try { ss.setActiveSheet(sh); sh.getRange('A1').activate(); ss.toast('Dashboard ready – one click on a button opens it.', 'Dashboard', 5); } catch (e) {} return 'Dashboard updated.'; }
function refreshDashboard() { var ss = ss_(); var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Dashboard'); if (!sh) return buildDashboard(); dbKpis_(ss, sh); dbSay_(ss, 'Numbers updated.'); }
function openDashboard() { var ss = ss_(); var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Dashboard') || dbBuild_(ss); ss.setActiveSheet(sh); try { sh.getRange('A1').activate(); } catch (e) {} }
function hideHelperTabs() {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!ss.getSheetByName('Dashboard')) dbBuild_(ss);
  var n = hideTabs_(ss);
  try { ss.setActiveSheet(ss.getSheetByName('Dashboard')); } catch (e) {}
  try { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(n + ' helper tabs hidden. They keep working. Use “Show all tabs” to bring them back.'); } catch (e) {}
  return n;
}
function showAllTabs() { var ss = ss_(), n = showTabs_(ss); try { ss.setActiveSheet(ss.getSheetByName('Dashboard') || ss.getSheets()[0]); } catch (e) {} try { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(n + ' tabs shown again.'); } catch (e) {} return n; }

/* ---------------- other site items (not in the main list) ---------------- */
function siItems_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.si) return MEMO_.si;
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(SI_TAB_), n = sh ? sh.getLastRow() : 0, out = [];
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, SI_HEAD_.length).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    if (!String(r[0]).trim()) return;
    var s = function (k) { return String(r[k] == null ? '' : r[k]).trim(); };
    out.push({ row: i + 2, code: s(0), site: s(1), cat: s(2), name: s(3), size: s(4), unit: s(5), cond: s(6), whose: s(7), loc: s(8), qty: Number(r[9]) || 0, note: s(10),
      ph: s(11) ? s(11).split(',').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(String) : [], created: dstr_(r[12]), by: s(13), checked: dstr_(r[14]), status: s(15) || 'OK',
      thumb: /^data:image\//.test(s(16)) ? s(16) : '' });
  });
  return (MEMO_.si = out);
}
function siPub_(i) { return { code: i.code, site: i.site, cat: i.cat, name: i.name, size: i.size, unit: i.unit, cond: i.cond, whose: i.whose, loc: i.loc, qty: i.qty, note: i.note, nph: i.ph.length, ph: i.ph, created: i.created, by: i.by, checked: i.checked, status: i.status, thumb: i.thumb }; }
function siSavePhotos_(ss, code, photos, have) {
  photos = (photos || []).filter(function (p) { return /^data:image\/\w+;base64,/.test(p || ''); }).slice(0, Math.max(0, 5 - (have || 0)));
  if (!photos.length) return [];
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Site Item Photos'), folder = it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Site Item Photos'), ids = [];
  photos.forEach(function (p, k) { try { var m = String(p).match(/^data:(image\/\w+);base64,(.+)$/); ids.push(folder.createFile(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(m[2]), m[1], code + ' ' + ((have || 0) + k + 1) + '.jpg')).getId()); } catch (e) { console.error('site item photo: ' + e); } });
  return ids;
}
function siMove_(ss, o) {
  var sh = smSheet_(ss), ref = 'SIM-' + ('000' + (maxRef_(sh) + 1)).slice(-4), now = new Date();
  room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + 1, SM_HEAD_.length);
  sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, SM_HEAD_.length).setValues([[ref, today_(), "'" + Utilities.formatDate(now, TZ_, 'HH:mm'), o.code, o.site, o.item, o.move, o.qty, o.unit, o.after, o.to || '', o.note || '', o.by, 'OK', '', '']]);
  return ref;
}
function siState_(ss, site) {
  var l = siItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.site === site && i.status === 'OK'; }).map(siPub_);
  var T = nTargets_(ss);
  return { ok: true, site: site, items: l, cats: X_CATS_, units: X_UNITS_, sites: T.sites.filter(function (s) { return s !== site; }), stores: T.stores, week: mondayOf_(today_()) };
}
function siFields_(d) {
  d = d || {};
  var f = { cat: nTxt_(d.cat, 40), name: nTxt_(d.name, 120), size: nTxt_(d.size, 60), unit: nTxt_(d.unit, 20), cond: nTxt_(d.cond, 20), whose: nTxt_(d.whose, 30), loc: nTxt_(d.loc, 80), note: nTxt_(d.note, 300) };
  if (!f.cat) return { error: 'Choose the category.' };
  if (!f.name) return { error: 'Type the name of the item.' };
  if (!f.unit) return { error: 'Choose the unit.' };
  if (!f.cond) return { error: 'Choose the condition.' };
  if (!f.whose) return { error: 'Choose whose it is.' };
  return f;
}
function loadSiteItems(site, pin, name) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = nWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error) return { ok: false, error: w.error };
  if (w.office || w.store) return { ok: false, error: 'Only for site storekeepers.' };
  return siState_(ss, w.site);
}
/** Storekeeper adds an item that is not in the main list. d = { cat, name, size, unit, cond, whose, loc, qty, note, photos:[dataURI], thumb } */
function siAdd(site, pin, name, d) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = nWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error || w.office || w.store) return { ok: false, error: w.error || 'Only for site storekeepers.' };
  var f = siFields_(d); if (f.error) return { ok: false, error: f.error };
  var q = xNum_(d.qty); if (!(q >= 0)) return { ok: false, error: 'Type how many there are now.' };
  if (siItems_(ss).some(function (i) { return i.site === w.site && i.status === 'OK' && i.name.toLowerCase() === f.name.toLowerCase() && i.size.toLowerCase() === f.size.toLowerCase(); }))
    return { ok: false, error: 'This item is already in your list – open it and update the quantity.' };
  var res = withLock_(function () {
    MEMO_.si = null;
    var sh = siSheet_(ss), code = 'SI-' + ('000' + (maxRef_(sh) + 1)).slice(-4), t = today_();
    var thumb = /^data:image\/\w+;base64,/.test(d.thumb || '') && String(d.thumb).length < 45000 ? String(d.thumb) : '';
    room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + 1, SI_HEAD_.length);
    sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, SI_HEAD_.length).setValues([[code, w.site, f.cat, f.name, f.size, f.unit, f.cond, f.whose, f.loc, q, f.note, '', t, w.name, t, 'OK', thumb]]);
    var ref = siMove_(ss, { code: code, site: w.site, item: f.name, move: 'Added', qty: q, unit: f.unit, after: q, by: w.name, note: f.whose });
    MEMO_.si = null;
    return { code: code, ref: ref };
  });
  try { var ids = siSavePhotos_(ss, res.code, d.photos, 0); if (ids.length) { var it = siItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.code === res.code; })[0]; siSheet_(ss).getRange(it.row, 12).setValue(ids.join(',')); MEMO_.si = null; } } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
  try { mark_(ss, 'Other site item added', w.site, w.name); } catch (e) {}
  var r = siState_(ss, w.site); r.msg = 'Saved – ' + res.code + ' · ' + q + ' ' + f.unit + ' ' + f.name + ' (' + res.ref + ')'; return r;
}
/** count (qty = the quantity counted now) · used · damaged · send (to a site or excess store) · remove (not at site any more). */
function siAct(site, pin, name, code, d) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = nWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error || w.office || w.store) return { ok: false, error: w.error || 'Only for site storekeepers.' };
  d = d || {};
  var type = d.type, q = xNum_(d.qty), note = nTxt_(d.note, 200), to = nTxt_(d.to, 80), T = nTargets_(ss);
  if (['count', 'used', 'damaged', 'send', 'remove'].indexOf(type) < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Unknown action' };
  if (type !== 'remove' && !(type === 'count' ? q >= 0 : q > 0)) return { ok: false, error: type === 'count' ? 'Type the quantity you counted.' : 'Type how many.' };
  if ((type === 'damaged' || type === 'remove') && !note) return { ok: false, error: 'Type the reason.' };
  if (type === 'send' && T.sites.indexOf(to) < 0 && T.stores.indexOf(to) < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose where it goes.' };
  if (type === 'send' && to === w.site) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose another site.' };
  var res = withLock_(function () {
    MEMO_.si = null;
    var it = siItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.code === code && i.site === w.site && i.status === 'OK'; })[0];
    if (!it) return { ok: false, error: 'Item not found.' };
    var have = it.qty, after = have, delta = 0, sh = siSheet_(ss), t = today_(), M = { count: 'Count', used: 'Used', damaged: 'Damaged', send: 'Sent', remove: 'Removed' }[type];
    if (type === 'count') { after = q; delta = r3_(q - have); }
    else if (type === 'remove') { after = 0; delta = -have; }
    else { if (q > have) return { ok: false, error: 'Only ' + have + ' ' + it.unit + ' at ' + w.site + '.' }; after = r3_(have - q); delta = -q; }
    sh.getRange(it.row, 10).setValue(after);
    if (type === 'count') sh.getRange(it.row, 15).setValue(t);
    if (type === 'remove') sh.getRange(it.row, 16).setValue('Removed');
    var ref = siMove_(ss, { code: code, site: w.site, item: it.name, move: M, qty: delta, unit: it.unit, after: after, to: to, note: note, by: w.name }), extra = '';
    if (type === 'send' && T.sites.indexOf(to) >= 0) {
      var ex = siItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.site === to && i.status === 'OK' && i.name.toLowerCase() === it.name.toLowerCase() && i.size.toLowerCase() === it.size.toLowerCase(); })[0];
      if (ex) { sh.getRange(ex.row, 10).setValue(r3_(ex.qty + q)); siMove_(ss, { code: ex.code, site: to, item: it.name, move: 'Received', qty: q, unit: it.unit, after: r3_(ex.qty + q), to: w.site, note: 'from ' + w.site + ' (' + ref + ')', by: w.name }); extra = ex.code; }
      else {
        var nc = 'SI-' + ('000' + (maxRef_(sh) + 1)).slice(-4);
        room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + 1, SI_HEAD_.length);
        sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, SI_HEAD_.length).setValues([[nc, to, it.cat, it.name, it.size, it.unit, it.cond, it.whose, '', q, 'Came from ' + w.site, it.ph.join(','), t, w.name, '', 'OK', it.thumb]]);
        siMove_(ss, { code: nc, site: to, item: it.name, move: 'Received', qty: q, unit: it.unit, after: q, to: w.site, note: 'from ' + w.site + ' (' + ref + ')', by: w.name }); extra = nc;
      }
    } else if (type === 'send') {   // into an excess store as a material card
      var xs = xiSheet_(ss), xc = xRef_(xs, 'EX-');
      room_(xs, xs.getLastRow() + 1, XI_N_);
      xs.getRange(xs.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, XI_N_).setValues([[xc, X_CATS_.indexOf(it.cat) >= 0 ? it.cat : 'Site materials', it.name, '', '', it.size, '', it.unit, it.cond === 'New' ? 'New' : it.cond === 'Damaged' ? 'Used – damaged' : 'Used – good', to, '', '', 'From ' + w.site + ' (' + code + ')', t, w.name, 'OK', it.thumb]]);
      MEMO_.xItems = null;
      xAddMove_(ss, { date: t, code: xc, item: it.name, move: 'Received', qty: q, unit: it.unit, from: w.site, to: to, noteNo: ref, by2: w.name, rec: w.name });
      extra = xc;
    }
    MEMO_.si = null;
    return { ok: true, ref: ref, item: it.name, unit: it.unit, have: have, after: after, extra: extra, M: M };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return res;
  if (type === 'send' && T.stores.indexOf(to) >= 0) try { xSync_(ss, res.extra); } catch (e) {}
  var r = siState_(ss, w.site);
  r.msg = res.M + ' · ' + res.item + ' · ' + res.have + ' → ' + res.after + ' ' + res.unit + (res.extra ? ' · now ' + res.extra + ' at ' + to : '') + ' (' + res.ref + ')';
  return r;
}
function siEdit(site, pin, name, code, d, photos, thumb) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = nWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error || w.office || w.store) return { ok: false, error: w.error || 'Only for site storekeepers.' };
  var f = siFields_(d); if (f.error) return { ok: false, error: f.error };
  var res = withLock_(function () {
    MEMO_.si = null;
    var it = siItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.code === code && i.site === w.site && i.status === 'OK'; })[0];
    if (!it) return { ok: false, error: 'Item not found.' };
    var sh = siSheet_(ss);
    sh.getRange(it.row, 3, 1, 7).setValues([[f.cat, f.name, f.size, f.unit, f.cond, f.whose, f.loc]]); sh.getRange(it.row, 11).setValue(f.note);
    if (!it.thumb && /^data:image\/\w+;base64,/.test(thumb || '') && String(thumb).length < 45000) sh.getRange(it.row, 17).setValue(thumb);
    MEMO_.si = null;
    return { ok: true, it: it };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return res;
  try { var ids = siSavePhotos_(ss, code, photos, res.it.ph.length); if (ids.length) { siSheet_(ss).getRange(res.it.row, 12).setValue(res.it.ph.concat(ids).join(',')); MEMO_.si = null; } } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
  var r = siState_(ss, w.site); r.msg = code + ' · ' + f.name + ' – saved'; return r;
}
function siPhoto(site, pin, name, id) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = nWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error) return { ok: false, error: w.error };
  if (!siItems_(ss).some(function (i) { return i.ph.indexOf(id) >= 0 && (w.office || i.site === w.site); })) return { ok: false, error: 'Photo not found' };
  try { var b = DriveApp.getFileById(id).getBlob(); return { ok: true, id: id, src: 'data:' + (b.getContentType() || 'image/jpeg') + ';base64,' + Utilities.base64Encode(b.getBytes()) }; }
  catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: 'Could not open the photo.' }; }
}
function siHistory(site, pin, name, code) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = nWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error) return { ok: false, error: w.error };
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(SM_TAB_), n = sh ? sh.getLastRow() : 0, out = [];
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, SM_HEAD_.length).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
    if (String(r[3]).trim() !== code) return;
    if (!w.office && String(r[4]).trim() !== w.site) return;
    out.push({ ref: String(r[0]), date: dstr_(r[1]), time: xTime_(r[2]), move: String(r[6]), qty: Number(r[7]) || 0, unit: String(r[8]), after: Number(r[9]) || 0, to: String(r[10] || ''), note: String(r[11] || ''), by: String(r[12] || ''), status: String(r[13] || 'OK') });
  });
  return { ok: true, code: code, moves: out.reverse() };
}
/** Office: all other site items. */
function loadSiteItemsOffice(pin) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  var wk = mondayOf_(today_()), cats = {};
  itemRows_(ss).forEach(function (r) { var c = String(r[2] || '').trim(); if (c) cats[c] = 1; });
  return { ok: true, items: siItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.status === 'OK'; }).map(siPub_), sites: sitesList_(ss), week: wk, mainCats: Object.keys(cats).sort(), units: X_UNITS_ };
}
/** Office: make a site item a normal item of the main list (all sites will see it). f = { cat, sub, unit } */
function siToMain(pin, code, f) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  f = f || {};
  var it = siItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.code === code && i.status === 'OK'; })[0];
  if (!it) return { ok: false, error: 'Item not found.' };
  if (!nTxt_(f.cat)) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose the category of the main list.' };
  var res = withLock_(function () {
    var ish = ss.getSheetByName('Items'), head = ish.getRange(1, 1, 1, ish.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0], maxId = 0;
    itemRows_(ss).forEach(function (r) { var v = Number(r[0]); if (v > maxId) maxId = v; });
    var id = maxId + 1, img = '';
    if (it.thumb) { var im = ss.getSheetByName('Images'); img = 'IMG-' + it.code; room_(im, im.getLastRow() + 1); im.getRange(im.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, 2).setValues([[img, it.thumb]]); }
    var row = head.map(function () { return ''; });
    row[0] = id; row[1] = it.name + (it.size ? ' ' + it.size : ''); row[2] = nTxt_(f.cat, 40); row[3] = nTxt_(f.sub, 40); row[4] = nTxt_(f.unit, 20) || it.unit; row[5] = img;
    room_(ish, ish.getLastRow() + 1); ish.getRange(ish.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    MEMO_.items = MEMO_.itemsTab = null;
    siSheet_(ss).getRange(it.row, 16).setValue('In main list (item ' + id + ')');
    siMove_(ss, { code: it.code, site: it.site, item: it.name, move: 'To main list', qty: 0, unit: it.unit, after: it.qty, to: 'Item ' + id, note: 'by office', by: 'Office' });
    ntAdd_(ss, { type: 'Task', title: 'Enter “' + row[1] + '” in Stock today', details: 'This item is now in the main list (item ' + id + '). Please enter its quantity in Stock today: you had ' + it.qty + ' ' + it.unit + ' (' + it.code + '). Reload the page first.',
      to: it.site, due: nAddDays_(today_(), 2), priority: 'Normal', from: 'Office', source: 'Office', key: 'SIMAIN|' + it.code });
    MEMO_.si = null;
    return { id: id };
  });
  try { liveSafe_(ss, ledger_(ss)); } catch (e) {}
  var r = loadSiteItemsOffice(pin); r.msg = it.name + ' is now item ' + res.id + ' of the main list. ' + it.site + ' got a task to enter the quantity in Stock today.'; return r;
}
function siCancel_(ss, ref, dry) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(SM_TAB_), n = sh ? sh.getLastRow() : 0, row = -1, r = null;
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, SM_HEAD_.length).getValues().forEach(function (x, i) { if (String(x[0]).trim() === ref) { row = i + 2; r = x; } });
  if (!r) return { ok: false, error: 'No entry ' + ref + ' was found.' };
  if (/Cancel/.test(String(r[13]))) return { ok: false, error: ref + ' is already cancelled.' };
  var mv = String(r[6]), code = String(r[3]).trim();
  if (mv === 'Sent' || mv === 'Received' || mv === 'To main list') return { ok: false, error: mv + ' entries cannot be cancelled here – correct the quantity at both sites with “Update quantity”.' };
  var it = siItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.code === code; })[0];
  if (!it) return { ok: false, error: 'Item ' + code + ' not found.' };
  var back = r3_(it.qty - (Number(r[7]) || 0));
  if (back < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Cannot cancel: the quantity would go below zero.' };
  var desc = 'Other site item ' + mv + ' ' + ref + ' · ' + code + ' ' + it.name + ' · ' + r[7] + ' ' + it.unit + ' · ' + it.site + ' · by ' + r[12];
  if (dry) return { ok: true, summary: desc + '\n\nAfter cancelling: ' + (mv === 'Added' ? 'the item is removed from the list.' : back + ' ' + it.unit + '.') };
  sh.getRange(row, 14, 1, 3).setValues([['Cancelled by office', 'Office', new Date()]]);
  var s2 = siSheet_(ss);
  if (mv === 'Added') s2.getRange(it.row, 16).setValue('Cancelled');
  else { s2.getRange(it.row, 10).setValue(back); if (mv === 'Removed') s2.getRange(it.row, 16).setValue('OK'); }
  MEMO_.si = null;
  return { ok: true };
}
/** PDF of the other site items with photos (office: all sites or one; storekeeper: his site). */
function makeSiteItemsPdf(site, pin, name, which) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = nWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error) return { ok: false, error: w.error };
  var only = w.office ? String(which || '').trim() : w.site;
  var l = siItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.status === 'OK' && (!only || i.site === only); }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.site + a.cat + a.name).localeCompare(b.site + b.cat + b.name); });
  if (!l.length) return { ok: false, error: 'No other site items' + (only ? ' at ' + only : '') + '.' };
  var wk = mondayOf_(today_()), last = '';
  var html = '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><style>@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:12mm}body{font-family:Arial;font-size:9pt;color:#1E2329}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}thead{display:table-header-group}tr{page-break-inside:avoid}th{background:' + SI_COL_ + ';color:#fff;padding:5px;text-align:left}td{border:1px solid #D5D5D5;padding:4px 6px;vertical-align:middle}img{width:60px;height:60px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:4px}.s td{background:#E8F5E9;font-weight:bold;font-size:10.5pt}</style></head><body>' +
    '<div style="font-size:17pt;font-weight:bold">OTHER ITEMS AT SITES (not in the main list)</div><div style="color:#C42D4E;font-weight:bold">WALK THROUGH GENERAL CONTRACTING - SOLE PROPRIETORSHIP LLC</div><div style="color:#555;margin-bottom:8px">' + esc_(only || 'All sites') + ' · ' + l.length + ' items · ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm') + '</div>' +
    '<table><thead><tr><th>Photo</th><th>Code</th><th>Category</th><th>Item</th><th>Size / colour</th><th style="text-align:right">Qty</th><th>Condition</th><th>Whose</th><th>Where on site</th><th>Last checked</th><th>Added by</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
    l.map(function (i) { var h = ''; if (i.site !== last) { last = i.site; h = '<tr class="s"><td colspan="11">' + esc_(i.site) + '</td></tr>'; }
      return h + '<tr><td style="width:66px">' + (i.thumb ? '<img src="' + i.thumb + '">' : '') + '</td><td>' + esc_(i.code) + '</td><td>' + esc_(i.cat) + '</td><td><b>' + esc_(i.name) + '</b>' + (i.note ? '<br><span style="color:#666">' + esc_(i.note) + '</span>' : '') + '</td><td>' + esc_(i.size) + '</td><td style="text-align:right;font-weight:bold">' + i.qty + ' ' + esc_(i.unit) + '</td><td>' + esc_(i.cond) + '</td><td>' + esc_(i.whose) + '</td><td>' + esc_(i.loc) + '</td><td' + (i.checked >= wk ? '' : ' style="color:#B3261E"') + '>' + esc_(i.checked || '–') + '</td><td>' + esc_(i.by) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') +
    '</tbody></table></body></html>';
  try {
    var pdf = Utilities.newBlob(html, 'text/html', 'si.html').getAs('application/pdf').setName('Other_site_items_' + today_() + (only ? '_' + only.replace(/\W+/g, '_') : '') + '.pdf'), url = '';
    try { var it2 = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('WTC Stock PDFs'), folder = it2.hasNext() ? it2.next() : DriveApp.createFolder('WTC Stock PDFs'); url = folder.createFile(pdf).getUrl(); } catch (e2) {}
    return { ok: true, name: pdf.getName(), url: url, b64: Utilities.base64Encode(pdf.getBytes()), count: l.length };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: 'Could not create the PDF: ' + e }; }
}

/* ======================= PPE: safety wear issued to company employees =======================
 * One store issues PPE (default MUSAFFAH STORE – change it on the page: office › PPE › Settings).
 * Tabs: PPE Items (office keeps the list, start balance, minimum stock) · PPE Employees · PPE Issued · PPE Purchases.
 * Balance = start balance + purchases (office only) − issues. Rows with status “Imported” are the history from the
 * old Excel (already inside the start balance). Storekeepers cannot cancel; only the office (ALL SITES PIN) can.
 * Early issue (before 3 months, helmet 1 year) needs the approval + photo, and damage photos (or the Camp Boss letter if lost).
 * Every change is written into the separate Google Sheet “PPE Master List – LIVE” (Drive folder “WTC PPE”).
 */
var PI_TAB_ = 'PPE Items', PE_TAB_ = 'PPE Employees', PS_TAB_ = 'PPE Issued', PP_TAB_ = 'PPE Purchases', P_COL_ = '#D35400';
var PI_HEAD_ = ['Item code', 'Item name', 'Type', 'Category', 'Size', 'Unit', 'Start balance', 'Minimum stock', 'Active'];
var PE_HEAD_ = ['Employee code', 'Employee name', 'Current site', 'Added by', 'Added on'];
var PS_HEAD_ = ['Ref', 'Date', 'Time', 'Employee code', 'Employee name', 'Site', 'Item code', 'Item name', 'Type', 'Category', 'Size', 'Qty', 'Unit', 'Remarks',
  'Issued by', 'Status', 'Early reason', 'Approved by', 'Approval file', 'Evidence files', 'Note', 'Cancelled by', 'Cancelled time', 'Cancel reason'];
var PP_HEAD_ = ['Ref', 'PO no', 'Received date', 'Item code', 'Item name', 'Type', 'Category', 'Size', 'Qty', 'Remarks', 'Entered by', 'Status', 'Time', 'Cancelled by'];
var P_DEF_ = { site: 'MUSAFFAH STORE', months: { 'COVER ALL': 3, 'SAFETY JACKET': 3, 'SAFETY SHOES': 3, 'HELMET': 12 }, other: 3, approver: 'Mr. Wissam Al Hadwi', file: '' };
var P_REASONS_ = ['Torn / damaged', 'Lost', 'Wrong size', 'Other'];

function pTab_(ss, name, head, textCols) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  if (sh) return sh;
  sh = ss.insertSheet(name);
  (textCols || []).forEach(function (c) { sh.getRange(c + ':' + c).setNumberFormat('@'); });
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setValues([head]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground(P_COL_).setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  try { sh.setTabColor(P_COL_); } catch (e) {}
  return sh;
}
function piSheet_(ss) { return pTab_(ss, PI_TAB_, PI_HEAD_, ['A', 'E']); }
function peSheet_(ss) { return pTab_(ss, PE_TAB_, PE_HEAD_, ['A', 'E']); }
function psSheet_(ss) { return pTab_(ss, PS_TAB_, PS_HEAD_, ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'F', 'G', 'K']); }
function ppSheet_(ss) { return pTab_(ss, PP_TAB_, PP_HEAD_, ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'H']); }

function pSet_() {
  var o = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(P_DEF_));
  try {
    var s = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('WTC_PPE') || '{}');
    ['site', 'approver', 'file', 'other'].forEach(function (k) { if (s[k] != null && s[k] !== '') o[k] = s[k]; });
    if (s.months) Object.keys(s.months).forEach(function (k) { o.months[k] = s.months[k]; });
  } catch (e) {}
  return o;
}
function pSaveSet_(o) { PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('WTC_PPE', JSON.stringify(o)); }
/** The site that runs the PPE store (as written in the Sites tab), or '' if it is not a site. */
function pSite_(ss) {
  var want = String(pSet_().site || '').trim().toLowerCase(), s = '';
  sitesList_(ss).forEach(function (x) { if (x.toLowerCase() === want) s = x; });
  return s;
}
function pOn_(ss) { var sh = ss.getSheetByName(PI_TAB_); return !!(sh && sh.getLastRow() > 1 && pSite_(ss)); }
function pDate_(v) { var s = dstr_(v); return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s) ? s.slice(0, 10) : s; }
function pNum_(v) { var n = Number(String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/,/g, '')); return isFinite(n) ? n : 0; }
function pAddM_(ds, m) {
  var p = String(ds).split('-'), y = +p[0], mo = +p[1] - 1 + Number(m || 0), d = +p[2];
  var first = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo, 1)), last = new Date(Date.UTC(first.getUTCFullYear(), first.getUTCMonth() + 1, 0)).getUTCDate();
  first.setUTCDate(Math.min(d, last));
  return fmtUTC_(first);
}
function pMonths_(set, type) { var m = set.months[String(type || '').toUpperCase()]; return m != null && m !== '' ? Number(m) : Number(set.other || 3); }

/** One time: the masks were not in the PPE list – remove Mask rows that were never issued or bought. */
function pNoMask_(ss, sh) {
  var P = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  if (P.getProperty('PPE_NO_MASK')) return;
  try {
    var n = sh.getLastRow(); if (n < 2) return;
    var used = {}, a = ss.getSheetByName(PS_TAB_), b = ss.getSheetByName(PP_TAB_);
    if (a && a.getLastRow() > 1) a.getRange(2, 7, a.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().forEach(function (r) { used[String(r[0]).trim()] = 1; });
    if (b && b.getLastRow() > 1) b.getRange(2, 4, b.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().forEach(function (r) { used[String(r[0]).trim()] = 1; });
    var v = sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, 3).getValues();
    for (var i = v.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if ((/mask/i.test(String(v[i][2])) || /\bmask\b/i.test(String(v[i][1]))) && !used[String(v[i][0]).trim()]) sh.deleteRow(i + 2);
    P.setProperty('PPE_NO_MASK', nNow_());
  } catch (e) { console.error('PPE mask cleanup: ' + e); }
}
function pItems_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.pItems) return MEMO_.pItems;
  var sh = piSheet_(ss);
  pNoMask_(ss, sh);
  var n = sh.getLastRow(), out = [];
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, PI_HEAD_.length).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    var code = String(r[0]).trim(); if (!code) return;
    out.push({ row: i + 2, code: code, name: String(r[1]).trim(), type: String(r[2]).trim().toUpperCase(), cat: String(r[3]).trim().toUpperCase(), size: String(r[4]).trim(),
      unit: String(r[5]).trim() || 'PIECE', start: pNum_(r[6]), min: r[7] === '' || r[7] == null ? '' : pNum_(r[7]), active: !/^(no|n|0|false)$/i.test(String(r[8]).trim()) });
  });
  return (MEMO_.pItems = out);
}
function pEmps_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.pEmps) return MEMO_.pEmps;
  var sh = peSheet_(ss), n = sh.getLastRow(), out = [];
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, PE_HEAD_.length).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    var code = String(r[0]).trim().toUpperCase(); if (!code) return;
    out.push({ row: i + 2, code: code, name: String(r[1]).trim(), site: String(r[2]).trim() });
  });
  return (MEMO_.pEmps = out);
}
function pIss_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.pIss) return MEMO_.pIss;
  var sh = psSheet_(ss), n = sh.getLastRow(), out = [];
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, PS_HEAD_.length).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    var ref = String(r[0]).trim(); if (!ref) return;
    out.push({ row: i + 2, ref: ref, date: pDate_(r[1]), time: xTime_(r[2]), ecode: String(r[3]).trim().toUpperCase(), ename: String(r[4]).trim(), site: String(r[5]).trim(),
      code: String(r[6]).trim(), item: String(r[7]).trim(), type: String(r[8]).trim().toUpperCase(), cat: String(r[9]).trim(), size: String(r[10]).trim(), qty: pNum_(r[11]),
      unit: String(r[12]).trim(), rem: String(r[13]).trim(), by: String(r[14]).trim(), status: String(r[15]).trim() || 'Issued', early: String(r[16]).trim(),
      appr: String(r[17]).trim(), apprFile: String(r[18]).trim(), ev: String(r[19]).trim(), note: String(r[20]).trim(), cxBy: String(r[21]).trim(), cxTime: String(r[22]).trim(), cxWhy: String(r[23]).trim() });
  });
  return (MEMO_.pIss = out);
}
function pPur_(ss) {
  if (MEMO_.pPur) return MEMO_.pPur;
  var sh = ppSheet_(ss), n = sh.getLastRow(), out = [];
  if (n > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, PP_HEAD_.length).getValues().forEach(function (r, i) {
    var ref = String(r[0]).trim(); if (!ref) return;
    out.push({ row: i + 2, ref: ref, po: String(r[1]).trim(), date: pDate_(r[2]), code: String(r[3]).trim(), item: String(r[4]).trim(), type: String(r[5]).trim(), cat: String(r[6]).trim(),
      size: String(r[7]).trim(), qty: pNum_(r[8]), rem: String(r[9]).trim(), by: String(r[10]).trim(), status: String(r[11]).trim() || 'Recorded', time: xTime_(r[12]), cxBy: String(r[13]).trim() });
  });
  return (MEMO_.pPur = out);
}
function pReset_() { MEMO_.pItems = MEMO_.pEmps = MEMO_.pIss = MEMO_.pPur = null; }
/** Balance per item code (start + new purchases − new issues). */
function pBal_(ss) {
  var b = {};
  pItems_(ss).forEach(function (i) { b[i.code] = i.start; });
  pPur_(ss).forEach(function (p) { if (p.status === 'Recorded' && b[p.code] != null) b[p.code] = r3_(b[p.code] + p.qty); });
  pIss_(ss).forEach(function (s) { if (s.status === 'Issued' && b[s.code] != null) b[s.code] = r3_(b[s.code] - s.qty); });
  return b;
}
/** Last issue per employee and PPE type: {code: {TYPE: [date, item]}} (cancelled rows do not count). */
function pLast_(ss) {
  var o = {};
  pIss_(ss).forEach(function (s) {
    if (s.status === 'Cancelled' || !s.ecode || !s.type) return;
    var e = o[s.ecode] || (o[s.ecode] = {}), l = e[s.type];
    if (!l || s.date >= l[0]) e[s.type] = [s.date, s.item];
  });
  return o;
}
/* View-only PPE logins (e.g. the CAMP BOSS): tab “PPE Viewers” – Name · PIN. They see stock, issues and history, and change nothing. */
var PV_SITE_ = 'PPE VIEW', PV_TAB_ = 'PPE Viewers';
function pvSheet_(ss) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(PV_TAB_);
  if (sh) return sh;
  sh = pTab_(ss, PV_TAB_, ['Name', 'PIN', 'Note'], ['B']);
  sh.getRange(2, 1, 1, 3).setValues([['CAMP BOSS', '', 'View only – PPE stock and issues. Type a PIN in column B to switch this login on.']]);
  return sh;
}
/** Sheet menu: makes the “PPE Viewers” tab (if missing) and sets the PIN of the first viewer (CAMP BOSS). */
function ppeViewersMenu() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = pvSheet_(ss), ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  ss.setActiveSheet(sh);
  var name = String(sh.getRange(2, 1).getValue()).trim() || 'CAMP BOSS';
  if (!String(sh.getRange(2, 1).getValue()).trim()) sh.getRange(2, 1).setValue(name);
  var r = ui.prompt('PPE view-only login', 'Type the PIN for “' + name + '” (numbers, e.g. 4444).\nHe signs in on the page with: PPE (view only) › ' + name + ' › this PIN.', ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL);
  if (r.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;
  var pin = String(r.getResponseText() || '').trim();
  if (!/^\d{3,8}$/.test(pin)) { ui.alert('Please type 3 to 8 numbers only. Nothing was changed.'); return; }
  sh.getRange(2, 2).setNumberFormat('@').setValue(pin);
  ui.alert('Done ✓\n\n' + name + ' can now sign in on the stock page:\nchoose “PPE (view only)” › ' + name + ' › PIN ' + pin + '.\n\nMore people: add rows in this tab (Name in A, PIN in B).');
}
function pvRows_(ss) {
  var sh = pvSheet_(ss), n = sh.getLastRow();
  return n > 1 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, 2).getValues().map(function (r) { return [String(r[0]).trim(), String(r[1] == null ? '' : r[1]).trim()]; }).filter(function (r) { return r[0] && r[1]; }) : [];
}
function pvOk_(ss, pin, name) { pin = String(pin || '').trim(); name = String(name || '').trim(); return !!pin && pvRows_(ss).some(function (r) { return r[0] === name && r[1] === pin; }); }
function pWho_(ss, site, pin, name) {
  site = String(site || '').trim(); name = String(name || '').trim();
  if (isAll_(site)) return okOffice_(ss, pin) ? { office: true, name: 'Office' } : { error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  if (site === PV_SITE_) return pvOk_(ss, pin, name) ? { view: true, name: name } : { error: 'Wrong name or PIN' };
  var ps = pSite_(ss);
  if (!ps || site !== ps) return { error: 'PPE is only for ' + (ps || 'the PPE store') + '.' };
  if (!name || !checkPin_(ss, site, pin, name)) return { error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
  return { site: site, name: name };
}
function pTx_(rows) {
  var by = {}, out = [];
  rows.forEach(function (s) {
    var t = by[s.ref];
    if (!t) { t = by[s.ref] = { ref: s.ref, date: s.date, time: s.time, ecode: s.ecode, ename: s.ename, site: s.site, by: s.by, status: s.status, early: '', appr: '', apprFile: '', ev: '', note: s.note, rem: s.rem, cxBy: s.cxBy, cxWhy: s.cxWhy, lines: [] }; out.push(t); }
    if (s.early) { t.early = s.early; t.appr = s.appr; t.apprFile = s.apprFile; t.ev = s.ev; }
    t.lines.push({ code: s.code, item: s.item, type: s.type, size: s.size, qty: s.qty, unit: s.unit });
  });
  return out;
}
function pState_(ss, w) {
  var set = pSet_(), bal = pBal_(ss), t = today_(), iss = pIss_(ss), sites = {};
  var items = pItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.active || w.office; }).map(function (i) {
    return { code: i.code, name: i.name, type: i.type, cat: i.cat, size: i.size, unit: i.unit, bal: bal[i.code] || 0, min: i.min, active: i.active };
  });
  var emps = pEmps_(ss).map(function (e) { if (e.site) sites[e.site] = 1; return { code: e.code, name: e.name, site: e.site }; });
  var recent = pTx_(iss.slice(-600)).reverse().slice(0, 150);
  var types = {}; items.forEach(function (i) { if (i.type) types[i.type] = 1; });
  var months = {}; Object.keys(types).concat(Object.keys(set.months)).forEach(function (k) { months[k] = pMonths_(set, k); });
  var st = {
    ok: true, role: w.office ? 'office' : w.view ? 'view' : 'store', site: pSite_(ss), today: t, items: items, emps: emps, last: pLast_(ss), sites: Object.keys(sites).sort(),
    rules: { months: months, other: Number(set.other || 3), approver: set.approver, reasons: P_REASONS_ }, recent: recent,
    counts: { today: pTx_(iss.filter(function (s) { return s.date === t && s.status === 'Issued'; })).length, issues: iss.filter(function (s) { return s.status !== 'Cancelled'; }).length,
      low: items.filter(function (i) { return i.active && i.min !== '' && i.bal < i.min; }).length, emps: emps.length, purchases: pPur_(ss).filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'Cancelled'; }).length }
  };
  if (w.office) {
    st.early = pTx_(iss.filter(function (s) { return s.early && s.status !== 'Imported'; })).reverse();
    st.purchases = pTx_(pPur_(ss).slice(-400).map(function (p) { return { ref: p.ref, date: p.date, time: p.time, ecode: '', ename: p.po, site: '', by: p.by, status: p.status, note: p.rem, rem: p.rem, cxBy: p.cxBy, code: p.code, item: p.item, type: p.type, size: p.size, qty: p.qty, unit: '' }; })).reverse().slice(0, 80);
    st.allSites = sitesList_(ss);
    st.file = set.file ? 'yes' : '';
  }
  return st;
}
function loadPpe(site, pin, name) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = pWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error) return { ok: false, error: w.error };
  if (!ss.getSheetByName(PI_TAB_)) return { ok: false, error: 'The PPE tabs are not in the sheet yet. Please ask the office.' };
  return pState_(ss, w);
}
function pFolder_(name) { var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(name); return it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(name); }
function pSaveFile_(folder, data, name) {
  var m = String(data || '').match(/^data:([\w\/\-\.+]+);base64,(.+)$/);
  if (!m) return '';
  var ext = /pdf/.test(m[1]) ? '.pdf' : '.jpg';
  return folder.createFile(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(m[2]), m[1], name + ext)).getId();
}
function pIsImg_(x) { return /^data:image\/\w+;base64,/.test(x || '') && String(x).length < 8e6; }
function pIsDoc_(x) { return (/^data:image\/\w+;base64,/.test(x || '') || /^data:application\/pdf;base64,/.test(x || '')) && String(x).length < 8e6; }
function pNextRef_(rows, prefix) { var mx = 0; rows.forEach(function (r) { var m = String(r.ref).match(/(\d+)$/); if (m && String(r.ref).indexOf(prefix) === 0) mx = Math.max(mx, +m[1]); }); return prefix + ('000' + (mx + 1)).slice(-4); }
function pTxt_(v, n) { return String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, n || 200); }

/** Storekeeper: issue PPE to one employee. d = {emp:{code,name,site,isNew}, lines:[{code,qty}], remarks, early:{reason, approved, approval, evidence:[], note}} */
function ppeIssue(site, pin, name, d) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  var w = pWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error) return { ok: false, error: w.error };
  if (w.office || w.view) return { ok: false, error: w.view ? 'View only – you cannot issue PPE.' : 'PPE is issued by the PPE storekeeper. The office can cancel or add stock.' };
  d = d || {};
  var e = d.emp || {}, ecode = pTxt_(e.code, 30).toUpperCase(), ename = pTxt_(e.name, 80), esite = pTxt_(e.site, 40);
  if (!ecode) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose the employee.' };
  var known = pEmps_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.code === ecode; })[0];
  if (!known && !e.isNew) return { ok: false, error: 'Employee ' + ecode + ' is not in the list. Use “New employee”.' };
  if (!known && !ename) return { ok: false, error: 'Type the name of the new employee.' };
  if (known) ename = known.name;
  if (!esite) return { ok: false, error: 'Type the current site of the employee.' };
  var items = {}, want = {}, order = [];
  pItems_(ss).forEach(function (i) { items[i.code] = i; });
  (d.lines || []).forEach(function (l) {
    var c = String(l.code || '').trim(), q = pNum_(l.qty);
    if (!c) return;
    if (!want[c]) { want[c] = 0; order.push(c); }
    want[c] = r3_(want[c] + q);
  });
  if (!order.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Add at least one PPE item.' };
  for (var i = 0; i < order.length; i++) {
    var it = items[order[i]];
    if (!it || !it.active) return { ok: false, error: 'An item was not found. Remove it and add it again.' };
    if (!(want[order[i]] > 0)) return { ok: false, error: 'Type a quantity for ' + it.name + '.' };
  }
  var set = pSet_(), t = today_(), rem = pTxt_(d.remarks, 150);
  var res = withLock_(function () {
    pReset_();
    var bal = pBal_(ss), last = (pLast_(ss)[ecode] || {}), early = {};
    for (var j = 0; j < order.length; j++) {
      var it2 = items[order[j]], b = bal[it2.code] || 0;
      if (b <= 0) return { ok: false, error: it2.name + ': 0 in stock. You cannot issue it. Ask the office to add stock.' };
      if (want[it2.code] > b) return { ok: false, error: 'Only ' + b + ' left of ' + it2.name + '. Change the quantity to ' + b + ' or less.' };
      var l = last[it2.type];
      if (l && t < pAddM_(l[0], pMonths_(set, it2.type))) early[it2.type] = l;
    }
    var et = Object.keys(early), x = d.early || {}, apprId = '', evIds = [], ref = pNextRef_(pIss_(ss), 'PPE-');
    if (et.length) {
      if (P_REASONS_.indexOf(x.reason) < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Early issue: choose why (' + P_REASONS_.join(', ') + ').' };
      if (x.approved !== true) return { ok: false, error: 'Early issue: tick the approval of ' + set.approver + '.' };
      if (!pIsImg_(x.approval)) return { ok: false, error: 'Early issue: add the photo of the approval.' };
      var ev = (x.evidence || []).filter(x.reason === 'Lost' ? pIsDoc_ : pIsImg_).slice(0, 6);
      if (!ev.length) return { ok: false, error: x.reason === 'Lost' ? 'Lost item: add the letter from the Camp Boss.' : 'Early issue: add at least 1 photo of the damaged item.' };
      try {
        var folder = pFolder_('WTC PPE Files'), base = ref + ' ' + ecode;
        apprId = pSaveFile_(folder, x.approval, base + ' approval');
        ev.forEach(function (f, k) { var id = pSaveFile_(folder, f, base + (x.reason === 'Lost' ? ' Camp Boss letter ' : ' damage ') + (k + 1)); if (id) evIds.push(id); });
      } catch (err) { console.error('PPE files: ' + err); }
      if (!apprId || !evIds.length) return { ok: false, error: 'The photos could not be saved. Please try again.' };
    }
    var sh = psSheet_(ss), now = "'" + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'HH:mm'), out = [];
    order.forEach(function (c) {
      var it3 = items[c], isE = !!early[it3.type];
      out.push([ref, t, now, ecode, ename, esite, it3.code, it3.name, it3.type, it3.cat, it3.size, want[c], it3.unit, rem, w.name, 'Issued',
        isE ? x.reason : '', isE ? set.approver : '', isE ? apprId : '', isE ? evIds.join(' ') : '', isE ? pTxt_(x.note, 300) : '', '', '', '']);
    });
    room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + out.length, PS_HEAD_.length);
    sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, out.length, PS_HEAD_.length).setValues(out);
    var es = peSheet_(ss);
    if (!known) { room_(es, es.getLastRow() + 1, PE_HEAD_.length); es.getRange(es.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, PE_HEAD_.length).setValues([[ecode, ename, esite, w.name, t]]); }
    else if (known.site !== esite) es.getRange(known.row, 3).setValue(esite);
    pReset_();
    var b2 = pBal_(ss);
    return { ok: true, ref: ref, early: et.length > 0, msg: ref + (et.length ? ' · EARLY – approved by ' + set.approver : '') + ' · ' + out.length + ' item' + (out.length == 1 ? '' : 's') + ' to ' + ecode + ' ' + ename + ' (' + esite + ') · ' +
      order.map(function (c) { return items[c].name + ': ' + (bal[c] || 0) + ' → ' + (b2[c] || 0); }).join(' · ') };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return res;
  mark_(ss, 'PPE issued ' + res.ref, w.site, w.name);
  pSyncSafe_(ss);
  res.state = pState_(ss, w);
  return res;
}
/** Office: cancel an issue (with a reason). */
function ppeCancel(pin, ref, reason) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Only the office can cancel.' };
  ref = String(ref || '').trim(); reason = pTxt_(reason, 200);
  if (!reason) return { ok: false, error: 'Type the reason for cancelling.' };
  var res = withLock_(function () {
    pReset_();
    var rows = pIss_(ss).filter(function (s) { return s.ref === ref && s.status === 'Issued'; });
    if (!rows.length) return { ok: false, error: ref + ' was not found or is already cancelled.' };
    var sh = psSheet_(ss), now = nNow_();
    rows.forEach(function (s) { sh.getRange(s.row, 16).setValue('Cancelled'); sh.getRange(s.row, 22, 1, 3).setValues([['Office', now, reason]]); });
    pReset_();
    return { ok: true, msg: ref + ' cancelled · ' + rows.length + ' item' + (rows.length == 1 ? '' : 's') + ' back in stock' };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return res;
  pSyncSafe_(ss);
  res.state = pState_(ss, { office: true });
  return res;
}
/** Office: new stock (Purchase IN). d = {po, date, lines:[{code,qty}], remarks} */
function ppePurchase(pin, d) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Only the office adds new PPE stock.' };
  d = d || {};
  var po = pTxt_(d.po, 40), date = pDate_(d.date) || today_(), rem = pTxt_(d.remarks, 150), items = {};
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date) || date > today_()) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose the received date (not in the future).' };
  pItems_(ss).forEach(function (i) { items[i.code] = i; });
  var lines = (d.lines || []).filter(function (l) { return items[String(l.code)] && pNum_(l.qty) > 0; });
  if (!lines.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Add at least one item with a quantity.' };
  var res = withLock_(function () {
    pReset_();
    var bal = pBal_(ss), sh = ppSheet_(ss), ref = pNextRef_(pPur_(ss), 'PIN-'), now = "'" + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'HH:mm');
    var out = lines.map(function (l) { var it = items[String(l.code)]; return [ref, po, date, it.code, it.name, it.type, it.cat, it.size, pNum_(l.qty), rem, 'Office', 'Recorded', now, '']; });
    room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + out.length, PP_HEAD_.length);
    sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, out.length, PP_HEAD_.length).setValues(out);
    pReset_();
    var b2 = pBal_(ss);
    return { ok: true, ref: ref, msg: ref + (po ? ' · PO ' + po : '') + ' · ' + lines.map(function (l) { var c = String(l.code); return items[c].name + ': ' + (bal[c] || 0) + ' → ' + (b2[c] || 0); }).join(' · ') };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return res;
  pSyncSafe_(ss);
  res.state = pState_(ss, { office: true });
  return res;
}
function ppeCancelPurchase(pin, ref, reason) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Only the office can cancel.' };
  ref = String(ref || '').trim(); reason = pTxt_(reason, 150);
  if (!reason) return { ok: false, error: 'Type the reason for cancelling.' };
  var res = withLock_(function () {
    pReset_();
    var rows = pPur_(ss).filter(function (p) { return p.ref === ref && p.status === 'Recorded'; });
    if (!rows.length) return { ok: false, error: ref + ' was not found or is already cancelled.' };
    var bal = pBal_(ss);
    for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) if ((bal[rows[i].code] || 0) - rows[i].qty < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Cannot cancel: ' + rows[i].item + ' was already issued (only ' + (bal[rows[i].code] || 0) + ' left).' };
    var sh = ppSheet_(ss);
    rows.forEach(function (p) { sh.getRange(p.row, 12).setValue('Cancelled'); sh.getRange(p.row, 14).setValue('Office ' + nNow_() + ' – ' + reason); });
    pReset_();
    return { ok: true, msg: ref + ' cancelled' };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return res;
  pSyncSafe_(ss);
  res.state = pState_(ss, { office: true });
  return res;
}
/** Office: settings (months per type, approver, PPE store) and minimum stock. */
function ppeSettings(pin, o) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Only the office can change the PPE settings.' };
  o = o || {};
  var set = pSet_();
  if (o.months) Object.keys(o.months).forEach(function (k) { var n = pNum_(o.months[k]); if (n >= 0 && n <= 60) set.months[String(k).toUpperCase()] = n; });
  if (o.other != null && pNum_(o.other) > 0) set.other = pNum_(o.other);
  if (o.approver) set.approver = pTxt_(o.approver, 60);
  if (o.site) { var s = String(o.site).trim(); if (sitesList_(ss).indexOf(s) < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose a site from the Sites tab.' }; set.site = s; }
  pSaveSet_(set);
  return { ok: true, msg: 'PPE settings saved', state: pState_(ss, { office: true }) };
}
/** Office: minimum stock and show / hide an item for the storekeeper (Active Yes / No). */
function ppeSetItem(pin, code, o) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Only the office can change PPE items.' };
  var it = pItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.code === String(code); })[0];
  if (!it) return { ok: false, error: 'Item not found' };
  o = o || {};
  var sh = piSheet_(ss), v = String(o.min == null ? '' : o.min).trim() === '' ? '' : pNum_(o.min), act = o.active !== false;
  sh.getRange(it.row, 8, 1, 2).setValues([[v, act ? 'Yes' : 'No']]);
  pReset_();
  pSyncSafe_(ss);
  return { ok: true, msg: it.name + ' · ' + (act ? 'shown to the storekeeper' : 'hidden from the storekeeper') + ' · minimum ' + (v === '' ? '–' : v), state: pState_(ss, { office: true }) };
}
function ppeSetMin(pin, code, min) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Only the office can change the minimum stock.' };
  var it = pItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.code === String(code); })[0];
  if (!it) return { ok: false, error: 'Item not found' };
  var v = String(min).trim() === '' ? '' : pNum_(min);
  piSheet_(ss).getRange(it.row, 8).setValue(v);
  pReset_();
  pSyncSafe_(ss);
  return { ok: true, msg: it.name + ': minimum stock ' + (v === '' ? 'cleared' : v), state: pState_(ss, { office: true }) };
}

/* ----- history and “can he get new PPE?” ----- */
function pHist_(ss, f) {
  f = f || {};
  var set = pSet_(), t = today_(), codes = (f.emps || []).map(function (c) { return String(c).trim().toUpperCase(); }).filter(String), site = String(f.site || '').trim();
  var from = pDate_(f.from) || '2000-01-01', to = pDate_(f.to) || t, emps = {}, iss = pIss_(ss);
  pEmps_(ss).forEach(function (e) { emps[e.code] = e; });
  if (!codes.length && site) { var s = {}; iss.forEach(function (r) { if (r.site === site && r.ecode) s[r.ecode] = 1; }); pEmps_(ss).forEach(function (e) { if (e.site === site) s[e.code] = 1; }); codes = Object.keys(s).sort(); }
  if (!codes.length) return { error: 'Choose one or more employees (or a site).' };
  if (codes.length > 150) codes = codes.slice(0, 150);
  var types = ['COVER ALL', 'SAFETY JACKET', 'SAFETY SHOES', 'HELMET'];
  iss.forEach(function (r) { if (r.type && types.indexOf(r.type) < 0 && codes.indexOf(r.ecode) >= 0) types.push(r.type); });
  var out = codes.map(function (c) {
    var all = iss.filter(function (r) { return r.ecode === c; }), e = emps[c] || {};
    var due = types.map(function (ty) {
      var l = null; all.forEach(function (r) { if (r.type === ty && r.status !== 'Cancelled' && (!l || r.date >= l.date)) l = r; });
      var m = pMonths_(set, ty), next = l ? pAddM_(l.date, m) : '';
      return { type: ty, months: m, last: l ? l.date : '', item: l ? l.item : '', next: next, ok: !l || t >= next };
    });
    var rows = all.filter(function (r) { return r.date >= from && r.date <= to; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.date < b.date ? 1 : a.date > b.date ? -1 : 0; })
      .map(function (r) { return { ref: r.ref, date: r.date, item: r.item, type: r.type, size: r.size, qty: r.qty, unit: r.unit, rem: r.rem, status: r.status, early: r.early, appr: r.appr, by: r.by, site: r.site, files: [r.apprFile].concat(String(r.ev || '').split(/\s+/)).filter(String).join(' ') }; });
    return { code: c, name: e.name || (all[0] || {}).ename || '', site: e.site || (all.length ? all[all.length - 1].site : ''), count: all.filter(function (r) { return r.status !== 'Cancelled'; }).length, first: all.length ? all[0].date : '', due: due, rows: rows };
  });
  return { list: out, from: from, to: to, rules: types.map(function (ty) { return [ty, pMonths_(set, ty)]; }), approver: set.approver };
}
function ppeHistory(site, pin, name, f) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = pWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error) return { ok: false, error: w.error };
  var h = pHist_(ss, f);
  if (h.error) return { ok: false, error: h.error };
  h.ok = true; h.today = today_();
  return h;
}
function pFd_(ds) { if (!ds) return '–'; var p = String(ds).split('-'); return +p[2] + ' ' + ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'][+p[1] - 1] + ' ' + p[0]; }
function pTypeName_(t) { return { 'COVER ALL': 'Cover all', 'SAFETY JACKET': 'Safety jacket', 'SAFETY SHOES': 'Safety shoes', 'HELMET': 'Helmet' }[t] || String(t || '').charAt(0) + String(t || '').slice(1).toLowerCase(); }
function pMonthTxt_(m) { return m % 12 === 0 ? (m / 12) + (m === 12 ? ' year' : ' years') : m + ' months'; }
function pPdfHead_(title, right) {
  return '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><style>@page{size:A4;margin:12mm}*{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:9pt;color:#1E2329}' +
    'table{font-size:8.5pt;width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;table-layout:fixed;margin-bottom:3px}thead{display:table-header-group}tr{page-break-inside:avoid}' +
    'th{background:#1E2329;color:#fff;text-align:left;padding:4px 5px;font-size:8pt;vertical-align:top;word-wrap:break-word;overflow-wrap:break-word}' +
    'td.nw{white-space:nowrap}td{font-size:8.5pt;border:1px solid #ddd;padding:3px 5px;vertical-align:top;word-wrap:break-word;overflow-wrap:break-word;white-space:normal}tr.z td{background:#F7F6F2}' +
    'table.band td{background:' + P_COL_ + ';color:#fff;border:0;padding:8px 12px;font-size:13pt;font-weight:bold}table.emh td{background:#F4F3EF;border:0;padding:6px 10px}' +
    '.inf{color:#444;margin:4px 0 6px;line-height:1.45}.ok{color:#1b7a3a;font-weight:bold}.no{color:#9A6200;font-weight:bold}.x{color:#999;text-decoration:line-through}.s{font-size:8pt;color:#777;font-weight:bold;margin:5px 0 2px}</style></head><body>' +
    '<table style="width:auto;table-layout:auto"><tr><td style="border:0;width:58px"><img src="' + LOGO_ + '" style="width:50px;height:50px"></td><td style="border:0"><div style="font-size:15pt;font-weight:bold">INVENTORY REPORT</div>' +
    '<div style="font-weight:bold;color:#C42D4E">WALK THROUGH GENERAL CONTRACTING - SOLE PROPRIETORSHIP LLC</div></td></tr></table>' +
    '<table class="band" style="margin:10px 0 6px"><tr><td style="width:70%">' + title + '</td><td style="width:30%;text-align:right">' + esc_(right) + '</td></tr></table>';
}
function pCols_(w) { return '<colgroup>' + w.map(function (x) { return '<col style="width:' + x + '%">'; }).join('') + '</colgroup>'; }
function pPdfOut_(html, name, office) {
  var pdf = Utilities.newBlob(html, 'text/html', 'ppe.html').getAs('application/pdf').setName(name), url = '';
  if (office) try { url = pFolder_('WTC Stock PDFs').createFile(pdf).getUrl(); } catch (e) {}
  return { ok: true, name: pdf.getName(), url: url, b64: Utilities.base64Encode(pdf.getBytes()) };
}
function ppePdf(site, pin, name, f) {
  try {
    var ss = ss_(), w = pWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
    if (w.error) return { ok: false, error: w.error };
    var h = pHist_(ss, f); if (h.error) return { ok: false, error: h.error };
    var only = String((f || {}).show || '');
    var list = h.list.filter(function (e) { return !only || (only === 'ok' ? e.due.some(function (d) { return d.ok && d.last; }) : e.due.some(function (d) { return !d.ok; })); });
    if (!list.length) return { ok: false, error: 'Nothing to print for this filter.' };
    var html = pPdfHead_('PPE ISSUE HISTORY &amp; NEXT ISSUE DUE', pFd_(today_())) +
      '<div class="inf"><b>Employees:</b> ' + esc_(list.length > 12 ? list.length + ' employees' : list.map(function (e) { return e.code; }).join(', ')) + ' · <b>Period:</b> ' + pFd_(h.from === '2000-01-01' ? '' : h.from).replace('–', 'all') + ' – ' + pFd_(h.to) +
      ' · <b>Timeline:</b> ' + h.rules.map(function (r) { return pTypeName_(r[0]) + ' ' + pMonthTxt_(r[1]); }).join(' · ') + ' · earlier only with approval of ' + esc_(h.approver) + '</div>';
