    list.forEach(function (e) {
      html += '<table class="emh" style="margin:12px 0 5px;border-left:5px solid ' + P_COL_ + '"><tr><td style="width:55%;font-size:11pt;font-weight:bold">' + esc_(e.code + ' · ' + e.name) + '</td><td style="width:45%;text-align:right;color:#555">Current site: ' + esc_(e.site || '–') + ' · ' + e.count + ' issues' + (e.first ? ' since ' + pFd_(e.first) : '') + '</td></tr></table>' +
        '<table>' + pCols_([14, 30, 12, 10, 13, 21]) + '<thead><tr><th>Type</th><th>Last item</th><th>Last issued</th><th>Timeline</th><th>Normal issue from</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
        e.due.filter(function (d) { return d.last || ['COVER ALL', 'SAFETY JACKET', 'SAFETY SHOES', 'HELMET'].indexOf(d.type) >= 0; }).map(function (d) {
          return '<tr><td>' + esc_(pTypeName_(d.type)) + '</td><td>' + esc_(d.item || '–') + '</td><td>' + pFd_(d.last) + '</td><td>' + pMonthTxt_(d.months) + '</td><td>' + (d.last ? pFd_(d.next) : '–') + '</td><td class="' + (d.ok ? 'ok' : 'no') + '">' + (d.ok ? (d.last ? '✔ CAN ISSUE' : '✔ never issued') : '⏳ EARLY – APPROVAL') + '</td></tr>';
        }).join('') + '</tbody></table><div class="s">History ' + (e.rows.length ? '(newest first)' : '– nothing in this period') + '</div>' +
        (e.rows.length ? '<table>' + pCols_([13, 11, 31, 6, 9, 9, 21]) + '<thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Ref</th><th>Item</th><th>Size</th><th>Qty</th><th>Site</th><th>Remarks / approval</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + e.rows.map(function (r, i) {
          var x = r.status === 'Cancelled';
          return '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '><td class="nw' + (x ? ' x' : '') + '">' + pFd_(r.date) + '</td><td class="nw">' + esc_(r.ref) + '</td><td' + (x ? ' class="x"' : '') + '>' + esc_(r.item) + '</td><td>' + esc_(r.size) + '</td><td>' + esc_(r.qty + ' ' + r.unit) + '</td><td>' + esc_(r.site) + '</td><td>' +
            esc_([x ? 'CANCELLED' : '', r.early ? 'EARLY – ' + r.early + ' – approved by ' + r.appr : '', r.rem].filter(String).join(' · ')) + '</td></tr>';
        }).join('') + '</tbody></table>' : '');
    });
    html += '<p style="font-size:7pt;color:#777;margin-top:10px">Printed ' + esc_(Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ_, 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm')) + ' by ' + esc_(w.office ? 'Office' : w.name) + ' from the WTC Inventory Report.</p></body></html>';
    var r = pPdfOut_(html, 'PPE_history_' + today_() + (list.length === 1 ? '_' + list[0].code : '') + '.pdf', w.office);
    r.count = list.length;
    return r;
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: 'Could not create the PDF: ' + e }; }
}
/** Office: early issues between two dates, with the damage photos / letters. */
function ppeEarlyPdf(pin, from, to) {
  try {
    var ss = ss_();
    if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Office only' };
    from = pDate_(from) || '2000-01-01'; to = pDate_(to) || today_();
    var tx = pTx_(pIss_(ss).filter(function (s) { return s.early && s.status !== 'Imported' && s.date >= from && s.date <= to; })).reverse();
    if (!tx.length) return { ok: false, error: 'No early issues in this period.' };
    var pics = 0, img = function (id) {
      if (pics >= 40) return '';
      try { var b = DriveApp.getFileById(id).getBlob(); if (!/^image\//.test(b.getContentType())) return '<span style="display:inline-block;border:1px solid #ccc;padding:14px 6px;margin:2px;font-size:7pt">PDF letter</span>'; pics++; return '<img src="data:' + b.getContentType() + ';base64,' + Utilities.base64Encode(b.getBytes()) + '" style="width:72px;height:60px;object-fit:cover;margin:2px;border:1px solid #ccc">'; } catch (e) { return ''; }
    };
    var html = pPdfHead_('PPE EARLY ISSUES (WITH APPROVAL)', pFd_(from === '2000-01-01' ? '' : from) + ' – ' + pFd_(to)) +
      '<table>' + pCols_([13, 17, 22, 18, 30]) + '<thead><tr><th>Ref · date</th><th>Employee</th><th>Items</th><th>Reason · approval</th><th>Photos / letter</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
      tx.map(function (t, i) {
        var ids = [t.apprFile].concat(String(t.ev || '').split(/\s+/)).filter(String);
        return '<tr' + (i % 2 ? ' class="z"' : '') + '><td><b>' + esc_(t.ref) + '</b><br>' + pFd_(t.date) + '<br>by ' + esc_(t.by) + (t.status === 'Cancelled' ? '<br><b style="color:#B3261E">CANCELLED</b>' : '') + '</td><td>' + esc_(t.ecode + ' ' + t.ename) + '<br>' + esc_(t.site) + '</td><td>' +
          t.lines.map(function (l) { return esc_(l.item + ' ×' + l.qty); }).join('<br>') + '</td><td>' + esc_(t.early) + '<br>Approved by ' + esc_(t.appr) + (t.note ? '<br>' + esc_(t.note) : '') + '</td><td>' + ids.map(img).join('') + '</td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table></body></html>';
    var r = pPdfOut_(html, 'PPE_early_issues_' + today_() + '.pdf', true);
    r.count = tx.length;
    return r;
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: 'Could not create the PDF: ' + e }; }
}
/** Shows an approval photo / damage photo / Camp Boss letter (office, or the PPE store). */
function ppeFile(site, pin, name, id) {
  var ss = ss_(), w = pWho_(ss, site, pin, name);
  if (w.error) return { ok: false, error: w.error };
  id = String(id || '').trim();
  if (!id || !pIss_(ss).some(function (s) { return s.apprFile === id || String(s.ev).split(/\s+/).indexOf(id) >= 0; })) return { ok: false, error: 'File not found' };
  try { var b = DriveApp.getFileById(id).getBlob(); return { ok: true, type: b.getContentType(), src: 'data:' + b.getContentType() + ';base64,' + Utilities.base64Encode(b.getBytes()) }; }
  catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: 'Could not open the file.' }; }
}
/** Office: link of the live PPE file in Drive (made now if it does not exist yet). */
function ppeFileUrl(pin) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Office only' };
  try { var f = pSync_(ss, true); return { ok: true, url: f.getUrl() }; } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: 'Could not open the PPE file: ' + e }; }
}

/* ----- the separate PPE file in Google Drive (same tabs as the old Excel) ----- */
var PX_ISS_HEAD_ = ['S. NO', 'RELEASED DATE', 'EMPLOYEE CODE', 'EMPLOYEE NAME', 'CURRENT SITE', 'ITEM CODE', 'ITEM NAME', 'TYPE OF ITEM', 'CATEGORY', 'SIZE', 'QTY', 'UNIT', 'REMARKS', 'ISSUED BY', 'REF', 'EARLY REASON', 'APPROVED BY', 'STATUS'];
var PX_PUR_HEAD_ = ['S. NO', 'PO NO', 'RECEIVED DATE', 'ITEM CODE', 'ITEM NAME', 'ITEM TYPE', 'CATEGORY', 'SIZE', 'PURCHASED QTY', 'REMARKS', 'REF', 'ENTERED BY', 'STATUS'];
var PX_MAS_HEAD_ = ['S. NO', 'ITEM CODE', 'ITEM NAME', 'ITEM TYPE', 'CATEGORY', 'SIZE', 'UNIT', 'START BALANCE', 'PURCHASED (new)', 'ISSUED (new)', 'BALANCE', 'MINIMUM STOCK TO KEEP', 'PROCUREMENT SUGGESTION', 'ORDER QTY'];
function pFileGet_(ss) {
  var set = pSet_(), f = null;
  if (set.file) try { f = SpreadsheetApp.openById(set.file); } catch (e) { f = null; }
  if (!f) {
    f = SpreadsheetApp.create('PPE Master List – LIVE');
    try { var file = DriveApp.getFileById(f.getId()), folder = pFolder_('WTC PPE'); file.moveTo(folder); } catch (e) { console.error('PPE file folder: ' + e); }
    set.file = f.getId(); pSaveSet_(set);
  }
  return f;
}
function pFileTab_(f, name, head, i) {
  var sh = f.getSheetByName(name);
  if (!sh) { sh = f.insertSheet(name, i); }
  return sh;
}
function pPd_(ds) { try { return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(ds) ? Utilities.parseDate(ds, TZ_, 'yyyy-MM-dd') : ds; } catch (e) { return ds; } }
function pWriteLog_(sh, head, rows, statusCol, full) {
  var n = sh.getLastRow() - 1, w = head.length, okHead = sh.getLastRow() >= 1 && String(sh.getRange(1, 1).getValue()) === head[0] && String(sh.getRange(1, w).getValue()) === head[w - 1];
  if (full || !okHead || n > rows.length || n < 0) {
    sh.clear();
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, w).setValues([head]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground(P_COL_).setFontColor('#FFFFFF');
    sh.setFrozenRows(1); n = 0;
  }
  if (rows.length > n) {
    var add = rows.slice(n);
    if (sh.getMaxRows() < rows.length + 1) sh.insertRowsAfter(sh.getMaxRows(), rows.length + 1 - sh.getMaxRows() + 200);
    sh.getRange(n + 2, 1, add.length, w).setValues(add);
  }
  if (rows.length) sh.getRange(2, statusCol, rows.length, 1).setValues(rows.map(function (r) { return [r[statusCol - 1]]; }));
}
/** Writes the PPE file: new rows are added, status updated, MASTER and Balance rebuilt. Returns the file. */
function pSync_(ss, full) {
  pReset_();
  var f = pFileGet_(ss), bal = pBal_(ss), pur = {}, iss = {};
  pPur_(ss).forEach(function (p) { if (p.status === 'Recorded') pur[p.code] = r3_((pur[p.code] || 0) + p.qty); });
  pIss_(ss).forEach(function (s) { if (s.status === 'Issued') iss[s.code] = r3_((iss[s.code] || 0) + s.qty); });
  var ms = pFileTab_(f, 'MASTER', PX_MAS_HEAD_, 0);
  var mrows = pItems_(ss).map(function (i, k) {
    var b = bal[i.code] || 0, low = i.min !== '' && b < i.min;
    return [k + 1, i.code, i.name, i.type, i.cat, i.size, i.unit, i.start, pur[i.code] || 0, iss[i.code] || 0, b, i.min, i.min === '' ? '' : low ? 'Place Order' : 'Stock Available', low ? r3_(i.min - b) : ''];
  });
  ms.clear(); ms.getRange(1, 1, 1, PX_MAS_HEAD_.length).setValues([PX_MAS_HEAD_]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground(P_COL_).setFontColor('#FFFFFF'); ms.setFrozenRows(1);
  if (mrows.length) { ms.getRange(2, 1, mrows.length, PX_MAS_HEAD_.length).setValues(mrows); ms.getRange(2, 2, mrows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('0'); }
  var ps = pFileTab_(f, 'Purchase-IN', PX_PUR_HEAD_, 1);
  pWriteLog_(ps, PX_PUR_HEAD_, pPur_(ss).map(function (p, k) { return [k + 1, p.po, pPd_(p.date), p.code, p.item, p.type, p.cat, p.size, p.qty, p.rem, p.ref, p.by, p.status]; }), PX_PUR_HEAD_.length, full);
  var is = pFileTab_(f, 'Issued-OUT', PX_ISS_HEAD_, 2);
  pWriteLog_(is, PX_ISS_HEAD_, pIss_(ss).map(function (s, k) { return [k + 1, pPd_(s.date), s.ecode, s.ename, s.site, s.code, s.item, s.type, s.cat, s.size, s.qty, s.unit, s.rem, s.by, s.ref, s.early, s.appr, s.status === 'Imported' ? 'Issued (Excel)' : s.status]; }), PX_ISS_HEAD_.length, full);
  try { if (is.getLastRow() > 1) is.getRange(2, 2, is.getLastRow() - 1, 1).setNumberFormat('dd-MMM-yyyy'); if (ps.getLastRow() > 1) ps.getRange(2, 3, ps.getLastRow() - 1, 1).setNumberFormat('dd-MMM-yyyy'); } catch (e) {}
  var es = pFileTab_(f, 'Employees', PE_HEAD_, 3);
  var er = pEmps_(ss).map(function (e) { return [e.code, e.name, e.site]; });
  es.clear(); es.getRange(1, 1, 1, 3).setValues([['EMPLOYEE CODE', 'EMPLOYEE NAME', 'CURRENT SITE']]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground(P_COL_).setFontColor('#FFFFFF'); es.setFrozenRows(1);
  if (er.length) es.getRange(2, 1, er.length, 3).setValues(er);
  try { var s1 = f.getSheetByName('Sheet1'); if (s1 && f.getSheets().length > 1) f.deleteSheet(s1); } catch (e) {}
  try { PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('WTC_PPE_SYNC', nNow_()); } catch (e) {}
  return f;
}
function pSyncSafe_(ss) { try { pSync_(ss, false); } catch (e) { console.error('PPE file: ' + e); try { PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('WTC_PPE_SYNC', 'failed ' + nNow_() + ': ' + e); } catch (x) {} } }
/** Menu: rebuild the PPE file in Drive from the sheet. */
function ppeRebuildFile() {
  var ss = ss_(), f = pSync_(ss, true);
  try { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('PPE file updated:\n' + f.getUrl()); } catch (e) {}
  return f.getUrl();
}

/* ======================= Logins: who signed in, when, and for how long =======================
 * "Logins" tab: one row per sign-in. The page reports every 2 minutes while it is open, so
 * "Minutes used" stays right even if someone closes the page without signing out.
 * "Login Summary" tab: logins, total and average minutes per storekeeper (formulas, updates by itself).
 */
var LG_HEAD_ = ['Session', 'Login time', 'Site', 'Storekeeper', 'Last activity', 'Logout time', 'How it ended', 'Minutes used', 'Actions saved', 'Device'];
var LG_ACTIVE_ = 'Still signed in', LG_STALE_MIN_ = 10;
function loginSheet_(ss) {
  var sh = tab_(ss, 'Logins', LG_HEAD_, []);
  if (!ss.getSheetByName('Login Summary')) {
    var sm = ss.insertSheet('Login Summary');
    sm.getRange(1, 1).setValue('Login summary per storekeeper (updates by itself from the Logins tab)');
    sm.getRange(1, 1).setFontWeight('bold');
    sm.getRange(3, 1).setFormula('=IFERROR(QUERY(Logins!A1:J,"select C, D, count(A), sum(H), avg(H), max(B), sum(I) where A <> \'\' group by C, D order by C, D ' +
      'label C \'Site\', D \'Storekeeper\', count(A) \'Logins\', sum(H) \'Total minutes\', avg(H) \'Average minutes per login\', max(B) \'Last login\', sum(I) \'Actions saved\'",1),"No logins yet")');
    sm.getRange('E:E').setNumberFormat('0.0');
    sm.getRange('F:F').setNumberFormat('dd mmm yyyy hh:mm');
    sm.setFrozenRows(3);
  }
  return sh;
}
function sessRow_(sh, sid) {
  if (!sid || sh.getLastRow() < 2) return 0;
  var c = sh.getRange(2, 1, sh.getLastRow() - 1, 1).createTextFinder(String(sid)).matchEntireCell(true).findNext();
  return c ? c.getRow() : 0;
}
function mins_(a, b) { return Math.max(0, Math.round((b.getTime() - a.getTime()) / 6000) / 10); }

/** Called when someone opens their stock (or ALL SITES). Returns a session id the page reports with. */
function startSession(site, pin, name, device) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  site = String(site || '').trim(); name = String(name || '').trim();
  var office = isAll_(site), xs = !office && !siteNames_(ss)[site] && xIs_(ss, site);
  if (site === PV_SITE_) { if (!pvOk_(ss, pin, name)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong name or PIN' }; }
  else if (xs) { var xe = xCheck_(ss, site, pin, name); if (xe) return { ok: false, error: xe }; }
  else {
  if (office ? !okOffice_(ss, pin) : !checkPin_(ss, site, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong site or PIN' };
  if (!office && (siteNames_(ss)[site] || []).indexOf(name) < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Choose your name from the list' };
  if (!office && !checkPin_(ss, site, pin, name)) return { ok: false, error: 'This PIN is not for ' + name + '. Type your own PIN.' };
  }
  return withLock_(function () {
    var sh = loginSheet_(ss), now = new Date();
    var sid = 'L' + Utilities.formatDate(now, TZ_, 'yyMMddHHmmss') + Math.floor(Math.random() * 900 + 100);
    room_(sh, sh.getLastRow() + 1, 10);
    sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, 10).setValues([[sid, now, office ? ALL_ : site, office ? 'Office' : name, now, '', LG_ACTIVE_, 0, 0,
      String(device || '').slice(0, 60)]]);
    try { sweepSessions_(ss); } catch (e) {}
    return { ok: true, sid: sid };
  });
}

/** The page reports every 2 minutes (and after each save) while it is open. */
function pingSession(sid, actions) {
  var ss = ss_(), sh = loginSheet_(ss), r = sessRow_(sh, sid);
  if (!r) return { ok: false };
  var v = sh.getRange(r, 1, 1, 10).getValues()[0];
  if (v[6] !== LG_ACTIVE_ && v[6] !== 'Left without signing out') return { ok: true, ended: true };
  var now = new Date(), start = v[1] instanceof Date ? v[1] : new Date(v[1]);
  sh.getRange(r, 5, 1, 5).setValues([[now, '', LG_ACTIVE_, mins_(start, now), Math.max(Number(v[8]) || 0, Number(actions) || 0)]]);
  return { ok: true };
}

/** how = 'Signed out' (button) or 'Closed the page'. */
function endSession(sid, how, actions) {
  var ss = ss_(), sh = loginSheet_(ss), r = sessRow_(sh, sid);
  if (!r) return { ok: false };
  var v = sh.getRange(r, 1, 1, 10).getValues()[0];
  if (/^Signed out/.test(String(v[6]))) return { ok: true };
  var now = new Date(), start = v[1] instanceof Date ? v[1] : new Date(v[1]);
  how = how === 'Signed out' ? 'Signed out' : how === 'Timed out' ? 'Signed out (15 min no activity)' : 'Closed the page';
  sh.getRange(r, 5, 1, 5).setValues([[now, now, how, mins_(start, now), Math.max(Number(v[8]) || 0, Number(actions) || 0)]]);
  return { ok: true };
}

/** Sessions with no report for 10 minutes: page left open-and-forgotten or phone switched off. */
function sweepSessions_(ss) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Logins');
  if (!sh || sh.getLastRow() < 2) return;
  var n = sh.getLastRow() - 1, from = Math.max(0, n - 300), vals = sh.getRange(2 + from, 1, n - from, 7).getValues(), now = new Date().getTime();
  vals.forEach(function (v, i) {
    var last = v[4] instanceof Date ? v[4].getTime() : new Date(v[4]).getTime();
    if (v[6] === LG_ACTIVE_ && isFinite(last) && now - last > LG_STALE_MIN_ * 60000) {
      sh.getRange(2 + from + i, 6, 1, 2).setValues([[v[4], 'Left without signing out']]);
    }
  });
}

/* ================= 🤖 AI voice assistant (ALL SITES page only) =================
 * Uses Google Gemini (free tier). The key is kept in Script properties (GEMINI_KEY) – set it with
 * WTC Stock › 🤖 AI voice assistant – set Gemini key. Read only: it never changes anything.
 */
var AI_KEY_PROP_ = 'GEMINI_KEY', AI_PICK_PROP_ = 'WTC_AI_MODELS', AI_VOICES_ = ['Kore', 'Aoede', 'Leda', 'Zephyr', 'Charon', 'Puck', 'Orus', 'Fenrir'];
var AI_BASE_ = 'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/';
function aiKey_() { return String(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(AI_KEY_PROP_) || '').trim(); }
function aiFetch_(path, body) {
  var o = { method: body ? 'post' : 'get', contentType: 'application/json', muteHttpExceptions: true, headers: { 'x-goog-api-key': aiKey_() } };
  if (body) o.payload = JSON.stringify(body);
  var r = UrlFetchApp.fetch(AI_BASE_ + path, o), j = {};
  try { j = JSON.parse(r.getContentText()); } catch (e) {}
  return { code: r.getResponseCode(), j: j };
}
function aiErr_(r) {
  var m = String((r.j && r.j.error && r.j.error.message) || ''), c = r.code;
  if (c === 400 && /api key/i.test(m) || c === 401 || c === 403 && /key/i.test(m)) return 'The Gemini key is not valid. Set it again: WTC Stock › 🤖 AI voice assistant – set Gemini key.';
  if (c === 429) return 'The free AI limit is used up for now. Please try again later.';
  if (c >= 500) return 'The AI service is busy. Please try again in a minute.';
  return 'AI error ' + c + (m ? ': ' + m.slice(0, 160) : '');
}
function aiRank_(n) {
  var m = String(n).match(/gemini-(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/), v = m ? parseFloat(m[1]) * 100 : 0;
  if (/lite/.test(n)) v -= 40;
  if (/preview|exp/.test(n)) v -= 150;   // stable models first – previews are often busy
  if (/latest/.test(n)) v -= 5;
  if (/pro/.test(n)) v -= 30;
  return v;
}
/** Keeps the model from "thinking" for long – simple data questions do not need it and it makes answers slow. */
function aiThink_(m) {
  if (/gemini-2\.5-flash/.test(m)) return { thinkingBudget: 0 };
  if (/gemini-[3-9]/.test(m)) return { thinkingLevel: 'low' };
  return null;
}
/** Finds the best Gemini models this key can use (checked once a day, so it keeps working when Google renames models). */
function aiModels_(force) {
  var p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(), c = null;
  try { c = JSON.parse(p.getProperty(AI_PICK_PROP_) || 'null'); } catch (e) {}
  if (c && !force && Date.now() - c.t < 864e5 && c.text.length) return c;
  var names = [], tok = '', guard = 0;
  do {
    var r = aiFetch_('models?pageSize=1000' + (tok ? '&pageToken=' + encodeURIComponent(tok) : ''));
    if (r.code !== 200) throw new Error(aiErr_(r));
    (r.j.models || []).forEach(function (m) { if ((m.supportedGenerationMethods || []).indexOf('generateContent') >= 0) names.push(String(m.name).replace(/^models\//, '')); });
    tok = r.j.nextPageToken || '';
  } while (tok && ++guard < 5);
  var bad = /(tts|image|live|audio|embed|vision|robotics|computer|native|transcrib|aqa|learnlm|thinking)/i;
  var text = names.filter(function (n) { return /^gemini-/.test(n) && /flash/.test(n) && !bad.test(n); }).sort(function (a, b) { return aiRank_(b) - aiRank_(a); });
  var tts = names.filter(function (n) { return /^gemini-/.test(n) && /tts/.test(n); }).sort(function (a, b) { return aiRank_(b) - aiRank_(a); });
  c = { t: Date.now(), text: text.slice(0, 4), tts: tts.slice(0, 3) };
  p.setProperty(AI_PICK_PROP_, JSON.stringify(c));
  return c;
}
/** All the data the assistant may use, as plain text. */
function aiContext_(ss) {
  var t = today_(), L = [], sites = sitesList_(ss), since = nAddDays_(t, -45), cut = function (a, n) { return a.length > n ? a.slice(0, n) : a; };
  var sec = function (title, fn) { try { var a = fn() || []; L.push('', '## ' + title); if (!a.length) L.push('(none)'); else L.push.apply(L, a); } catch (e) { console.error('AI data ' + title + ': ' + e); } };
  L.push('TODAY: ' + t + ' (' + ['Sunday', 'Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday'][nDow_(t)] + '). Week starts Monday ' + mondayOf_(t) + '.');
  L.push('SITES: ' + sites.join(', '));
  try { L.push('EXCESS STORES: ' + xStores_(ss).join(', ')); } catch (e) {}
  var items = itemsById_(ss), rows = ledger_(ss);
  sec('SITE STOCK NOW (item [category] | unit | qty at each site | total)', function () {
    var q = stockAsOf_(rows, '', null), out = [];
    Object.keys(q).forEach(function (id) {
      var it = items[id], tot = 0, parts = Object.keys(q[id]).filter(function (s) { return sites.indexOf(s) >= 0 && q[id][s][0] !== ''; }).map(function (s) { tot += Number(q[id][s][0]) || 0; return s + ' ' + q[id][s][0]; });
      if (parts.length) out.push((it ? String(it[1]) + ' [' + String(it[2]) + ']' : 'Item ' + id) + ' | ' + (it ? String(it[4]) : '') + ' | ' + parts.join('; ') + ' | total ' + r3_(tot));
    });
    return out;
  });
  sec('LAST STOCK ENTRY DATE PER SITE', function () {
    var d = {}; rows.forEach(function (r) { var s = String(r[2]).trim(), x = dstr_(r[1]); if (!d[s] || x > d[s]) d[s] = x; });
    return sites.map(function (s) { return s + ': ' + (d[s] || 'never'); });
  });
  Object.keys(MV_).forEach(function (k) {
    sec(MV_[k].word.toUpperCase() + ' ENTRIES, LAST 45 DAYS (date | site | ref | ' + MV_[k].i1 + ' | items)', function () {
      return cut(mvList_(ss, k).filter(function (g) { return g.date >= since && !/cancel/i.test(g.status); }).reverse().map(function (g) {
        return g.date + ' | ' + g.site + ' | ' + g.ref + ' | ' + g.info1 + ' | ' + g.lines.map(function (l) { return l.item + ' ' + l.qty + ' ' + l.unit; }).join(', ') + (g.by ? ' | by ' + g.by : '');
      }), 400);
    });
  });
  sec('TRANSFERS (last 45 days or still open: date | id | from → to | items + status)', function () {
    return cut(trList_(ss).filter(function (x) { return x.date >= since || x.lines.some(function (l) { return !/Received|Cancel/i.test(l.status) || l.pending; }); }).reverse().map(function (x) {
      return x.date + ' | ' + x.id + ' | ' + x.from + ' → ' + x.to + ' | ' + x.lines.map(function (l) { return l.item + ' ' + l.qty + ' ' + l.unit + ' (' + (l.status || 'In transit') + (l.missing ? ', ' + l.missing + ' missing' : '') + ')'; }).join(', ');
    }), 300);
  });
  sec('WEEKLY STOCK CHECK – THIS WEEK', function () {
    var w = nWeekTable_(ss); return w.rows.map(function (r) { return r.site + ': ' + (r.done ? 'done ' + r.done + (r.by ? ' by ' + r.by : '') : 'NOT done') + (r.rem ? ' (reminders: ' + r.rem + ')' : ''); });
  });
  sec('OPEN NOTICES & TASKS FROM THE OFFICE (type | title | to | due | who has not finished)', function () {
    return cut(ntList_(ss).filter(function (n) { return n.status === 'Open'; }).reverse().map(function (n) {
      var rec = nRecipients_(ss, n.to), left = rec.filter(function (s) { return nState_(ss, n, s).st !== 'done'; });
      return n.type + ' | ' + n.title + ' | ' + nToText_(n.to) + ' | ' + (n.due ? 'due ' + n.due + (n.due < t && left.length ? ' LATE' : '') : 'no due date') + ' | ' + (left.length ? 'not finished: ' + left.join(', ') : 'all finished') + ' | sent ' + n.created;
    }), 120);
  });
  sec('ASSISTANT CHECK – THINGS THAT NEED ATTENTION', function () {
    var a = asRun_(ss, false, false); return a.find.map(function (f) { return f.site + ': ' + f.text; });
  });
  sec('RENTED MACHINES ON HIRE (site | machine | company | since | expected return | rate)', function () {
    return rnList_(ss).filter(function (o) { return o.status === 'On hire'; }).map(function (o) {
      return o.site + ' | ' + o.machine + (o.size ? ' ' + o.size : '') + ' x' + o.qty + ' | ' + o.company + ' | since ' + o.on + ' | ' + (o.exp ? 'expected back ' + o.exp + (o.exp < t ? ' (OVERDUE)' : '') : 'no return date') + ' | ' + (o.rate ? o.rate + ' AED per ' + o.per : '');
    });
  });
  sec('EXCESS MATERIALS IN STORE (code | name | size/colour | condition | store · location | qty in store | where it came from | issued to)', function () {
    var ob = function (o) { return Object.keys(o).map(function (k) { return k + ' ' + o[k]; }).join(', '); };
    return xState_(ss).cards.map(function (c) {
      return c.code + ' | ' + c.name + (c.brand ? ' (' + c.brand + ')' : '') + ' | ' + [c.size, c.colour].filter(String).join(' ') + ' | ' + c.cond + ' | ' + [c.store, c.loc].filter(String).join(' · ') + ' | ' + c.total + ' ' + c.unit + ' | from ' + ob(c.src) + ' | ' + (Object.keys(c.dst).length ? 'issued ' + ob(c.dst) : 'not issued');
    });
  });
  sec('EXCESS MOVES, LAST 45 DAYS (date | move | code | item | qty | from → to)', function () {
    return cut(xMoves_(ss).filter(function (m) { return m.date >= since && !/cancel/i.test(m.status); }).reverse().map(function (m) { return m.date + ' | ' + m.move + ' | ' + m.code + ' | ' + m.item + ' | ' + m.qty + ' ' + m.unit + ' | ' + m.from + ' → ' + m.to; }), 300);
  });
  sec('OTHER ITEMS KEPT AT SITES (site | item | size | qty | condition | whose | location)', function () {
    return siItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.status === 'OK'; }).map(function (i) { return i.site + ' | ' + i.name + ' | ' + i.size + ' | ' + i.qty + ' ' + i.unit + ' | ' + i.cond + ' | ' + i.whose + ' | ' + i.loc; });
  });
  sec('PPE STOCK (item | type | size | balance | minimum)', function () {
    var b = pBal_(ss); return pItems_(ss).filter(function (i) { return i.active; }).map(function (i) { var x = b[i.code] || 0; return i.name + ' | ' + i.type + ' | ' + i.size + ' | ' + x + ' ' + i.unit + ' | ' + (i.min !== '' ? 'min ' + i.min + (x < i.min ? ' LOW' : '') : '') + (x <= 0 ? ' OUT OF STOCK' : ''); });
  });
  sec('PPE RULES', function () { var s = pSet_(); return ['Store: ' + pSite_(ss), 'Months between issues: ' + JSON.stringify(s.months) + ', other types ' + s.other, 'Early issue needs approval of ' + s.approver]; });
  sec('PPE ISSUED, LAST 90 DAYS (date | ref | employee code · name | site | items | early reason · approved by)', function () {
    var f = nAddDays_(t, -90);
    return cut(pTx_(pIss_(ss).filter(function (s) { return s.date >= f && s.status !== 'Cancelled'; })).reverse().map(function (x) {
      return x.date + ' | ' + x.ref + ' | ' + x.ecode + ' · ' + x.ename + ' | ' + x.site + ' | ' + x.lines.map(function (l) { return l.item + ' ' + l.size + ' x' + l.qty; }).join(', ') + (x.early ? ' | EARLY: ' + x.early + ' · ' + x.appr : '');
    }), 500);
  });
  sec('LAST PPE PER EMPLOYEE (code · name: type date)', function () {
    var last = pLast_(ss), nm = {}; pEmps_(ss).forEach(function (e) { nm[e.code] = e.name; });
    return cut(Object.keys(last).map(function (k) { return k + ' · ' + (nm[k] || '') + ': ' + Object.keys(last[k]).map(function (ty) { return ty + ' ' + last[k][ty][0]; }).join('; '); }), 1500);
  });
  var s = L.join('\n');
  return s.length > 400000 ? s.slice(0, 400000) + '\n(data cut here – too long)' : s;
}
/** The data text, kept ready for 5 minutes so questions answer fast. */
function aiCtx_(ss, fresh) {
  var c = CacheService.getScriptCache(), n = Number(c.get('aiCtx_n') || 0);
  if (n && !fresh) {
    var keys = []; for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) keys.push('aiCtx_' + i);
    var got = c.getAll(keys), parts = keys.map(function (k) { return got[k]; });
    if (parts.every(function (x) { return typeof x === 'string'; })) return parts.join('');
  }
  var t = aiContext_(ss), o = {}, k = 0;
  for (var j = 0; j < t.length; j += 30000) o['aiCtx_' + (k++)] = t.slice(j, j + 30000);
  o.aiCtx_n = String(k);
  try { c.putAll(o, 300); } catch (e) {}
  return t;
}
/** Called when the assistant opens: gets the data and models ready in the background. */
function aiWarm(pin) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin) || !aiKey_()) return { ok: false };
  try { aiModels_(false); aiCtx_(ss, true); } catch (e) { return { ok: false }; }
  return { ok: true };
}
/** The page asks a question (office PIN only). history = [{q, a}] of the last turns. */
function aiAsk(pin, question, history, audio) {
  var ss = ss_();
  ensure_(ss);
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  if (!aiKey_()) return { ok: false, setup: true, error: 'The AI assistant is not set up yet. In the Google Sheet open WTC Stock › 🤖 AI voice assistant – set Gemini key.' };
  question = String(question || '').trim().slice(0, 1000);
  var au = audio && audio.data && /^audio\//.test(String(audio.mime || '')) ? audio : null;
  if (au && String(au.data).length > 6e6) return { ok: false, error: 'That recording is too long. Please ask in under a minute.' };
  if (!question && !au) return { ok: false, error: 'Ask a question first.' };
  var models;
  try { models = aiModels_(false); } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: String(e.message || e) }; }
  if (!models.text.length) return { ok: false, error: 'No Gemini text model is available for this key.' };
  var sys = [
    'You are the voice assistant inside the WTC Inventory Report web page of Walk Through General Contracting (UAE). You talk to the Inventory Manager on the ALL SITES office page.',
    'Answer ONLY from the DATA below. Never invent items, numbers, names or dates. If the data does not contain the answer, say so plainly and suggest which page or tab to check.',
    'Copy numbers exactly from the data. When you add numbers, do it carefully. Dates in the data are YYYY-MM-DD.',
    'Reply in the language the manager used (English, Urdu, Hindi, Arabic or Bengali). Urdu must be written in Urdu script.',
    'Return JSON: "say" = the spoken answer, natural and friendly, short (normally under 60 words, at most 120), no markdown, no emoji, no lists, say dates like "4 November"; "show" = up to 12 short lines with the exact figures you used (item – site – quantity – date), so the manager can check them; empty if there are no figures.',
    'You can only read. If asked to change, add, issue or delete something, explain that this must be done on the page itself.',
    '', 'DATA:', aiCtx_(ss, false)
  ].join('\n');
  var contents = [];
  (history || []).slice(-6).forEach(function (h) {
    if (h && h.q && h.a) { contents.push({ role: 'user', parts: [{ text: String(h.q).slice(0, 1000) }] }); contents.push({ role: 'model', parts: [{ text: String(h.a).slice(0, 2000) }] }); }
  });
  if (au) contents.push({ role: 'user', parts: [{ inlineData: { mimeType: String(au.mime).split(';')[0], data: String(au.data) } },
    { text: 'The manager asked this by voice (recording above). Put exactly what he said in "heard" (in his language and script), then answer it.' }] });
  else contents.push({ role: 'user', parts: [{ text: question }] });
  var body = { systemInstruction: { parts: [{ text: sys }] }, contents: contents,
    generationConfig: { temperature: 0.2, maxOutputTokens: 2048, responseMimeType: 'application/json',
      responseSchema: { type: 'OBJECT', properties: { heard: { type: 'STRING' }, say: { type: 'STRING' }, show: { type: 'ARRAY', items: { type: 'STRING' } } }, required: ['say', 'show'] } } };
  var err = '', tried = 0, list = models.text.slice();
  for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
    var bi = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(body)), tc = aiThink_(list[i]);
    if (tc) bi.generationConfig.thinkingConfig = tc;
    var r = aiFetch_('models/' + list[i] + ':generateContent', bi); tried++;
    if (tc && r.code === 400 && /think/i.test(JSON.stringify(r.j))) { r = aiFetch_('models/' + list[i] + ':generateContent', body); }
    if (r.code === 503 || r.code === 500) { Utilities.sleep(1500); r = aiFetch_('models/' + list[i] + ':generateContent', bi); }
    if (r.code === 200) {
      var parts = (((r.j.candidates || [])[0] || {}).content || {}).parts || [], txt = parts.filter(function (p) { return !p.thought && p.text; }).map(function (p) { return p.text; }).join('');
      var o = null; try { o = JSON.parse(txt); } catch (e) { o = { say: txt.replace(/[{}"]/g, ' ').trim(), show: [] }; }
      if (!o || !o.say) { err = 'The AI gave no answer. Please ask again.'; continue; }
      return { ok: true, say: String(o.say), show: (o.show || []).slice(0, 12).map(String), heard: au ? String(o.heard || '') : question, model: list[i] };
    }
    err = aiErr_(r);
    if (r.code === 404 && i === list.length - 1 && tried < 8) { try { var m2 = aiModels_(true).text.filter(function (x) { return list.indexOf(x) < 0; }); list = list.concat(m2); } catch (e) {} }
    if (r.code === 400 && /api key/i.test(err) || r.code === 401) break;
  }
  return { ok: false, error: err || 'The AI did not answer. Please try again.' };
}
/** A natural Gemini voice for the answer. Returns base64 audio, or ok:false (the page then uses the phone's own voice). */
function aiVoice(pin, text, voice) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  if (!aiKey_()) return { ok: false };
  text = String(text || '').trim().slice(0, 1500);
  if (!text) return { ok: false };
  voice = AI_VOICES_.indexOf(String(voice)) >= 0 ? String(voice) : AI_VOICES_[0];
  var models; try { models = aiModels_(false); } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: String(e.message || e) }; }
  var err = '';
  for (var i = 0; i < models.tts.length; i++) {
    var r = aiFetch_('models/' + models.tts[i] + ':generateContent', { contents: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ text: 'Read this aloud in a clear, warm, natural voice at a calm pace: ' + text }] }],
      generationConfig: { responseModalities: ['AUDIO'], speechConfig: { voiceConfig: { prebuiltVoiceConfig: { voiceName: voice } } } } });
    if (r.code === 200) {
      var p = ((((r.j.candidates || [])[0] || {}).content || {}).parts || []).filter(function (x) { return x.inlineData && x.inlineData.data; })[0];
      if (p) return { ok: true, mime: p.inlineData.mimeType || 'audio/L16;rate=24000', data: p.inlineData.data };
    }
    err = aiErr_(r);
  }
  return { ok: false, error: err };
}
/** Menu: paste the Gemini key (from aistudio.google.com/apikey) and test it. */
function aiSetupMenu() {
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(), p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(), has = !!aiKey_();
  var r = ui.prompt('🤖 AI voice assistant – Gemini key', (has ? 'A key is already saved. Paste a new key to replace it, or type DELETE to remove it.\n\n' : '') +
    '1. Open aistudio.google.com/apikey (sign in with your Google account)\n2. Tap "Create API key" and copy it\n3. Paste it here and press OK', ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL);
  if (r.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;
  var k = r.getResponseText().trim();
  if (!k) return;
  if (k.toUpperCase() === 'DELETE') { p.deleteProperty(AI_KEY_PROP_); p.deleteProperty(AI_PICK_PROP_); ui.alert('The key was removed. The assistant is off.'); return; }
  p.setProperty(AI_KEY_PROP_, k);
  try {
    var m = aiModels_(true);
    if (!m.text.length) throw new Error('No Gemini text model is available for this key.');
    var good = '', tries = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < m.text.length && !good; i++) {
      for (var a = 0; a < 2 && !good; a++) {   // a busy model gets a second try after 2 seconds
        var t = aiFetch_('models/' + m.text[i] + ':generateContent', { contents: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ text: 'Reply with the single word OK.' }] }] });
        if (t.code === 200) good = m.text[i];
        else { tries.push(m.text[i] + ' → ' + aiErr_(t)); if (t.code >= 500 || t.code === 429) Utilities.sleep(2000); else break; }
      }
    }
    if (!good) throw new Error(tries.join('\n'));
    if (good !== m.text[0]) { m.text = [good].concat(m.text.filter(function (x) { return x !== good; })); p.setProperty(AI_PICK_PROP_, JSON.stringify(m)); }
    ui.alert('✅ The AI voice assistant is ready.\n\nAnswers: ' + good + '\nVoice: ' + (m.tts[0] || 'phone voice (no Gemini voice for this key)') + '\n\nOpen the page as ALL SITES and tap the 🎙 button.');
  } catch (e) { ui.alert('The key was saved, but the test failed:\n\n' + (e.message || e)); }
}

/* ---------- 🎙 WTC Voice app page (outside Google's frame, so the microphone works) ----------
 * The page posts {fn, args} as text. Only the assistant functions can be called, only with the office PIN,
 * and 5 wrong PINs lock the voice app for 15 minutes.
 */

function aiPing(pin) {
  var ss = ss_();
  if (!okOffice_(ss, pin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Wrong office PIN' };
  return { ok: true, ready: !!aiKey_(), version: VERSION_ };
}
function doPost(e) {
  var out = function (o) { return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(o)).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); };
  try {
    var q = JSON.parse((e && e.postData && e.postData.contents) || '{}'), fn = String(q.fn || ''), args = Array.isArray(q.args) ? q.args : [];
    var API = { aiAsk: aiAsk, aiVoice: aiVoice, aiWarm: aiWarm, aiPing: aiPing };
    if (!API.hasOwnProperty(fn)) return out({ ok: false, error: 'Not allowed' });
    var c = CacheService.getScriptCache(), bad = Number(c.get('aiApiBad') || 0);
    if (bad >= 5) return out({ ok: false, error: 'Too many wrong PINs. The voice app is locked for 15 minutes.' });
    var r = API[fn].apply(null, args) || {};
    if (r.error === 'Wrong office PIN') c.put('aiApiBad', String(bad + 1), 900);
    return out(r);
  } catch (err) { return out({ ok: false, error: 'Server error: ' + (err.message || err) }); }
}
